FILE: README.md
====================================================================================================
# LIMS

Laboratory Information Management System

## Architecture

- Modular Monolith
- Clean Architecture
- Domain-Driven Design
- CQRS-oriented Application layer
- API-first; no standalone UI
- Shared database/schema
- Laboratory is a domain Aggregate Root, not a Tenant

## Projects

- LIMS.Api
- LIMS.Application
- LIMS.Domain
- LIMS.Infrastructure
- LIMS.Contracts

## Initial domain modules

Requests, Samples, Experiments, Protocols, Instruments, Results,
Workflows, Approvals, Files, Laboratories.

## Development status

Initial solution scaffolding.
Persistence provider, authentication provider and external storage
will be selected and configured in subsequent architecture decisions.

FILE: src\LIMS.Api\Areas\Experiments\Controllers\ExperimentsController.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Experiments.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Experiments.DTOs;
using LIMS.Application.Experiments.Queries;
using LIMS.Contracts.Experiments.Requests;
using LIMS.Contracts.Experiments.Responses;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace LIMS.Api.Areas.Experiments.Controllers;

[Area("Experiments")]
[ApiController]
[Route("api/v1/experiments")]
[Produces("application/json")]
public sealed class ExperimentsController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly IDispatcher _dispatcher;

    public ExperimentsController(IDispatcher dispatcher)
    {
        _dispatcher = dispatcher;
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(Guid), StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ValidationProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create(
        [FromBody] CreateExperimentRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var id = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new CreateExperimentCommand(
                request.LaboratoryId,
                request.SampleId,
                request.ExperimentCode,
                request.Title,
                request.Description,
                request.CreatedByUserId),
            cancellationToken);

        return CreatedAtAction(
            nameof(GetById),
            new { experimentId = id },
            id);
    }

    [HttpGet("{experimentId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ExperimentDetailsResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetById(
        Guid experimentId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync<
            GetExperimentByIdQuery,
            ExperimentDetailsDto?>(
            new GetExperimentByIdQuery(experimentId),
            cancellationToken);

        if (result is null)
            return NotFound();

        return Ok(ToDetailsResponse(result));
    }

    [HttpGet("by-code/{laboratoryId:guid}/{experimentCode}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ExperimentResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByCode(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string experimentCode,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync<
            GetExperimentByCodeQuery,
            ExperimentDto?>(
            new GetExperimentByCodeQuery(laboratoryId, experimentCode),
            cancellationToken);

        if (result is null)
            return NotFound();

        return Ok(ToResponse(result));
    }

    [HttpGet("by-laboratory/{laboratoryId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(IReadOnlyCollection<ExperimentResponse>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByLaboratory(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync<
            GetExperimentsByLaboratoryIdQuery,
            IReadOnlyCollection<ExperimentDto>>(
            new GetExperimentsByLaboratoryIdQuery(laboratoryId),
            cancellationToken);

        return Ok(result.Select(ToResponse).ToArray());
    }

    [HttpGet("by-sample/{sampleId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(IReadOnlyCollection<ExperimentResponse>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetBySample(
        Guid sampleId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync<
            GetExperimentsBySampleIdQuery,
            IReadOnlyCollection<ExperimentDto>>(
            new GetExperimentsBySampleIdQuery(sampleId),
            cancellationToken);

        return Ok(result.Select(ToResponse).ToArray());
    }

    [HttpGet("by-assigned-user/{userId}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(IReadOnlyCollection<ExperimentResponse>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByAssignedUser(
        string userId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync<
            GetExperimentsByAssignedUserIdQuery,
            IReadOnlyCollection<ExperimentDto>>(
            new GetExperimentsByAssignedUserIdQuery(userId),
            cancellationToken);

        return Ok(result.Select(ToResponse).ToArray());
    }

    [HttpPut("{experimentId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ValidationProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Update(
        Guid experimentId,
        [FromBody] UpdateExperimentRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new UpdateExperimentInformationCommand(
                experimentId,
                request.Title,
                request.Description),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{experimentId:guid}/assign")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ValidationProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Assign(
        Guid experimentId,
        [FromBody] AssignExperimentRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new AssignExperimentCommand(experimentId, request.UserId),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpDelete("{experimentId:guid}/assignment")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Unassign(
        Guid experimentId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new UnassignExperimentCommand(experimentId),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{experimentId:guid}/start")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Start(
        Guid experimentId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new StartExperimentCommand(experimentId),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{experimentId:guid}/complete")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Complete(
        Guid experimentId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new CompleteExperimentCommand(experimentId),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{experimentId:guid}/cancel")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ValidationProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Cancel(
        Guid experimentId,
        [FromBody] CancelExperimentRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new CancelExperimentCommand(experimentId, request.Reason),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{experimentId:guid}/archive")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Archive(
        Guid experimentId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new ArchiveExperimentCommand(experimentId),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    private static ExperimentResponse ToResponse(ExperimentDto dto)
    {
        return new ExperimentResponse(
            dto.Id,
            dto.LaboratoryId,
            dto.SampleId,
            dto.ExperimentCode,
            dto.Title,
            dto.Description,
            dto.Status.ToString(),
            dto.CreatedByUserId,
            dto.AssignedToUserId,
            dto.CreatedAt,
            dto.UpdatedAt,
            dto.StartedAt,
            dto.CompletedAt,
            dto.ArchivedAt);
    }

    private static ExperimentDetailsResponse ToDetailsResponse(
        ExperimentDetailsDto dto)
    {
        return new ExperimentDetailsResponse(
            dto.Id,
            dto.LaboratoryId,
            dto.SampleId,
            dto.ExperimentCode,
            dto.Title,
            dto.Description,
            dto.Status.ToString(),
            dto.CreatedByUserId,
            dto.AssignedToUserId,
            dto.CreatedAt,
            dto.UpdatedAt,
            dto.StartedAt,
            dto.CompletedAt,
            dto.ArchivedAt);
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Api\Areas\Health\Controllers\HealthController.cs
====================================================================================================
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace LIMS.Api.Areas.Health.Controllers;

[Area("Health")]
[ApiController]
[Route("api/v1/health")]
public sealed class HealthController : ControllerBase
{
    [HttpGet]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public IActionResult Get()
    {
        return Ok(new
        {
            Status = "Healthy",
            Service = "LIMS",
            Version = "1.0",
            Timestamp = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,
            TraceId = HttpContext.TraceIdentifier
        });
    }

    [HttpGet("live")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public IActionResult Live()
    {
        return Ok(new
        {
            Status = "Alive",
            Timestamp = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow
        });
    }

    [HttpGet("ready")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status503ServiceUnavailable)]
    public IActionResult Ready()
    {
        return Ok(new
        {
            Status = "Ready",
            Timestamp = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow
        });
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Api\Areas\Instruments\Controllers\InstrumentsController.cs

using LIMS.Application.Common.Dispatching;
using LIMS.Application.Instruments.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Instruments.Queries;
using LIMS.Contracts.Instruments;
using LIMS.Contracts.Instruments.Mappings;
using LIMS.Contracts.Instruments.Requests;
using LIMS.Contracts.Instruments.Responses;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace LIMS.Api.Areas.Instruments.Controllers;

[ApiController]
[Area("Instruments")]
[Route("api/v1/instruments")]
[Produces("application/json")]
public sealed class InstrumentsController(IDispatcher dispatcher) : ControllerBase
{
    [HttpPost]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(Guid), StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ValidationProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Register(
        [FromBody] RegisterInstrumentRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var id = await dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new RegisterInstrumentCommand(
                request.LaboratoryId,
                request.InstrumentCode,
                request.Name,
                request.Type.ToDomain(),
                request.RegisteredByUserId,
                request.Manufacturer,
                request.Model,
                request.SerialNumber,
                request.Location,
                request.Description),
            cancellationToken);

        return CreatedAtAction(nameof(GetById), new { instrumentId = id }, new { id });
    }

    [HttpGet("{instrumentId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(InstrumentDetailsResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetById(
        Guid instrumentId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await dispatcher.QueryAsync(
            new GetInstrumentByIdQuery(instrumentId),
            cancellationToken);

        if (result is null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return Ok(ToDetailsResponse(result));
    }

    [HttpGet("by-code/{laboratoryId:guid}/{instrumentCode}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(InstrumentResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByCode(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string instrumentCode,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await dispatcher.QueryAsync(
            new GetInstrumentByCodeQuery(laboratoryId, instrumentCode),
            cancellationToken);

        return result is null ? NotFound() : Ok(ToResponse(result));
    }

    [HttpGet("by-laboratory/{laboratoryId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(IReadOnlyCollection<InstrumentResponse>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByLaboratory(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var results = await dispatcher.QueryAsync(
            new GetInstrumentsByLaboratoryIdQuery(laboratoryId),
            cancellationToken);

        return Ok(results.Select(ToResponse).ToArray());
    }

    [HttpGet("by-status/{laboratoryId:guid}/{status}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(IReadOnlyCollection<InstrumentResponse>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByStatus(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        InstrumentStatusContract status,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(status))
        {
            return BadRequest();
        }

        var results = await dispatcher.QueryAsync(
            new GetInstrumentsByStatusQuery(laboratoryId, status.ToDomain()),
            cancellationToken);

        return Ok(results.Select(ToResponse).ToArray());
    }

    [HttpPut("{instrumentId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ValidationProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Update(
        Guid instrumentId,
        [FromBody] UpdateInstrumentRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new UpdateInstrumentInformationCommand(
                instrumentId,
                request.Name,
                request.Type.ToDomain(),
                request.Manufacturer,
                request.Model,
                request.SerialNumber,
                request.Location,
                request.Description),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{instrumentId:guid}/use")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> MarkInUse(
        Guid instrumentId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new MarkInstrumentInUseCommand(instrumentId),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{instrumentId:guid}/available")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> MarkAvailable(
        Guid instrumentId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new MarkInstrumentAvailableCommand(instrumentId),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{instrumentId:guid}/maintenance")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(Guid), StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ValidationProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> ScheduleMaintenance(
        Guid instrumentId,
        [FromBody] ScheduleInstrumentMaintenanceRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var maintenanceId = await dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new ScheduleInstrumentMaintenanceCommand(
                instrumentId,
                request.Type.ToDomain(),
                request.Description,
                request.ScheduledAt,
                request.ScheduledByUserId),
            cancellationToken);

        return CreatedAtAction(
            nameof(GetById),
            new { instrumentId },
            new { maintenanceId });
    }

    [HttpPost("{instrumentId:guid}/maintenance/{maintenanceId:guid}/complete")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ValidationProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> CompleteMaintenance(
        Guid instrumentId,
        Guid maintenanceId,
        [FromBody] CompleteInstrumentMaintenanceRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new CompleteInstrumentMaintenanceCommand(
                instrumentId,
                maintenanceId,
                request.CompletedByUserId,
                request.CompletionNotes),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{instrumentId:guid}/out-of-service")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ValidationProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> MarkOutOfService(
        Guid instrumentId,
        [FromBody] MarkInstrumentOutOfServiceRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new MarkInstrumentOutOfServiceCommand(instrumentId, request.Reason),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{instrumentId:guid}/restore-service")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> RestoreService(
        Guid instrumentId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new RestoreInstrumentServiceCommand(instrumentId),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{instrumentId:guid}/retire")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Retire(
        Guid instrumentId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new RetireInstrumentCommand(instrumentId),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    private static InstrumentResponse ToResponse(
        LIMS.Application.Instruments.DTOs.InstrumentDto dto)
    {
        return new InstrumentResponse(
            dto.Id,
            dto.LaboratoryId,
            dto.InstrumentCode,
            dto.Name,
            dto.Manufacturer,
            dto.Model,
            dto.SerialNumber,
            dto.Type.ToContract(),
            dto.Status.ToContract(),
            dto.Location,
            dto.Description,
            dto.RegisteredAt,
            dto.UpdatedAt,
            dto.RetiredAt);
    }

    private static InstrumentDetailsResponse ToDetailsResponse(
        LIMS.Application.Instruments.DTOs.InstrumentDetailsDto dto)
    {
        return new InstrumentDetailsResponse(
            dto.Id,
            dto.LaboratoryId,
            dto.InstrumentCode,
            dto.Name,
            dto.Manufacturer,
            dto.Model,
            dto.SerialNumber,
            dto.Type.ToContract(),
            dto.Status.ToContract(),
            dto.Location,
            dto.Description,
            dto.RegisteredAt,
            dto.UpdatedAt,
            dto.RetiredAt,
            dto.MaintenanceRecords
                .Select(x => new InstrumentMaintenanceResponse(
                    x.Id,
                    x.InstrumentId,
                    x.Type.ToContract(),
                    x.Description,
                    x.ScheduledAt,
                    x.ScheduledByUserId,
                    x.CompletedAt,
                    x.CompletedByUserId,
                    x.CompletionNotes))
                .ToArray());
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Api\Areas\Inventory\Controllers\InventoryController.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.CQRS;
using LIMS.Application.Inventory.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Inventory.Queries;
using LIMS.Contracts.Inventory.Mappings;
using LIMS.Contracts.Inventory.Requests;
using LIMS.Contracts.Inventory.Responses;
using LIMS.Domain.Inventory.Enums;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace LIMS.Api.Areas.Inventory.Controllers;

[Area("Inventory")]
[ApiController]
[Route("api/v1/inventory")]
[Produces("application/json")]
public sealed class InventoryController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly IDispatcher _dispatcher;

    public InventoryController(IDispatcher dispatcher)
    {
        _dispatcher = dispatcher;
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(object), StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create(
        [FromBody] CreateInventoryItemRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(request.Type))
            return BadRequest("Inventory item type is invalid.");

        var id = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new CreateInventoryItemCommand(
                request.LaboratoryId,
                request.ItemCode,
                request.Name,
                request.Description,
                request.Type.ToDomain(),
                request.CasNumber,
                request.ChemicalFormula,
                request.MolecularWeight,
                request.HazardClassification,
                request.SafetyDataSheetReference,
                request.DefaultUnit,
                request.MinimumStockLevel),
            cancellationToken);

        return CreatedAtAction(
            nameof(GetById),
            new { inventoryItemId = id },
            new { id });
    }

    [HttpGet("{inventoryItemId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(InventoryItemDetailsResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetById(
        Guid inventoryItemId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var item = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync(
            new GetInventoryItemByIdQuery(inventoryItemId),
            cancellationToken);

        return item is null
            ? NotFound()
            : Ok(item.ToResponse());
    }

    [HttpGet("by-code/{laboratoryId:guid}/{itemCode}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(InventoryItemResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByCode(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string itemCode,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var item = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync(
            new GetInventoryItemByCodeQuery(laboratoryId, itemCode),
            cancellationToken);

        return item is null
            ? NotFound()
            : Ok(item.ToResponse());
    }

    [HttpGet("by-laboratory/{laboratoryId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(IReadOnlyCollection<InventoryItemResponse>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByLaboratory(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        [FromQuery] InventoryItemStatus? status,
        [FromQuery] InventoryItemType? type,
        [FromQuery] int pageNumber = 1,
        [FromQuery] int pageSize = 50,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var items = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync(
            new GetInventoryItemsByLaboratoryQuery(
                laboratoryId,
                status,
                type,
                pageNumber,
                pageSize),
            cancellationToken);

        return Ok(items.Select(x => x.ToResponse()).ToArray());
    }

    [HttpPut("{inventoryItemId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Update(
        Guid inventoryItemId,
        [FromBody] UpdateInventoryItemRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(request.Type))
            return BadRequest("Inventory item type is invalid.");

        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new UpdateInventoryItemCommand(
                inventoryItemId,
                request.Name,
                request.Description,
                request.Type.ToDomain(),
                request.CasNumber,
                request.ChemicalFormula,
                request.MolecularWeight,
                request.HazardClassification,
                request.SafetyDataSheetReference,
                request.DefaultUnit,
                request.MinimumStockLevel),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{inventoryItemId:guid}/lots")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(object), StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> ReceiveStock(
        Guid inventoryItemId,
        [FromBody] ReceiveInventoryStockRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var userId = GetCurrentUserId();

        var lotId = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new ReceiveInventoryStockCommand(
                inventoryItemId,
                request.LotNumber,
                request.Supplier,
                request.Manufacturer,
                request.Quantity,
                request.Unit,
                request.MinimumStockLevel,
                request.ManufactureDate,
                request.ExpiryDate,
                request.StorageLocation,
                request.StorageConditions,
                request.CertificateOfAnalysisNumber,
                userId,
                request.ReferenceNumber,
                request.Notes),
            cancellationToken);

        return Created(
            $"/api/v1/inventory/{inventoryItemId}/lots/{lotId}",
            new { lotId });
    }

    [HttpPost("{inventoryItemId:guid}/lots/consume")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> ConsumeStock(
        Guid inventoryItemId,
        [FromBody] ConsumeInventoryStockRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new ConsumeInventoryStockCommand(
                inventoryItemId,
                request.LotId,
                request.Quantity,
                GetCurrentUserId(),
                request.ExperimentId,
                request.Notes),
            cancellationToken);

        return result ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{inventoryItemId:guid}/lots/adjust")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> AdjustStock(
        Guid inventoryItemId,
        [FromBody] AdjustInventoryStockRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new AdjustInventoryStockCommand(
                inventoryItemId,
                request.LotId,
                request.Quantity,
                request.Increase,
                GetCurrentUserId(),
                request.Reason),
            cancellationToken);

        return result ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{inventoryItemId:guid}/lots/quarantine")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> QuarantineLot(
        Guid inventoryItemId,
        [FromQuery] Guid lotId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new QuarantineInventoryLotCommand(inventoryItemId, lotId),
            cancellationToken);

        return result ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{inventoryItemId:guid}/lots/release")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> ReleaseLot(
        Guid inventoryItemId,
        [FromQuery] Guid lotId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new ReleaseInventoryLotCommand(inventoryItemId, lotId),
            cancellationToken);

        return result ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{inventoryItemId:guid}/lots/dispose")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> DisposeLot(
        Guid inventoryItemId,
        [FromBody] DisposeInventoryLotRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new DisposeInventoryLotCommand(
                inventoryItemId,
                request.LotId,
                GetCurrentUserId(),
                request.Reason),
            cancellationToken);

        return result ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{inventoryItemId:guid}/deactivate")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Deactivate(
        Guid inventoryItemId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new DeactivateInventoryItemCommand(inventoryItemId),
            cancellationToken);

        return result ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{inventoryItemId:guid}/reactivate")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Reactivate(
        Guid inventoryItemId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new ReactivateInventoryItemCommand(inventoryItemId),
            cancellationToken);

        return result ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{inventoryItemId:guid}/discontinue")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Discontinue(
        Guid inventoryItemId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new DiscontinueInventoryItemCommand(inventoryItemId),
            cancellationToken);

        return result ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    private string GetCurrentUserId()
    {
        return User.FindFirst(
                   System.Security.Claims.ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier)?.Value
               ?? throw new UnauthorizedAccessException(
                   "Authenticated user ID is unavailable.");
    }
}

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.DTOs;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Queries;
using LIMS.Contracts.Common;
using LIMS.Contracts.Laboratories;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace LIMS.Api.Areas.Laboratories.Controllers;

[Area("Laboratories")]
[ApiController]
[Route("api/v1/laboratories")]
[Produces("application/json")]
public sealed class LaboratoriesController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly IDispatcher _dispatcher;

    public LaboratoriesController(IDispatcher dispatcher)
    {
        _dispatcher = dispatcher;
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(object), StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create(
        [FromBody] CreateLaboratoryRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var id = await _dispatcher.SendAsync<CreateLaboratoryCommand, Guid>(
            new CreateLaboratoryCommand(
                request.Name,
                request.Code,
                request.Description),
            cancellationToken);

        return CreatedAtAction(nameof(GetById), new { laboratoryId = id }, new { id });
    }

    [HttpGet("{laboratoryId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(LaboratoryDetailsDto), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetById(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync<
            GetLaboratoryByIdQuery,
            LaboratoryDetailsDto?>(
            new GetLaboratoryByIdQuery(laboratoryId),
            cancellationToken);

        return result is null ? NotFound() : Ok(result);
    }

    [HttpGet("by-code/{code}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(LaboratoryDto), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByCode(
        string code,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync<
            GetLaboratoryByCodeQuery,
            LaboratoryDto?>(
            new GetLaboratoryByCodeQuery(code),
            cancellationToken);

        return result is null ? NotFound() : Ok(result);
    }

    [HttpGet("by-user/{userId}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(
        typeof(IReadOnlyCollection<LaboratoryDto>),
        StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByUser(
        string userId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync<
            GetLaboratoriesByUserIdQuery,
            IReadOnlyCollection<LaboratoryDto>>(
            new GetLaboratoriesByUserIdQuery(userId),
            cancellationToken);

        return Ok(result);
    }

    [HttpPut("{laboratoryId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Update(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        [FromBody] UpdateLaboratoryRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        await _dispatcher.SendAsync<UpdateLaboratoryInformationCommand, bool>(
            new UpdateLaboratoryInformationCommand(
                laboratoryId,
                request.Name,
                request.Code,
                request.Description),
            cancellationToken);

        return NoContent();
    }

    [HttpPost("{laboratoryId:guid}/members")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> AddMember(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        [FromBody] AddLaboratoryMemberRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        await _dispatcher.SendAsync<AddLaboratoryMemberCommand, bool>(
            new AddLaboratoryMemberCommand(
                laboratoryId,
                request.UserId,
                request.Role),
            cancellationToken);

        return NoContent();
    }

    [HttpDelete("{laboratoryId:guid}/members/{userId}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> RemoveMember(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string userId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        await _dispatcher.SendAsync<RemoveLaboratoryMemberCommand, bool>(
            new RemoveLaboratoryMemberCommand(laboratoryId, userId),
            cancellationToken);

        return NoContent();
    }

    [HttpPatch("{laboratoryId:guid}/status")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> ChangeStatus(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        [FromBody] ChangeLaboratoryStatusRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        await _dispatcher.SendAsync<ChangeLaboratoryStatusCommand, bool>(
            new ChangeLaboratoryStatusCommand(
                laboratoryId,
                request.Status),
            cancellationToken);

        return NoContent();
    }
}

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Protocols.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Protocols.DTOs;
using LIMS.Application.Protocols.Queries;
using LIMS.Contracts.Common;
using LIMS.Contracts.Protocols;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace LIMS.Api.Areas.Protocols.Controllers;

[ApiController]
[Area("Protocols")]
[Route("api/v1/protocols")]
[Produces("application/json")]
public sealed class ProtocolsController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly IDispatcher _dispatcher;

    public ProtocolsController(IDispatcher dispatcher)
    {
        _dispatcher = dispatcher;
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(Guid), StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ValidationErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create(
        [FromBody] CreateProtocolRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var id = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new CreateProtocolCommand(
                request.LaboratoryId,
                request.ProtocolCode,
                request.Name,
                request.Description,
                request.Version,
                request.CreatedByUserId),
            cancellationToken);

        return CreatedAtAction(
            nameof(GetById),
            new { protocolId = id },
            id);
    }

    [HttpGet("{protocolId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProtocolDetailsResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetById(
        Guid protocolId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync(
            new GetProtocolByIdQuery(protocolId),
            cancellationToken);

        return result is null
            ? NotFound()
            : Ok(Map(result));
    }

    [HttpGet("by-code/{laboratoryId:guid}/{protocolCode}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProtocolResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByCode(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string protocolCode,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync(
            new GetProtocolByCodeQuery(laboratoryId, protocolCode),
            cancellationToken);

        return result is null
            ? NotFound()
            : Ok(Map(result));
    }

    [HttpGet("by-laboratory/{laboratoryId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(IReadOnlyCollection<ProtocolResponse>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByLaboratory(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var results = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync(
            new GetProtocolsByLaboratoryIdQuery(laboratoryId),
            cancellationToken);

        return Ok(results.Select(Map).ToArray());
    }

    [HttpPut("{protocolId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ValidationErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Update(
        Guid protocolId,
        [FromBody] UpdateProtocolRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new UpdateProtocolInformationCommand(
                protocolId,
                request.Name,
                request.Description,
                request.Version),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{protocolId:guid}/steps")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(Guid), StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ValidationErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> AddStep(
        Guid protocolId,
        [FromBody] AddProtocolStepRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var stepId = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new AddProtocolStepCommand(
                protocolId,
                request.Sequence,
                request.Title,
                request.Instruction,
                request.Type.ToDomain(),
                request.IsRequired,
                request.TargetValue,
                request.TargetUnit,
                request.MinimumValue,
                request.MaximumValue,
                request.Notes),
            cancellationToken);

        return CreatedAtAction(
            nameof(GetById),
            new { protocolId },
            stepId);
    }

    [HttpPut("{protocolId:guid}/steps/{stepId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ValidationErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> UpdateStep(
        Guid protocolId,
        Guid stepId,
        [FromBody] UpdateProtocolStepRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new UpdateProtocolStepCommand(
                protocolId,
                stepId,
                request.Sequence,
                request.Title,
                request.Instruction,
                request.Type.ToDomain(),
                request.IsRequired,
                request.TargetValue,
                request.TargetUnit,
                request.MinimumValue,
                request.MaximumValue,
                request.Notes),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpDelete("{protocolId:guid}/steps/{stepId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> RemoveStep(
        Guid protocolId,
        Guid stepId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var removed = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new RemoveProtocolStepCommand(protocolId, stepId),
            cancellationToken);

        return removed ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{protocolId:guid}/activate")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Activate(
        Guid protocolId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var activated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new ActivateProtocolCommand(protocolId),
            cancellationToken);

        return activated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{protocolId:guid}/deprecate")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Deprecate(
        Guid protocolId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var deprecated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new DeprecateProtocolCommand(protocolId),
            cancellationToken);

        return deprecated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{protocolId:guid}/archive")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Archive(
        Guid protocolId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var archived = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new ArchiveProtocolCommand(protocolId),
            cancellationToken);

        return archived ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    private static ProtocolResponse Map(ProtocolDto dto)
    {
        return new ProtocolResponse(
            dto.Id,
            dto.LaboratoryId,
            dto.ProtocolCode,
            dto.Name,
            dto.Description,
            dto.Version,
            (ProtocolStatusContract)(int)dto.Status,
            dto.CreatedByUserId,
            dto.CreatedAt,
            dto.UpdatedAt,
            dto.ActivatedAt,
            dto.DeprecatedAt,
            dto.ArchivedAt);
    }

    private static ProtocolDetailsResponse Map(ProtocolDetailsDto dto)
    {
        return new ProtocolDetailsResponse(
            dto.Id,
            dto.LaboratoryId,
            dto.ProtocolCode,
            dto.Name,
            dto.Description,
            dto.Version,
            (ProtocolStatusContract)(int)dto.Status,
            dto.CreatedByUserId,
            dto.CreatedAt,
            dto.UpdatedAt,
            dto.ActivatedAt,
            dto.DeprecatedAt,
            dto.ArchivedAt,
            dto.Steps.Select(step => new ProtocolStepResponse(
                step.Id,
                step.ProtocolId,
                step.Sequence,
                step.Title,
                step.Instruction,
                (ProtocolStepTypeContract)(int)step.Type,
                step.IsRequired,
                step.TargetValue,
                step.TargetUnit,
                step.MinimumValue,
                step.MaximumValue,
                step.Notes)).ToArray());
    }
}

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Identity;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.CQRS;
using LIMS.Application.Requests.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Requests.Queries;
using LIMS.Contracts.Common;
using LIMS.Contracts.Requests.Mapping;
using LIMS.Contracts.Requests.Requests;
using LIMS.Contracts.Requests.Responses;
using LIMS.Domain.Requests.Enums;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace LIMS.Api.Areas.Requests.Controllers;

[Area("Requests")]
[ApiController]
[Route("api/v1/sample-requests")]
[Produces("application/json")]
public sealed class SampleRequestsController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly IDispatcher _dispatcher;
    private readonly ICurrentUser _currentUser;

    public SampleRequestsController(
        IDispatcher dispatcher,
        ICurrentUser currentUser)
    {
        _dispatcher = dispatcher;
        _currentUser = currentUser;
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(object), StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ValidationErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create(
        [FromBody] CreateSampleRequestRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var command = new CreateSampleRequestCommand(
            request.RequestCode,
            request.Title,
            request.Description,
            request.SampleName,
            request.SampleDescription,
            request.RequestedSampleType.ToDomain(),
            request.SampleSource,
            RequireCurrentUserId(),
            request.Priority.ToDomain());

        var requestId = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            command,
            cancellationToken);

        return CreatedAtAction(
            nameof(GetById),
            new { requestId },
            new { id = requestId });
    }

    [HttpGet("{requestId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(SampleRequestResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetById(
        Guid requestId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync(
            new GetSampleRequestByIdQuery(requestId),
            cancellationToken);

        return result is null ? NotFound() : Ok(result.ToResponse());
    }

    [HttpGet("by-code/{requestCode}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(SampleRequestResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByCode(
        string requestCode,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync(
            new GetSampleRequestByCodeQuery(requestCode),
            cancellationToken);

        return result is null ? NotFound() : Ok(result.ToResponse());
    }

    [HttpGet("by-requester/{userId}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(IReadOnlyCollection<SampleRequestResponse>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByRequester(
        string userId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var results = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync(
            new GetSampleRequestsByRequesterQuery(userId),
            cancellationToken);

        return Ok(results.Select(x => x.ToResponse()).ToArray());
    }

    [HttpGet("by-laboratory/{laboratoryId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(IReadOnlyCollection<SampleRequestResponse>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByLaboratory(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var results = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync(
            new GetSampleRequestsByLaboratoryQuery(laboratoryId),
            cancellationToken);

        return Ok(results.Select(x => x.ToResponse()).ToArray());
    }

    [HttpGet("by-status/{status}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(IReadOnlyCollection<SampleRequestResponse>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByStatus(
        string status,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!Enum.TryParse<SampleRequestStatus>(
                status,
                true,
                out var parsedStatus)
            || !Enum.IsDefined(parsedStatus))
        {
            return BadRequest(new ErrorResponse(
                "invalid_request_status",
                "The supplied request status is invalid.",
                HttpContext.TraceIdentifier));
        }

        var results = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync(
            new GetSampleRequestsByStatusQuery(parsedStatus),
            cancellationToken);

        return Ok(results.Select(x => x.ToResponse()).ToArray());
    }

    [HttpGet("pending-review")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(IReadOnlyCollection<SampleRequestResponse>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetPendingReview(
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var results = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync(
            new GetPendingSampleRequestsQuery(),
            cancellationToken);

        return Ok(results.Select(x => x.ToResponse()).ToArray());
    }

    [HttpPut("{requestId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ValidationErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Update(
        Guid requestId,
        [FromBody] UpdateSampleRequestRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var command = new UpdateSampleRequestCommand(
            requestId,
            request.Title,
            request.Description,
            request.SampleName,
            request.SampleDescription,
            request.RequestedSampleType.ToDomain(),
            request.SampleSource,
            request.Priority.ToDomain());

        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            command,
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{requestId:guid}/submit")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Submit(
        Guid requestId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new SubmitSampleRequestCommand(
                requestId,
                RequireCurrentUserId()),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{requestId:guid}/start-review")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> StartReview(
        Guid requestId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new StartSampleRequestReviewCommand(
                requestId,
                RequireCurrentUserId()),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{requestId:guid}/approve")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Approve(
        Guid requestId,
        [FromBody] ApproveSampleRequestRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new ApproveSampleRequestCommand(
                requestId,
                RequireCurrentUserId(),
                request.LaboratoryId,
                request.Comments),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{requestId:guid}/reject")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Reject(
        Guid requestId,
        [FromBody] RejectSampleRequestRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new RejectSampleRequestCommand(
                requestId,
                RequireCurrentUserId(),
                request.Reason),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{requestId:guid}/cancel")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Cancel(
        Guid requestId,
        [FromBody] CancelSampleRequestRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new CancelSampleRequestCommand(
                requestId,
                RequireCurrentUserId(),
                request.Reason),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{requestId:guid}/fulfill")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Fulfill(
        Guid requestId,
        [FromQuery] Guid sampleId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new FulfillSampleRequestCommand(requestId, sampleId),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    private string RequireCurrentUserId()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_currentUser.UserId))
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "An authenticated user is required for this operation.");

        return _currentUser.UserId;
    }
}

using LIMS.Application;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Queries;
using LIMS.Contracts.Results.Mappings;
using LIMS.Contracts.Results.Requests;
using LIMS.Contracts.Results.Responses;
using LIMS.Domain.Results.Enums;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace LIMS.Api.Areas.Results.Controllers;

[ApiController]
[Area("Results")]
[Route("api/v1/results")]
[Produces("application/json")]
public sealed class ResultsController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly IDispatcher _dispatcher;

    public ResultsController(IDispatcher dispatcher)
    {
        _dispatcher = dispatcher;
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(Guid), StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create(
        [FromBody] CreateAnalysisResultRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var resultId = await _dispatcher.SendAsync(
            new CreateAnalysisResultCommand(
                request.LaboratoryId,
                request.ExperimentId,
                request.SampleId,
                request.InstrumentId,
                request.ResultCode,
                request.Title,
                request.Description,
                request.CreatedByUserId),
            cancellationToken);

        return CreatedAtAction(
            nameof(GetById),
            new { resultId },
            new { id = resultId });
    }

    [HttpGet("{resultId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(AnalysisResultDetailsResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetById(
        Guid resultId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync(
            new GetAnalysisResultByIdQuery(resultId),
            cancellationToken);

        return result is null
            ? NotFound()
            : Ok(result.ToDetailsResponse());
    }

    [HttpGet("by-code/{laboratoryId:guid}/{resultCode}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(AnalysisResultResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByCode(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string resultCode,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync(
            new GetAnalysisResultByCodeQuery(laboratoryId, resultCode),
            cancellationToken);

        return result is null
            ? NotFound()
            : Ok(result.ToResponse());
    }

    [HttpGet("by-laboratory/{laboratoryId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(IReadOnlyCollection<AnalysisResultResponse>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByLaboratory(

        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var results = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync(

    [HttpGet("by-status/{laboratoryId:guid}/{status}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(IReadOnlyCollection<AnalysisResultResponse>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByStatus(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string status,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!Enum.TryParse<ResultStatus>(status, true, out var parsedStatus) ||
            !Enum.IsDefined(parsedStatus))
        {
            return BadRequest(new
            {
                code = "invalid_result_status",
                message = "The specified result status is invalid."

        var results = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync(
            new GetAnalysisResultsByStatusQuery(laboratoryId, parsedStatus),
            cancellationToken);

        return Ok(results.Select(x => x.ToResponse()).ToArray());
    }

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.DTOs;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.Queries;
using LIMS.Contracts.Common;
using LIMS.Contracts.Samples;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace LIMS.Api.Areas.Samples.Controllers;

[Area("Samples")]
[ApiController]
[Route("api/v1/samples")]
[Produces("application/json")]
public sealed class SamplesController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly IDispatcher _dispatcher;

    public SamplesController(IDispatcher dispatcher)
    {
        _dispatcher = dispatcher;
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(Guid), StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ValidationErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Register(
        [FromBody] RegisterSampleRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var command = new RegisterSampleCommand(
            request.LaboratoryId,
            request.SampleCode,
            request.Name,
            request.Description,
            (LIMS.Domain.Samples.Enums.SampleType)request.Type,
            (LIMS.Domain.Samples.Enums.SamplePriority)request.Priority,
            request.Source,
            request.RegisteredByUserId);

        var sampleId = await _dispatcher.SendAsync<RegisterSampleCommand, Guid>(
            command,
            cancellationToken);

        return CreatedAtAction(
            nameof(GetById),
            new { sampleId },
            sampleId);
    }

    [HttpGet("{sampleId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(SampleDetailsDto), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetById(
        Guid sampleId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync<GetSampleByIdQuery, SampleDetailsDto?>(
            new GetSampleByIdQuery(sampleId),
            cancellationToken);

        return result is null ? NotFound() : Ok(result);
    }

    [HttpGet("by-code/{laboratoryId:guid}/{sampleCode}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(SampleDto), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByCode(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string sampleCode,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync<GetSampleByCodeQuery, SampleDto?>(
            new GetSampleByCodeQuery(laboratoryId, sampleCode),
            cancellationToken);

        return result is null ? NotFound() : Ok(result);
    }

    [HttpGet("by-laboratory/{laboratoryId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(IReadOnlyCollection<SampleDto>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByLaboratory(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync<
            GetSamplesByLaboratoryIdQuery,
            IReadOnlyCollection<SampleDto>>(
                new GetSamplesByLaboratoryIdQuery(laboratoryId),
                cancellationToken);

        return Ok(result);
    }

    [HttpGet("by-assigned-user/{userId}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(IReadOnlyCollection<SampleDto>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetByAssignedUser(
        string userId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var result = await _dispatcher.QueryAsync<
            GetSamplesByAssignedUserIdQuery,
            IReadOnlyCollection<SampleDto>>(
                new GetSamplesByAssignedUserIdQuery(userId),
                cancellationToken);

        return Ok(result);
    }

    [HttpPut("{sampleId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ValidationErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Update(
        Guid sampleId,
        [FromBody] UpdateSampleRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync<UpdateSampleInformationCommand, bool>(
            new UpdateSampleInformationCommand(
                sampleId,
                request.Name,
                request.Description,
                request.Source),
            cancellationToken);

    [HttpPost("{sampleId:guid}/receive")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Receive(

        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync<ReceiveSampleCommand, bool>(

    }

    [HttpPost("{sampleId:guid}/preparation")]

    public async Task<IActionResult> MarkReadyForAnalysis(

    {
        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync<MarkSampleReadyForAnalysisCommand, bool>(
            new MarkSampleReadyForAnalysisCommand(sampleId),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{sampleId:guid}/analysis/start")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> StartAnalysis(
        Guid sampleId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)

        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync<StartSampleAnalysisCommand, bool>(
            new StartSampleAnalysisCommand(sampleId),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();

    [HttpPost("{sampleId:guid}/analysis/complete")]

    public async Task<IActionResult> CompleteAnalysis(

            new CompleteSampleAnalysisCommand(sampleId),

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();

    [HttpPost("{sampleId:guid}/reject")]

    public async Task<IActionResult> Reject(
        Guid sampleId,
        [FromBody] RejectSampleRequest request,

    {
        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync<RejectSampleCommand, bool>(

            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpPost("{sampleId:guid}/assign")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ValidationErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Assign(
        Guid sampleId,
        [FromBody] AssignSampleRequest request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync<AssignSampleCommand, bool>(
            new AssignSampleCommand(sampleId, request.UserId),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

    [HttpDelete("{sampleId:guid}/assignment")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Unassign(
        Guid sampleId,

    [HttpPost("{sampleId:guid}/archive")]

    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Archive(
        Guid sampleId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var updated = await _dispatcher.SendAsync<ArchiveSampleCommand, bool>(
            new ArchiveSampleCommand(sampleId),
            cancellationToken);

        return updated ? NoContent() : NotFound();
    }

FILE: src\LIMS.Api\Configuration\ApiBehaviorConfiguration.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Contracts.Common;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace LIMS.Api.Configuration;

public static class ApiBehaviorConfiguration
{
    public static IServiceCollection AddApiBehaviorConfiguration(
        this IServiceCollection services)
    {
        services.Configure<ApiBehaviorOptions>(options =>
        {
            options.InvalidModelStateResponseFactory = context =>
            {
                var errors = context.ModelState
                    .Where(entry => entry.Value?.Errors.Count > 0)
                    .SelectMany(entry =>
                        entry.Value!.Errors.Select(error =>
                            new ValidationError(
                                entry.Key,
                                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(error.ErrorMessage)
                                    ? "The supplied value is invalid."
                                    : error.ErrorMessage)))
                    .ToArray();

                var response = new ValidationErrorResponse(
                    Code: "VALIDATION_ERROR",
                    Message: "One or more validation errors occurred.",
                    TraceId: context.HttpContext.TraceIdentifier,
                    Errors: errors);

                return new BadRequestObjectResult(response);
            };
        });

        return services;
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Api\Configuration\CorsConfiguration.cs
====================================================================================================
namespace LIMS.Api.Configuration;

public static class CorsConfiguration
{
    public const string PolicyName = "LimsCors";

    public static IServiceCollection AddLimsCors(
        this IServiceCollection services,
        IConfiguration configuration)
    {
        var allowedOrigins = configuration
            .GetSection("Cors:AllowedOrigins")
            .Get<string[]>() ?? [];

        services.AddCors(options =>
        {
            options.AddPolicy(PolicyName, policy =>
            {
                if (allowedOrigins.Length == 0)
                    return;

                policy
                    .WithOrigins(allowedOrigins)
                    .AllowAnyHeader()
                    .AllowAnyMethod();
            });
        });

        return services;
    }
}

using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.Json.Serialization;

namespace LIMS.Api.Configuration;

public static class JsonConfiguration
{
    public static IMvcBuilder AddLimsJsonOptions(
        this IMvcBuilder builder)
    {
        builder.AddJsonOptions(options =>
        {
            options.JsonSerializerOptions.PropertyNamingPolicy =
                JsonNamingPolicy.CamelCase;

            options.JsonSerializerOptions.DictionaryKeyPolicy =
                JsonNamingPolicy.CamelCase;

            options.JsonSerializerOptions.DefaultIgnoreCondition =
                JsonIgnoreCondition.WhenWritingNull;

            options.JsonSerializerOptions.Converters.Add(
                new JsonStringEnumConverter(JsonNamingPolicy.CamelCase));

            options.JsonSerializerOptions.NumberHandling =
                JsonNumberHandling.Strict;
        });

        return builder;
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Api\Configuration\LimsProblemDetailsFactory.cs
====================================================================================================
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.Infrastructure;

namespace LIMS.Api.Configuration;

public sealed class LimsProblemDetailsFactory : ProblemDetailsFactory
{
    public override ProblemDetails CreateProblemDetails(
        HttpContext httpContext,
        int? statusCode = null,
        string? title = null,
        string? type = null,
        string? detail = null,
        string? instance = null)
    {
        var problem = new ProblemDetails
        {
            Status = statusCode ?? StatusCodes.Status500InternalServerError,
            Title = title,
            Type = type,
            Detail = detail,
            Instance = instance ?? httpContext.Request.Path
        };

        AddExtensions(problem, httpContext);

        return problem;
    }

    public override ValidationProblemDetails CreateValidationProblemDetails(
        HttpContext httpContext,
        IDictionary<string, string[]> errors,
        int? statusCode = null,
        string? title = null,
        string? type = null,
        string? detail = null,
        string? instance = null)
    {
        var problem = new ValidationProblemDetails(errors)
        {
            Status = statusCode ?? StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest,
            Title = title ?? "One or more validation errors occurred.",
            Type = type,
            Detail = detail,
            Instance = instance ?? httpContext.Request.Path
        };

        AddExtensions(problem, httpContext);

        return problem;
    }

    private static void AddExtensions(
        ProblemDetails problem,
        HttpContext httpContext)
    {
        problem.Extensions["traceId"] = httpContext.TraceIdentifier;

        if (httpContext.Items.TryGetValue(
                Middleware.CorrelationIdMiddleware.ItemKey,
                out var correlationId))
        {
            problem.Extensions["correlationId"] = correlationId;
        }
    }
}

using Microsoft.AspNetCore.RateLimiting;
using System.Threading.RateLimiting;

namespace LIMS.Api.Configuration;

public static class RateLimitingConfiguration
{
    public const string DefaultPolicy = "default";

    public static IServiceCollection AddLimsRateLimiting(
        this IServiceCollection services)
    {
        services.AddRateLimiter(options =>
        {
            options.RejectionStatusCode =
                StatusCodes.Status429TooManyRequests;

            options.AddFixedWindowLimiter(DefaultPolicy, limiter =>
            {
                limiter.PermitLimit = 100;
                limiter.Window = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(1);
                limiter.QueueLimit = 0;
                limiter.AutoReplenishment = true;
            });
        });

        return services;
    }
}

namespace LIMS.Api.Configuration;

public static class RequestSizeConfiguration
{
    public const long DefaultMaximumRequestBodySize = 10 * 1024 * 1024;

    public static IServiceCollection AddLimsRequestSizeLimits(
        this IServiceCollection services)
    {
        services.Configure<Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http.Features.FormOptions>(
            options =>
            {
                options.MultipartBodyLengthLimit =
                    DefaultMaximumRequestBodySize;
            });

        services.Configure<Microsoft.AspNetCore.Server.Kestrel.Core.KestrelServerOptions>(
            options =>
            {
                options.Limits.MaxRequestBodySize =
                    DefaultMaximumRequestBodySize;
            });

        return services;
    }
}
