FILE: src\LIMS.Api\Configuration\SecurityHeadersMiddleware.cs
====================================================================================================
namespace LIMS.Api.Middleware;

public sealed class SecurityHeadersMiddleware
{
    private readonly RequestDelegate _next;

    public SecurityHeadersMiddleware(RequestDelegate next)
    {
        _next = next;
    }

    public async Task InvokeAsync(HttpContext context)
    {
        context.Response.OnStarting(() =>
        {
            var headers = context.Response.Headers;

            headers["X-Content-Type-Options"] = "nosniff";
            headers["X-Frame-Options"] = "DENY";
            headers["Referrer-Policy"] = "no-referrer";
            headers["Permissions-Policy"] =
                "camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=()";

            headers.Remove("Server");

            return Task.CompletedTask;
        });

        await _next(context);
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Api\Middleware\CorrelationIdMiddleware.cs
====================================================================================================
namespace LIMS.Api.Middleware;

public sealed class CorrelationIdMiddleware
{
    public const string HeaderName = "X-Correlation-ID";
    public const string ItemKey = "CorrelationId";

    private readonly RequestDelegate _next;

    public CorrelationIdMiddleware(RequestDelegate next)
    {
        _next = next;
    }

    public async Task InvokeAsync(HttpContext context)
    {
        var correlationId =
            context.Request.Headers[HeaderName].FirstOrDefault();

        if (!IsValidCorrelationId(correlationId))
        {
            correlationId = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
        }

        context.Items[ItemKey] = correlationId;

        context.Response.OnStarting(() =>
        {
            context.Response.Headers[HeaderName] = correlationId;
            return Task.CompletedTask;
        });

        await _next(context);
    }

    private static bool IsValidCorrelationId(string? value)
    {
        return !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value)
               && value.Length <= 128
               && value.All(c =>
                   char.IsLetterOrDigit(c) ||
                   c is '-' or '_' or '.');
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Api\Middleware\ExceptionHandlingMiddleware.cs
====================================================================================================
using System.Text.Json;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Validation;
using LIMS.Contracts.Common;

namespace LIMS.Api.Middleware;

public sealed class ExceptionHandlingMiddleware
{
    private readonly RequestDelegate _next;
    private readonly ILogger<ExceptionHandlingMiddleware> _logger;

    private static readonly JsonSerializerOptions JsonOptions =
        new(JsonSerializerDefaults.Web);

    public ExceptionHandlingMiddleware(
        RequestDelegate next,
        ILogger<ExceptionHandlingMiddleware> logger)
    {
        _next = next;
        _logger = logger;
    }

    public async Task InvokeAsync(HttpContext context)
    {
        try
        {
            await _next(context);
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException)
            when (context.RequestAborted.IsCancellationRequested)
        {
            _logger.LogInformation(
                "Request cancelled. TraceId: {TraceId}",
                context.TraceIdentifier);
        }
        catch (ValidationException exception)
        {
            await HandleValidationExceptionAsync(context, exception);
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            _logger.LogError(
                exception,
                "Unhandled exception. TraceId: {TraceId}, Path: {Path}",
                context.TraceIdentifier,
                context.Request.Path);

            await HandleExceptionAsync(context);
        }
    }

    private static async Task HandleValidationExceptionAsync(
        HttpContext context,
        ValidationException exception)
    {
        if (context.Response.HasStarted)
            return;

        context.Response.Clear();
        context.Response.StatusCode = StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest;
        context.Response.ContentType = "application/json";

        var errors = exception.Errors
            .Select(x => new ValidationError(
                x.PropertyName,
                x.ErrorMessage,
                x.ErrorCode))
            .ToArray();

        var response = new ValidationErrorResponse(
            Code: "VALIDATION_ERROR",
            Message: "One or more validation errors occurred.",
            TraceId: context.TraceIdentifier,
            Errors: errors);

        await JsonSerializer.SerializeAsync(
            context.Response.Body,
            response,
            JsonOptions,
            context.RequestAborted);
    }

    private static async Task HandleExceptionAsync(HttpContext context)
    {
        if (context.Response.HasStarted)
            return;

        context.Response.Clear();
        context.Response.StatusCode =
            StatusCodes.Status500InternalServerError;

        context.Response.ContentType = "application/json";

        var response = new ErrorResponse(
            Code: "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR",
            Message: "An unexpected error occurred.",
            TraceId: context.TraceIdentifier);

        await JsonSerializer.SerializeAsync(
            context.Response.Body,
            response,
            JsonOptions,
            context.RequestAborted);
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Api\Middleware\GlobalUsings.cs
====================================================================================================
global using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http;
global using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Api\Middleware\RequestLoggingMiddleware.cs
====================================================================================================
namespace LIMS.Api.Middleware;

public sealed class RequestLoggingMiddleware
{
    private readonly RequestDelegate _next;
    private readonly ILogger<RequestLoggingMiddleware> _logger;

    public RequestLoggingMiddleware(
        RequestDelegate next,
        ILogger<RequestLoggingMiddleware> logger)
    {
        _next = next;
        _logger = logger;
    }

    public async Task InvokeAsync(HttpContext context)
    {
        var startedAt = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;
        var correlationId =
            context.Items[CorrelationIdMiddleware.ItemKey]?.ToString();

        using var scope = _logger.BeginScope(
            new Dictionary<string, object?>
            {
                ["CorrelationId"] = correlationId,
                ["TraceId"] = context.TraceIdentifier
            });

        try
        {
            await _next(context);
        }
        finally
        {
            var elapsed = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow - startedAt;

            _logger.LogInformation(
                "HTTP {Method} {Path} responded {StatusCode} in {ElapsedMilliseconds} ms",
                context.Request.Method,
                context.Request.Path,
                context.Response.StatusCode,
                elapsed.TotalMilliseconds);
        }
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Api\Program.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Api.Configuration;
using LIMS.Api.Middleware;
using LIMS.Application;
using LIMS.Infrastructure;

var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

builder.Logging.AddLimsLogging();

builder.Services
    .AddControllers()
    .AddLimsJsonOptions();

builder.Services.AddApiBehaviorConfiguration();
builder.Services.AddApiResponseConfiguration();

builder.Services.AddLimsApiVersioning();
builder.Services.AddLimsRequestSizeLimits();
builder.Services.AddLimsRateLimiting();
builder.Services.AddLimsCors(builder.Configuration);
builder.Services.AddLimsAuthorization();

builder.Services.AddOpenApi();

builder.Services.AddApplication();
builder.Services.AddInfrastructure();

builder.Services.AddLimsHealthChecks();

var app = builder.Build();

app.UseMiddleware<ExceptionHandlingMiddleware>();
app.UseMiddleware<CorrelationIdMiddleware>();
app.UseMiddleware<SecurityHeadersMiddleware>();
app.UseMiddleware<RequestLoggingMiddleware>();

if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
{
    app.MapOpenApi();
}

app.UseHttpsRedirection();

app.UseCors(CorsConfiguration.PolicyName);
app.UseRateLimiter();
app.UseAuthorization();

app.MapControllers()
    .RequireRateLimiting(RateLimitingConfiguration.DefaultPolicy);

app.MapLimsHealthChecks();

app.Run();

public partial class Program;

FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Abstractions\Validation\ValidationException.cs

namespace LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Validation;

public sealed class ValidationException : Exception
{
    public IReadOnlyCollection<ValidationFailure> Errors { get; }

    public ValidationException(IEnumerable<ValidationFailure> errors)
        : base("One or more validation errors occurred.")
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(errors);

        Errors = errors.ToArray();

        if (Errors.Count == 0)
            throw new ArgumentException(
                "At least one validation error is required.",
                nameof(errors));
    }
}

public sealed record ValidationFailure(

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Abstractions\Validation\ValidationResult.cs
====================================================================================================
namespace LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Validation;

public sealed class ValidationResult
{
    private readonly List<ValidationFailure> _errors = [];

    public IReadOnlyCollection<ValidationFailure> Errors => _errors.AsReadOnly();

    public bool IsValid => _errors.Count == 0;

    public bool IsInvalid => !IsValid;

    public ValidationResult() { }

    public ValidationResult(IEnumerable<ValidationFailure> errors)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(errors);
        _errors.AddRange(errors);
    }

    public void AddError(
        string propertyName,
        string errorMessage,
        string? errorCode = null)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(errorMessage))
            throw new ArgumentException(
                "Error message cannot be empty.",
                nameof(errorMessage));

        _errors.Add(new ValidationFailure(
            propertyName,
            errorMessage,
            errorCode));
    }

    public void AddErrors(IEnumerable<ValidationFailure> errors)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(errors);
        _errors.AddRange(errors);
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Behaviors\Dispatcher.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Validation;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;

namespace LIMS.Application.Behaviors;

public sealed class Dispatcher : IDispatcher
{
    private readonly IServiceProvider _serviceProvider;
    private readonly IValidationService _validationService;

    public Dispatcher(
        IServiceProvider serviceProvider,
        IValidationService validationService)
    {
        _serviceProvider = serviceProvider;
        _validationService = validationService;
    }

    public async Task<TResult> SendAsync<TCommand, TResult>(
        TCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        where TCommand : ICommand<TResult>
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(command);

        await _validationService.ValidateAsync(
            command,
            cancellationToken);

        var handler = _serviceProvider.GetRequiredService<
            ICommandHandler<TCommand, TResult>>();

        return await handler.HandleAsync(
            command,
            cancellationToken);
    }

    public async Task<TResult> QueryAsync<TQuery, TResult>(
        TQuery query,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        where TQuery : IQuery<TResult>
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(query);

        var handler = _serviceProvider.GetRequiredService<
            IQueryHandler<TQuery, TResult>>();

        return await handler.HandleAsync(
            query,
            cancellationToken);
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Behaviors\ValidationService.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Validation;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;

namespace LIMS.Application.Behaviors;

public sealed class ValidationService : IValidationService
{
    private readonly IServiceProvider _serviceProvider;

    public ValidationService(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
    {
        _serviceProvider = serviceProvider;
    }

    public async Task ValidateAsync<T>(
        T instance,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(instance);

        var validators = _serviceProvider
            .GetServices<IValidator<T>>()
            .ToArray();

        if (validators.Length == 0)
            return;

        var failures = new List<ValidationFailure>();

        foreach (var validator in validators)
        {
            cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();

            var result = await validator.ValidateAsync(
                instance,
                cancellationToken);

            if (result.IsInvalid)
                failures.AddRange(result.Errors);
        }

        if (failures.Count > 0)
            throw new ValidationException(failures);
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\DependencyInjection.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Behaviors;
using LIMS.Application.Experiments.Handlers;
using LIMS.Application.Instruments.Handlers;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Handlers;
using LIMS.Application.Protocols.Handlers;
using LIMS.Application.Requests;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Handlers;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Handlers.Queries;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Repositories;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Validators;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.Handlers;
using LIMS.Application.Validation;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;

namespace LIMS.Application;

public static class DependencyInjection
{
    public static IServiceCollection AddApplication(
        this IServiceCollection services)
    {
        services.AddScoped<IDispatcher, Dispatcher>();
        services.AddScoped<IValidationService, ValidationService>();
        services.AddRequestsModule();


        RegisterLaboratoryHandlers(services);
        RegisterSampleHandlers(services);
        RegisterExperimentHandlers(services);
        RegisterProtocolHandlers(services);
        RegisterInstrumentHandlers(services);
        RegisterResultHandlers(services);
        RegisterResultValidators(services);

        return services;
    }

    private static void RegisterLaboratoryHandlers(IServiceCollection services)
    {
        services.ScanAndRegisterHandlersFromAssemblyContaining<
            CreateLaboratoryCommandHandler>();
    }

    private static void RegisterSampleHandlers(IServiceCollection services)
    {
        services.ScanAndRegisterHandlersFromAssemblyContaining<
            RegisterSampleCommandHandler>();
    }

    private static void RegisterExperimentHandlers(IServiceCollection services)
    {
        services.ScanAndRegisterHandlersFromAssemblyContaining<
            CreateExperimentCommandHandler>();
    }

    private static void RegisterProtocolHandlers(IServiceCollection services)
    {
        services.ScanAndRegisterHandlersFromAssemblyContaining<
            CreateProtocolCommandHandler>();
    }

    private static void RegisterInstrumentHandlers(IServiceCollection services)
    {
        services.ScanAndRegisterHandlersFromAssemblyContaining<
            RegisterInstrumentCommandHandler>();
    }

    private static void RegisterResultHandlers(IServiceCollection services)
    {
        services.AddScoped<IAnalysisResultRepository, AnalysisResultRepository>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<CreateAnalysisResultCommand, Guid>,
            CreateAnalysisResultCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<UpdateAnalysisResultInformationCommand, bool>,
            UpdateAnalysisResultInformationCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<AddResultEntryCommand, Guid>,
            AddResultEntryCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<UpdateResultEntryCommand, bool>,
            UpdateResultEntryCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<RemoveResultEntryCommand, bool>,
            RemoveResultEntryCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<SubmitAnalysisResultCommand, bool>,
            SubmitAnalysisResultCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<StartAnalysisResultReviewCommand, bool>,
            StartAnalysisResultReviewCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<ApproveAnalysisResultCommand, bool>,
            ApproveAnalysisResultCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<RejectAnalysisResultCommand, bool>,
            RejectAnalysisResultCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<ReturnAnalysisResultToDraftCommand, bool>,
            ReturnAnalysisResultToDraftCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<ArchiveAnalysisResultCommand, bool>,
            ArchiveAnalysisResultCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IQueryHandler<GetAnalysisResultByIdQuery,
                LIMS.Application.Results.DTOs.AnalysisResultDetailsDto?>,
            GetAnalysisResultByIdQueryHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IQueryHandler<GetAnalysisResultByCodeQuery,
                LIMS.Application.Results.DTOs.AnalysisResultDto?>,
            GetAnalysisResultByCodeQueryHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IQueryHandler<GetAnalysisResultsByLaboratoryIdQuery,
                IReadOnlyCollection<LIMS.Application.Results.DTOs.AnalysisResultDto>>,
            GetAnalysisResultsByLaboratoryIdQueryHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IQueryHandler<GetAnalysisResultsByExperimentIdQuery,
                IReadOnlyCollection<LIMS.Application.Results.DTOs.AnalysisResultDto>>,
            GetAnalysisResultsByExperimentIdQueryHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IQueryHandler<GetAnalysisResultsBySampleIdQuery,
                IReadOnlyCollection<LIMS.Application.Results.DTOs.AnalysisResultDto>>,
            GetAnalysisResultsBySampleIdQueryHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IQueryHandler<GetAnalysisResultsByStatusQuery,
                IReadOnlyCollection<LIMS.Application.Results.DTOs.AnalysisResultDto>>,
            GetAnalysisResultsByStatusQueryHandler>();
    }

    private static void RegisterResultValidators(IServiceCollection services)
    {
        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<CreateAnalysisResultCommand>,
            CreateAnalysisResultCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<UpdateAnalysisResultInformationCommand>,
            UpdateAnalysisResultInformationCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<AddResultEntryCommand>,
            AddResultEntryCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<UpdateResultEntryCommand>,
            UpdateResultEntryCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<RemoveResultEntryCommand>,
            RemoveResultEntryCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<SubmitAnalysisResultCommand>,
            SubmitAnalysisResultCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<StartAnalysisResultReviewCommand>,
            StartAnalysisResultReviewCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<ApproveAnalysisResultCommand>,
            ApproveAnalysisResultCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<RejectAnalysisResultCommand>,
            RejectAnalysisResultCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<ReturnAnalysisResultToDraftCommand>,
            ReturnAnalysisResultToDraftCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<ArchiveAnalysisResultCommand>,
            ArchiveAnalysisResultCommandValidator>();
    }
}

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Domain.Experiments;

namespace LIMS.Application.Experiments.Abstractions;

public interface IExperimentRepository : IRepository<Experiment, Guid>
{
    Task<bool> ExistsByCodeAsync(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string experimentCode,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<Experiment?> GetWithDetailsAsync(
        Guid experimentId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<Experiment?> GetByCodeAsync(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string experimentCode,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<IReadOnlyCollection<Experiment>> GetByLaboratoryIdAsync(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<IReadOnlyCollection<Experiment>> GetBySampleIdAsync(
        Guid sampleId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<IReadOnlyCollection<Experiment>> GetByAssignedUserIdAsync(
        string userId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Experiments\Handlers\ArchiveExperimentCommandHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Experiments.Abstractions;
using LIMS.Application.Experiments.Commands;

namespace LIMS.Application.Experiments.Handlers;

public sealed class ArchiveExperimentCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<ArchiveExperimentCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IExperimentRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public ArchiveExperimentCommandHandler(
        IExperimentRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        ArchiveExperimentCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var experiment = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.ExperimentId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (experiment is null)
            return false;

        experiment.Archive(_dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(experiment);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Experiments\Handlers\CreateExperimentCommandHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Experiments.Abstractions;
using LIMS.Application.Experiments.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Abstractions;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Interfaces;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.Abstractions;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.Interfaces;
using LIMS.Domain.Experiments;

namespace LIMS.Application.Experiments.Handlers;

public sealed class CreateExperimentCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<CreateExperimentCommand, Guid>
{
    private readonly IExperimentRepository _experimentRepository;
    private readonly ISampleRepository _sampleRepository;
    private readonly ILaboratoryRepository _laboratoryRepository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public CreateExperimentCommandHandler(
        IExperimentRepository experimentRepository,
        ISampleRepository sampleRepository,
        ILaboratoryRepository laboratoryRepository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _experimentRepository = experimentRepository;
        _sampleRepository = sampleRepository;
        _laboratoryRepository = laboratoryRepository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<Guid> HandleAsync(
        CreateExperimentCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var laboratory = await _laboratoryRepository.GetByIdAsync(
            command.LaboratoryId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (laboratory is null)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                $"Laboratory '{command.LaboratoryId}' was not found.");
        }

        var sample = await _sampleRepository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.SampleId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (sample is null)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                $"Sample '{command.SampleId}' was not found.");
        }

        if (sample.LaboratoryId != command.LaboratoryId)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The sample does not belong to the specified laboratory.");
        }

        if (await _experimentRepository.ExistsByCodeAsync(
                command.LaboratoryId,
                command.ExperimentCode,
                cancellationToken))
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                $"Experiment code '{command.ExperimentCode}' already exists in this laboratory.");
        }

        var experiment = Experiment.Create(
            command.LaboratoryId,
            command.SampleId,
            command.ExperimentCode,
            command.Title,
            command.Description,
            command.CreatedByUserId,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        await _experimentRepository.AddAsync(experiment, cancellationToken);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return experiment.Id;
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Experiments\Handlers\ExperimentMapping.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Experiments.DTOs;
using LIMS.Domain.Experiments;

namespace LIMS.Application.Experiments.Handlers;

internal static class ExperimentMapping
{
    public static ExperimentDto ToDto(this Experiment experiment)
    {
        return new ExperimentDto(
            experiment.Id,
            experiment.LaboratoryId,
            experiment.SampleId,
            experiment.ExperimentCode,
            experiment.Title,
            experiment.Description,
            experiment.Status,
            experiment.CreatedByUserId,
            experiment.AssignedToUserId,
            experiment.CreatedAt,
            experiment.UpdatedAt,
            experiment.StartedAt,
            experiment.CompletedAt,
            experiment.ArchivedAt);
    }

    public static ExperimentDetailsDto ToDetailsDto(this Experiment experiment)
    {
        return new ExperimentDetailsDto(
            experiment.Id,
            experiment.LaboratoryId,
            experiment.SampleId,
            experiment.ExperimentCode,
            experiment.Title,
            experiment.Description,
            experiment.Status,
            experiment.CreatedByUserId,
            experiment.AssignedToUserId,
            experiment.CreatedAt,
            experiment.UpdatedAt,
            experiment.StartedAt,
            experiment.CompletedAt,
            experiment.ArchivedAt);
    }
}

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Experiments.Abstractions;
using LIMS.Application.Experiments.DTOs;
using LIMS.Application.Experiments.Queries;

namespace LIMS.Application.Experiments.Handlers;

public sealed class GetExperimentByCodeQueryHandler
    : IQueryHandler<GetExperimentByCodeQuery, ExperimentDto?>
{
    private readonly IExperimentRepository _repository;

    public GetExperimentByCodeQueryHandler(IExperimentRepository repository)
    {
        _repository = repository;
    }

    public async Task<ExperimentDto?> HandleAsync(
        GetExperimentByCodeQuery query,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var experiment = await _repository.GetByCodeAsync(
            query.LaboratoryId,
            query.ExperimentCode,
            cancellationToken);

        return experiment?.ToDto();
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Experiments\Handlers\StartExperimentCommandHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Experiments.Abstractions;
using LIMS.Application.Experiments.Commands;

namespace LIMS.Application.Experiments.Handlers;

public sealed class StartExperimentCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<StartExperimentCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IExperimentRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public StartExperimentCommandHandler(
        IExperimentRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        StartExperimentCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var experiment = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.ExperimentId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (experiment is null)
            return false;

        experiment.Start(_dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(experiment);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Experiments\Handlers\UnassignExperimentCommandHandler.cs

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Experiments.Abstractions;
using LIMS.Application.Experiments.Commands;

namespace LIMS.Application.Experiments.Handlers;

public sealed class UnassignExperimentCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<UnassignExperimentCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IExperimentRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public UnassignExperimentCommandHandler(
        IExperimentRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        UnassignExperimentCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var experiment = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.ExperimentId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (experiment is null)
            return false;

        experiment.Unassign(_dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(experiment);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Experiments.Abstractions;
using LIMS.Application.Experiments.Commands;

namespace LIMS.Application.Experiments.Handlers;

public sealed class UpdateExperimentInformationCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<UpdateExperimentInformationCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IExperimentRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public UpdateExperimentInformationCommandHandler(
        IExperimentRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        UpdateExperimentInformationCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var experiment = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.ExperimentId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (experiment is null)
            return false;

        experiment.UpdateInformation(
            command.Title,
            command.Description,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(experiment);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

using LIMS.Application.Experiments.Commands;

namespace LIMS.Application.Experiments.Validators;

public sealed class CreateExperimentCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<CreateExperimentCommand>
{
    public CreateExperimentCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.LaboratoryId)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty, "LaboratoryId is required.");

        RuleFor(x => x.SampleId)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty, "SampleId is required.");

        RuleFor(x => x.ExperimentCode)
            .NotEmpty("ExperimentCode is required.")
            .MaximumLength(50, "ExperimentCode cannot exceed 50 characters.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Title)
            .NotEmpty("Title is required.")
            .MaximumLength(200, "Title cannot exceed 200 characters.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Description)
            .MaximumLength(2000, "Description cannot exceed 2000 characters.");

        RuleFor(x => x.CreatedByUserId)
            .NotEmpty("CreatedByUserId is required.")
            .MaximumLength(150, "CreatedByUserId cannot exceed 150 characters.");
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Files\Handlers\FileCommandHandlers.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Storage;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.CQRS;
using LIMS.Application.Files.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Files.Repositories;
using LIMS.Domain.Files;
using LIMS.Domain.Files.Entities;

namespace LIMS.Application.Files.Handlers;

public sealed class RegisterFileCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<RegisterFileCommand, Guid>
{
    private readonly IFileAssetRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public RegisterFileCommandHandler(
        IFileAssetRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<Guid> HandleAsync(
        RegisterFileCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        if (await _repository.ExistsByStorageKeyAsync(
                command.StorageKey,
                cancellationToken))
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "A file with this storage key already exists.");
        }

        var file = FileAsset.Register(
            command.OriginalFileName,
            command.StorageKey,
            command.ContentType,
            command.FileExtension,
            command.SizeInBytes,
            command.Sha256Checksum,
            command.Category,
            command.UploadedByUserId,
            command.Description,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        await _repository.AddAsync(file, cancellationToken);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return file.Id;
    }
}

public sealed class CompleteFileUploadCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<CompleteFileUploadCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IFileAssetRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public CompleteFileUploadCommandHandler(
        IFileAssetRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        CompleteFileUploadCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var file = await _repository.GetByIdAsync(
            command.FileId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (file is null)
            return false;

        file.MarkAvailable(_dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(file);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

public sealed class QuarantineFileCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<QuarantineFileCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IFileAssetRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public QuarantineFileCommandHandler(
        IFileAssetRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        QuarantineFileCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var file = await _repository.GetByIdAsync(
            command.FileId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (file is null)
            return false;

        file.Quarantine(command.Reason, _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(file);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

{
    private readonly IFileAssetRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;

        IFileAssetRepository repository,

    {
        _repository = repository;

    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(

    {
        var file = await _repository.GetByIdAsync(

            cancellationToken);

        if (file is null)
            return false;

        file.Reject(command.Reason);

        _repository.Update(file);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

public sealed class DeleteFileCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<DeleteFileCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IFileAssetRepository _repository;
    private readonly IObjectStorage _objectStorage;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public DeleteFileCommandHandler(
        IFileAssetRepository repository,
        IObjectStorage objectStorage,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _objectStorage = objectStorage;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        DeleteFileCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var file = await _repository.GetByIdAsync(
            command.FileId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (file is null)
            return false;

        file.Delete(
            command.DeletedByUserId,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(file);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        await _objectStorage.DeleteAsync(
            file.StorageKey,
            cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

public sealed class UpdateFileDescriptionCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<UpdateFileDescriptionCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IFileAssetRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;

    public UpdateFileDescriptionCommandHandler(
        IFileAssetRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        UpdateFileDescriptionCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var file = await _repository.GetByIdAsync(
            command.FileId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (file is null)
            return false;

        file.UpdateDescription(command.Description);

        _repository.Update(file);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

public sealed class LinkFileCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<LinkFileCommand, Guid>
{
    private readonly IFileAssetRepository _fileRepository;
    private readonly IFileReferenceRepository _referenceRepository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public LinkFileCommandHandler(
        IFileAssetRepository fileRepository,
        IFileReferenceRepository referenceRepository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _fileRepository = fileRepository;
        _referenceRepository = referenceRepository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<Guid> HandleAsync(
        LinkFileCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var file = await _fileRepository.GetByIdAsync(
            command.FileId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (file is null)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("File was not found.");

        if (file.Status != Domain.Files.Enums.FileStatus.Available)
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "Only available files can be linked.");

        if (await _referenceRepository.ExistsAsync(
                command.FileId,
                command.ReferenceType,
                command.ReferenceId,
                cancellationToken))
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "This file is already linked to the specified resource.");
        }

        var reference = new FileReference(
            command.FileId,
            command.ReferenceType,
            command.ReferenceId,
            command.LinkedByUserId,
            command.Purpose,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        await _referenceRepository.AddAsync(reference, cancellationToken);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return reference.Id;
    }
}

public sealed class UnlinkFileCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<UnlinkFileCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IFileReferenceRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;

    public UnlinkFileCommandHandler(
        IFileReferenceRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        UnlinkFileCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var reference = await _repository.GetAsync(
            command.FileReferenceId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (reference is null)
            return false;

        _repository.Remove(reference);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.CQRS;
using LIMS.Application.Files.DTOs;
using LIMS.Application.Files.Mapping;
using LIMS.Application.Files.Queries;
using LIMS.Application.Files.Repositories;
using LIMS.Domain.Files.Enums;

namespace LIMS.Application.Files.Handlers;

public sealed class GetFileByIdQueryHandler
    : IQueryHandler<GetFileByIdQuery, FileAssetDto?>
{
    private readonly IFileAssetRepository _repository;

    public GetFileByIdQueryHandler(IFileAssetRepository repository)
    {
        _repository = repository;
    }

    public async Task<FileAssetDto?> HandleAsync(
        GetFileByIdQuery query,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var file = await _repository.GetByIdAsync(
            query.FileId,
            cancellationToken);

        return file?.ToDto();
    }
}

public sealed class GetFilesByReferenceQueryHandler
    : IQueryHandler<
        GetFilesByReferenceQuery,
        IReadOnlyCollection<FileAssetDto>>
{
    private readonly IFileReferenceRepository _referenceRepository;
    private readonly IFileAssetRepository _fileRepository;

    public GetFilesByReferenceQueryHandler(
        IFileReferenceRepository referenceRepository,
        IFileAssetRepository fileRepository)
    {
        _referenceRepository = referenceRepository;
        _fileRepository = fileRepository;
    }

    public async Task<IReadOnlyCollection<FileAssetDto>> HandleAsync(
        GetFilesByReferenceQuery query,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var references = await _referenceRepository.GetByReferenceAsync(
            query.ReferenceType,
            query.ReferenceId,
            cancellationToken);

        var files = new List<FileAssetDto>();

        foreach (var reference in references)
        {
            var file = await _fileRepository.GetByIdAsync(
                reference.FileId,
                cancellationToken);

            if (file is not null && file.Status != FileStatus.Deleted)
                files.Add(file.ToDto());
        }

        return files;
    }
}

public sealed class GetFileReferencesQueryHandler
    : IQueryHandler<
        GetFileReferencesQuery,
        IReadOnlyCollection<FileReferenceDto>>
{
    private readonly IFileReferenceRepository _repository;

    public GetFileReferencesQueryHandler(
        IFileReferenceRepository repository)
    {
        _repository = repository;
    }

    public async Task<IReadOnlyCollection<FileReferenceDto>> HandleAsync(
        GetFileReferencesQuery query,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var references = await _repository.GetByFileIdAsync(
            query.FileId,
            cancellationToken);

        return references.Select(x => x.ToDto()).ToArray();
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Files\Mapping\FileMapping.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Files.DTOs;
using LIMS.Domain.Files;
using LIMS.Domain.Files.Entities;

namespace LIMS.Application.Files.Mapping;

public static class FileMapping
{
    public static FileAssetDto ToDto(this FileAsset file)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(file);

        return new FileAssetDto(
            file.Id,
            file.OriginalFileName,
            file.ContentType,
            file.FileExtension,
            file.SizeInBytes,
            file.Sha256Checksum,
            file.Category,
            file.Status,
            file.UploadedByUserId,
            file.Description,
            file.QuarantineReason,
            file.RejectionReason,
            file.UploadedAt,
            file.AvailableAt,
            file.QuarantinedAt,
            file.DeletedAt);
    }

    public static FileReferenceDto ToDto(this FileReference reference)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(reference);

        return new FileReferenceDto(
            reference.Id,
            reference.FileId,
            reference.ReferenceType,
            reference.ReferenceId,
            reference.LinkedByUserId,
            reference.Purpose,
            reference.LinkedAt);
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Files\Validators\FileCommandValidators.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Files.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Validation;

namespace LIMS.Application.Files.Validators;

public sealed class RegisterFileCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<RegisterFileCommand>
{
    public RegisterFileCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.OriginalFileName)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(255);

        RuleFor(x => x.StorageKey)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(500);

        RuleFor(x => x.ContentType)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(150);

        RuleFor(x => x.FileExtension)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(20);

        RuleFor(x => x.SizeInBytes)
            .Must(x => x > 0)
            .WithMessage("File size must be greater than zero.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Sha256Checksum)
            .NotEmpty()
            .Must(x => x.Length == 64 && x.All(Uri.IsHexDigit))
            .WithMessage("A valid SHA-256 checksum is required.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Category)
            .Must(x => Enum.IsDefined(x))
            .WithMessage("File category is invalid.");

        RuleFor(x => x.UploadedByUserId)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(150);

        RuleFor(x => x.Description).MaximumLength(2000);
    }
}

public sealed class FileIdCommandValidator<TCommand>
    : AbstractValidator<TCommand>
    where TCommand : class
{
    public FileIdCommandValidator(
        System.Linq.Expressions.Expression<Func<TCommand, Guid>> idExpression)
    {
        RuleFor(idExpression)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty)
            .WithMessage("File ID is required.");
    }
}

public sealed class QuarantineFileCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<QuarantineFileCommand>
{
    public QuarantineFileCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.FileId)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty)
            .WithMessage("File ID is required.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Reason)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(2000);
    }
}

public sealed class RejectFileCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<RejectFileCommand>
{
    public RejectFileCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.FileId)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty)
            .WithMessage("File ID is required.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Reason)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(2000);
    }
}

public sealed class DeleteFileCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<DeleteFileCommand>
{
    public DeleteFileCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.FileId)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty)
            .WithMessage("File ID is required.");

        RuleFor(x => x.DeletedByUserId)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(150);
    }
}

public sealed class LinkFileCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<LinkFileCommand>
{
    public LinkFileCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.FileId)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty)
            .WithMessage("File ID is required.");

        RuleFor(x => x.ReferenceType)
            .Must(x => Enum.IsDefined(x))
            .WithMessage("Reference type is invalid.");

        RuleFor(x => x.ReferenceId)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty)
            .WithMessage("Reference ID is required.");

        RuleFor(x => x.LinkedByUserId)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(150);

        RuleFor(x => x.Purpose).MaximumLength(500);
    }
}

public sealed class UnlinkFileCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<UnlinkFileCommand>
{
    public UnlinkFileCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.FileReferenceId)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty)
            .WithMessage("File reference ID is required.");
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Instruments\Handlers\InstrumentMaintenanceCommandHandlers.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Instruments.Abstractions;
using LIMS.Domain.Instruments;

namespace LIMS.Application.Instruments.Handlers;

public sealed class ScheduleInstrumentMaintenanceCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<Commands.ScheduleInstrumentMaintenanceCommand, Guid>
{
    private readonly IInstrumentRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public ScheduleInstrumentMaintenanceCommandHandler(
        IInstrumentRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<Guid> HandleAsync(
        Commands.ScheduleInstrumentMaintenanceCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var instrument = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(command.InstrumentId, cancellationToken)
            ?? throw new InstrumentDomainException("Instrument was not found.");

        instrument.ScheduleMaintenance(
            command.Type,
            command.Description,
            command.ScheduledAt,
            command.ScheduledByUserId,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        var maintenanceId = instrument.MaintenanceRecords.Last().Id;

        _repository.Update(instrument);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return maintenanceId;
    }
}

public sealed class CompleteInstrumentMaintenanceCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<Commands.CompleteInstrumentMaintenanceCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IInstrumentRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public CompleteInstrumentMaintenanceCommandHandler(
        IInstrumentRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        Commands.CompleteInstrumentMaintenanceCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var instrument = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(command.InstrumentId, cancellationToken);
        if (instrument is null) return false;

        instrument.CompleteMaintenance(
            command.MaintenanceId,
            command.CompletedByUserId,
            command.CompletionNotes,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(instrument);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);
        return true;
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Instruments\Handlers\RegisterInstrumentCommandHandler.cs

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Instruments.Abstractions;
using LIMS.Domain.Instruments;

namespace LIMS.Application.Instruments.Handlers;

public sealed class RegisterInstrumentCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<Commands.RegisterInstrumentCommand, Guid>
{
    private readonly IInstrumentRepository _instrumentRepository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public RegisterInstrumentCommandHandler(
        IInstrumentRepository instrumentRepository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _instrumentRepository = instrumentRepository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<Guid> HandleAsync(
        Commands.RegisterInstrumentCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var exists = await _instrumentRepository.ExistsByCodeAsync(
            command.LaboratoryId,
            command.InstrumentCode,
            cancellationToken);

        if (exists)
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("An instrument with this code already exists in the laboratory.");

        var instrument = Instrument.Register(
            command.LaboratoryId,
            command.InstrumentCode,
            command.Name,
            command.Type,
            command.Manufacturer,
            command.Model,
            command.SerialNumber,
            command.Location,
            command.Description,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        await _instrumentRepository.AddAsync(instrument, cancellationToken);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return instrument.Id;
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Instruments\Handlers\UpdateInstrumentInformationCommandHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Instruments.Abstractions;
using LIMS.Domain.Instruments;

namespace LIMS.Application.Instruments.Handlers;

public sealed class UpdateInstrumentInformationCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<Commands.UpdateInstrumentInformationCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IInstrumentRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public UpdateInstrumentInformationCommandHandler(
        IInstrumentRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        Commands.UpdateInstrumentInformationCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var instrument = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.InstrumentId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (instrument is null)
            return false;

        instrument.UpdateInformation(
            command.Name,
            command.Type,
            command.Manufacturer,
            command.Model,
            command.SerialNumber,
            command.Location,
            command.Description,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(instrument);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Domain.Instruments;
using LIMS.Domain.Instruments.Enums;

namespace LIMS.Application.Instruments.Repositories;

public interface IInstrumentRepository : IRepository<Instrument, Guid>
{
    Task<bool> ExistsByCodeAsync(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string instrumentCode,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<Instrument?> GetWithDetailsAsync(
        Guid instrumentId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<Instrument?> GetByCodeAsync(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string instrumentCode,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<IReadOnlyCollection<Instrument>> GetByLaboratoryIdAsync(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<IReadOnlyCollection<Instrument>> GetByStatusAsync(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        InstrumentStatus status,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Inventory\Handlers\InventoryCommandHandlers.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.CQRS;
using LIMS.Application.Inventory.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Inventory.Repositories;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Repositories;
using LIMS.Domain.Inventory;

namespace LIMS.Application.Inventory.Handlers;

public sealed class CreateInventoryItemCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<CreateInventoryItemCommand, Guid>
{
    private readonly IInventoryItemRepository _repository;
    private readonly ILaboratoryRepository _laboratoryRepository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public CreateInventoryItemCommandHandler(
        IInventoryItemRepository repository,
        ILaboratoryRepository laboratoryRepository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _laboratoryRepository = laboratoryRepository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<Guid> HandleAsync(
        CreateInventoryItemCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var laboratory = await _laboratoryRepository.GetByIdAsync(
            command.LaboratoryId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (laboratory is null)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Laboratory was not found.");

        if (await _repository.ExistsByCodeAsync(
                command.LaboratoryId,
                command.ItemCode,
                cancellationToken))
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                $"Inventory item code '{command.ItemCode}' already exists.");
        }

        var item = InventoryItem.Create(
            command.LaboratoryId,
            command.ItemCode,
            command.Name,
            command.Description,
            command.Type,
            command.CasNumber,
            command.ChemicalFormula,
            command.MolecularWeight,
            command.HazardClassification,
            command.SafetyDataSheetReference,
            command.DefaultUnit,
            command.MinimumStockLevel,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        await _repository.AddAsync(item, cancellationToken);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return item.Id;
    }
}

public sealed class UpdateInventoryItemCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<UpdateInventoryItemCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IInventoryItemRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public UpdateInventoryItemCommandHandler(
        IInventoryItemRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        UpdateInventoryItemCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var item = await _repository.GetByIdAsync(
            command.InventoryItemId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (item is null)
            return false;

        item.UpdateInformation(
            command.Name,
            command.Description,
            command.Type,
            command.CasNumber,
            command.ChemicalFormula,
            command.MolecularWeight,
            command.HazardClassification,
            command.SafetyDataSheetReference,
            command.DefaultUnit,
            command.MinimumStockLevel,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(item);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

public sealed class ReceiveInventoryStockCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<ReceiveInventoryStockCommand, Guid>
{
    private readonly IInventoryItemRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public ReceiveInventoryStockCommandHandler(
        IInventoryItemRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<Guid> HandleAsync(
        ReceiveInventoryStockCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var item = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.InventoryItemId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (item is null)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Inventory item was not found.");

        var lotId = item.ReceiveStock(
            command.LotNumber,
            command.Supplier,
            command.Manufacturer,
            command.Quantity,
            command.Unit,
            command.MinimumStockLevel,
            command.ManufactureDate,
            command.ExpiryDate,
            command.StorageLocation,
            command.StorageConditions,
            command.CertificateOfAnalysisNumber,
            command.PerformedByUserId,
            command.ReferenceNumber,
            command.Notes,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(item);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return lotId;
    }
}

public sealed class ConsumeInventoryStockCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<ConsumeInventoryStockCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IInventoryItemRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public ConsumeInventoryStockCommandHandler(
        IInventoryItemRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        ConsumeInventoryStockCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var item = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.InventoryItemId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (item is null)
            return false;

        item.ConsumeStock(
            command.LotId,
            command.Quantity,
            command.ConsumedByUserId,
            command.ExperimentId,
            command.Notes,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(item);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

public sealed class AdjustInventoryStockCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<AdjustInventoryStockCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IInventoryItemRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public AdjustInventoryStockCommandHandler(
        IInventoryItemRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        AdjustInventoryStockCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var item = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.InventoryItemId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (item is null)
            return false;

        item.AdjustStock(
            command.LotId,
            command.Quantity,
            command.Increase,
            command.PerformedByUserId,
            command.Reason,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(item);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

public sealed class QuarantineInventoryLotCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<QuarantineInventoryLotCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IInventoryItemRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public QuarantineInventoryLotCommandHandler(
        IInventoryItemRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        QuarantineInventoryLotCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var item = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.InventoryItemId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (item is null)
            return false;

        item.QuarantineLot(command.LotId, _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);
        _repository.Update(item);

        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);
        return true;
    }
}

public sealed class ReleaseInventoryLotCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<ReleaseInventoryLotCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IInventoryItemRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public ReleaseInventoryLotCommandHandler(
        IInventoryItemRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        ReleaseInventoryLotCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var item = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.InventoryItemId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (item is null)
            return false;

        var now = _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow;

        item.ReleaseLotFromQuarantine(
            command.LotId,
            DateOnly.FromDateTime(now),
            now);

        _repository.Update(item);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

public sealed class DisposeInventoryLotCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<DisposeInventoryLotCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IInventoryItemRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public DisposeInventoryLotCommandHandler(
        IInventoryItemRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        DisposeInventoryLotCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var item = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.InventoryItemId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (item is null)
            return false;

        item.DisposeLot(
            command.LotId,
            command.PerformedByUserId,
            command.Reason,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(item);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

public sealed class DeactivateInventoryItemCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<DeactivateInventoryItemCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IInventoryItemRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public DeactivateInventoryItemCommandHandler(
        IInventoryItemRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        DeactivateInventoryItemCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        return ChangeStatusAsync(
            command.InventoryItemId,
            static (item, now) => item.Deactivate(now),
            cancellationToken);
    }

    private async Task<bool> ChangeStatusAsync(
        Guid id,
        Action<InventoryItem, DateTime> operation,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var item = await _repository.GetByIdAsync(id, cancellationToken);
        if (item is null)
            return false;

        operation(item, _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);
        _repository.Update(item);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);
        return true;
    }
}

public sealed class ReactivateInventoryItemCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<ReactivateInventoryItemCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IInventoryItemRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
