using LIMS.Application.CQRS;
using LIMS.Application.Inventory.DTOs;
using LIMS.Application.Inventory.Mapping;
using LIMS.Application.Inventory.Queries;
using LIMS.Application.Inventory.Repositories;

namespace LIMS.Application.Inventory.Handlers;

public sealed class GetInventoryItemByIdQueryHandler
    : IQueryHandler<GetInventoryItemByIdQuery, InventoryItemDetailsDto?>
{
    private readonly IInventoryItemRepository _repository;

    public GetInventoryItemByIdQueryHandler(
        IInventoryItemRepository repository)
    {
        _repository = repository;
    }

    public async Task<InventoryItemDetailsDto?> HandleAsync(
        GetInventoryItemByIdQuery query,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var item = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            query.InventoryItemId,
            cancellationToken);

        return item?.ToDetailsDto();
    }
}

public sealed class GetInventoryItemByCodeQueryHandler
    : IQueryHandler<GetInventoryItemByCodeQuery, InventoryItemDto?>
{
    private readonly IInventoryItemRepository _repository;

    public GetInventoryItemByCodeQueryHandler(
        IInventoryItemRepository repository)
    {
        _repository = repository;
    }

    public async Task<InventoryItemDto?> HandleAsync(
        GetInventoryItemByCodeQuery query,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var item = await _repository.GetByCodeAsync(
            query.LaboratoryId,
            query.ItemCode,
            cancellationToken);

        return item?.ToDto();
    }
}

public sealed class GetInventoryItemsByLaboratoryQueryHandler
    : IQueryHandler<
        GetInventoryItemsByLaboratoryQuery,
        IReadOnlyCollection<InventoryItemDto>>
{
    private readonly IInventoryItemRepository _repository;

    public GetInventoryItemsByLaboratoryQueryHandler(
        IInventoryItemRepository repository)
    {
        _repository = repository;
    }

    public async Task<IReadOnlyCollection<InventoryItemDto>> HandleAsync(
        GetInventoryItemsByLaboratoryQuery query,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var items = await _repository.GetByLaboratoryIdAsync(
            query.LaboratoryId,
            query.Status,
            query.Type,
            query.PageNumber,
            query.PageSize,
            cancellationToken);

        return items.Select(x => x.ToDto()).ToArray();
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Inventory\Mapping\InventoryMapping.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Inventory.DTOs;
using LIMS.Domain.Inventory;
using LIMS.Domain.Inventory.Entities;

namespace LIMS.Application.Inventory.Mapping;

public static class InventoryMapping
{
    public static InventoryItemDto ToDto(this InventoryItem item)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(item);

        return new InventoryItemDto(
            item.Id,
            item.LaboratoryId,
            item.ItemCode,
            item.Name,
            item.Description,
            item.Type,
            item.Status,
            item.CasNumber,
            item.ChemicalFormula,
            item.MolecularWeight,
            item.HazardClassification,
            item.SafetyDataSheetReference,
            item.DefaultUnit,
            item.MinimumStockLevel,
            item.TotalAvailableQuantity,
            item.CreatedAt,
            item.UpdatedAt);
    }

    public static InventoryLotDto ToDto(this InventoryLot lot)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(lot);

        return new InventoryLotDto(
            lot.Id,
            lot.LotNumber,
            lot.Supplier,
            lot.Manufacturer,
            lot.InitialQuantity,
            lot.AvailableQuantity,
            lot.Unit,
            lot.MinimumStockLevel,
            lot.ManufactureDate,
            lot.ExpiryDate,
            lot.StorageLocation,
            lot.StorageConditions,
            lot.CertificateOfAnalysisNumber,
            lot.Status,
            lot.ReceivedAt);
    }

    public static InventoryMovementDto ToDto(this InventoryMovement movement)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(movement);

        return new InventoryMovementDto(
            movement.Id,
            movement.LotId,
            movement.Type,
            movement.Quantity,
            movement.BalanceAfterMovement,
            movement.Unit,
            movement.PerformedByUserId,
            movement.ExperimentId,
            movement.ReferenceNumber,
            movement.Notes,
            movement.OccurredAt);
    }

    public static InventoryItemDetailsDto ToDetailsDto(
        this InventoryItem item)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(item);

        return new InventoryItemDetailsDto(
            item.ToDto(),
            item.Lots.Select(x => x.ToDto()).ToArray(),
            item.Movements.Select(x => x.ToDto()).ToArray());
    }
}

using LIMS.Domain.Inventory;
using LIMS.Domain.Inventory.Enums;

namespace LIMS.Application.Inventory.Repositories;

public interface IInventoryItemRepository
{
    Task<bool> ExistsByCodeAsync(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string itemCode,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<InventoryItem?> GetByIdAsync(
        Guid inventoryItemId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<InventoryItem?> GetWithDetailsAsync(
        Guid inventoryItemId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<InventoryItem?> GetByCodeAsync(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string itemCode,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<IReadOnlyCollection<InventoryItem>> GetByLaboratoryIdAsync(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        InventoryItemStatus? status,
        InventoryItemType? type,
        int pageNumber,
        int pageSize,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task AddAsync(
        InventoryItem item,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    void Update(InventoryItem item);
}

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Validation;
using LIMS.Application.Inventory.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Validation;

namespace LIMS.Application.Inventory.Validators;

public sealed class CreateInventoryItemCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<CreateInventoryItemCommand>
{
    public CreateInventoryItemCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.LaboratoryId)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty)
            .WithMessage("Laboratory ID is required.");

        RuleFor(x => x.ItemCode)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(50)
            .Matches("^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$")
            .WithMessage("Item code contains invalid characters.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Name)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(200);

        RuleFor(x => x.Description)
            .MaximumLength(2000);

        RuleFor(x => x.Type)
            .Must(x => Enum.IsDefined(x))
            .WithMessage("Inventory item type is invalid.");

        RuleFor(x => x.CasNumber)
            .MaximumLength(50);

        RuleFor(x => x.ChemicalFormula)
            .MaximumLength(100);

        RuleFor(x => x.MolecularWeight)
            .MaximumLength(50);

        RuleFor(x => x.HazardClassification)
            .MaximumLength(1000);

        RuleFor(x => x.SafetyDataSheetReference)
            .MaximumLength(500);

        RuleFor(x => x.DefaultUnit)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(30);

        RuleFor(x => x.MinimumStockLevel)
            .Must(x => x >= 0)
            .WithMessage("Minimum stock level cannot be negative.");
    }
}

public sealed class UpdateInventoryItemCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<UpdateInventoryItemCommand>
{
    public UpdateInventoryItemCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.InventoryItemId)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty)
            .WithMessage("Inventory item ID is required.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Name)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(200);

        RuleFor(x => x.Description)
            .MaximumLength(2000);

        RuleFor(x => x.Type)
            .Must(x => Enum.IsDefined(x))
            .WithMessage("Inventory item type is invalid.");

        RuleFor(x => x.CasNumber).MaximumLength(50);
        RuleFor(x => x.ChemicalFormula).MaximumLength(100);
        RuleFor(x => x.MolecularWeight).MaximumLength(50);
        RuleFor(x => x.HazardClassification).MaximumLength(1000);
        RuleFor(x => x.SafetyDataSheetReference).MaximumLength(500);

        RuleFor(x => x.DefaultUnit)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(30);

        RuleFor(x => x.MinimumStockLevel)
            .Must(x => x >= 0)
            .WithMessage("Minimum stock level cannot be negative.");
    }
}

public sealed class ReceiveInventoryStockCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<ReceiveInventoryStockCommand>
{
    public ReceiveInventoryStockCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.InventoryItemId)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty)
            .WithMessage("Inventory item ID is required.");

        RuleFor(x => x.LotNumber)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(100);

        RuleFor(x => x.Supplier).MaximumLength(200);
        RuleFor(x => x.Manufacturer).MaximumLength(200);

        RuleFor(x => x.Quantity)
            .Must(x => x > 0)
            .WithMessage("Quantity must be greater than zero.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Unit)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(30);

        RuleFor(x => x.MinimumStockLevel)
            .Must(x => !x.HasValue || x.Value >= 0)
            .WithMessage("Minimum stock level cannot be negative.");

        RuleFor(x => x.StorageLocation).MaximumLength(200);
        RuleFor(x => x.StorageConditions).MaximumLength(1000);
        RuleFor(x => x.CertificateOfAnalysisNumber).MaximumLength(150);

        RuleFor(x => x.PerformedByUserId)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(150);

        RuleFor(x => x.ReferenceNumber).MaximumLength(150);
        RuleFor(x => x.Notes).MaximumLength(2000);

        RuleFor(x => x.ExpiryDate)
            .Must((command, expiryDate) =>
                !expiryDate.HasValue
                || !command.ManufactureDate.HasValue
                || expiryDate.Value >= command.ManufactureDate.Value)
            .WithMessage(
                "Expiry date cannot be earlier than manufacture date.");
    }
}

public sealed class ConsumeInventoryStockCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<ConsumeInventoryStockCommand>
{
    public ConsumeInventoryStockCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.InventoryItemId)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty)
            .WithMessage("Inventory item ID is required.");

        RuleFor(x => x.LotId)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty)
            .WithMessage("Lot ID is required.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Quantity)
            .Must(x => x > 0)
            .WithMessage("Quantity must be greater than zero.");

        RuleFor(x => x.ConsumedByUserId)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(150);

        RuleFor(x => x.ExperimentId)
            .Must(x => !x.HasValue || x.Value != Guid.Empty)
            .WithMessage("Experiment ID cannot be empty.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Notes).MaximumLength(2000);
    }
}

public sealed class AdjustInventoryStockCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<AdjustInventoryStockCommand>
{
    public AdjustInventoryStockCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.InventoryItemId)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty)
            .WithMessage("Inventory item ID is required.");

        RuleFor(x => x.LotId)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty)
            .WithMessage("Lot ID is required.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Quantity)
            .Must(x => x > 0)
            .WithMessage("Quantity must be greater than zero.");

        RuleFor(x => x.PerformedByUserId)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(150);

        RuleFor(x => x.Reason)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(2000);
    }
}

    where TCommand : class

        Func<TCommand, Guid> idSelector)

            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty)
            .WithMessage("Inventory item ID is required.");
    }

        RuleFor(x => x.ItemCode)
            .NotEmpty()

    : AbstractValidator<GetInventoryItemsByLaboratoryQuery>
{
    public GetInventoryItemsByLaboratoryQueryValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.LaboratoryId)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty)
            .WithMessage("Laboratory ID is required.");

        RuleFor(x => x.PageNumber)
            .Must(x => x >= 1)
            .WithMessage("Page number must be greater than zero.");

        RuleFor(x => x.PageSize)
            .Must(x => x is >= 1 and <= 200)
            .WithMessage("Page size must be between 1 and 200.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Status)
            .Must(x => !x.HasValue || Enum.IsDefined(x.Value))
            .WithMessage("Inventory status is invalid.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Type)
            .Must(x => !x.HasValue || Enum.IsDefined(x.Value))
            .WithMessage("Inventory item type is invalid.");
    }

FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Laboratories\Handlers\AddLaboratoryMemberCommandHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Interfaces;

namespace LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Handlers;

public sealed class AddLaboratoryMemberCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<AddLaboratoryMemberCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly ILaboratoryRepository _laboratoryRepository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public AddLaboratoryMemberCommandHandler(
        ILaboratoryRepository laboratoryRepository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _laboratoryRepository = laboratoryRepository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        AddLaboratoryMemberCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(command);

        if (command.LaboratoryId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new ArgumentException("Laboratory identifier is required.");

        var laboratory = await _laboratoryRepository.GetWithMembersAsync(
            command.LaboratoryId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (laboratory is null)
            throw new KeyNotFoundException("Laboratory was not found.");

        laboratory.AddMember(
            command.UserId,
            command.Role,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Laboratories\Handlers\ChangeLaboratoryStatusCommandHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Interfaces;

namespace LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Handlers;

public sealed class ChangeLaboratoryStatusCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<ChangeLaboratoryStatusCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly ILaboratoryRepository _laboratoryRepository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public ChangeLaboratoryStatusCommandHandler(
        ILaboratoryRepository laboratoryRepository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _laboratoryRepository = laboratoryRepository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        ChangeLaboratoryStatusCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(command);

        if (command.LaboratoryId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new ArgumentException("Laboratory identifier is required.");

        var laboratory = await _laboratoryRepository.GetByIdAsync(
            command.LaboratoryId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (laboratory is null)
            throw new KeyNotFoundException("Laboratory was not found.");

        laboratory.ChangeStatus(
            command.Status,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Laboratories\Handlers\CreateLaboratoryCommandHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Interfaces;
using LIMS.Domain.Laboratories;

namespace LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Handlers;

public sealed class CreateLaboratoryCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<CreateLaboratoryCommand, Guid>
{
    private readonly ILaboratoryRepository _laboratoryRepository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public CreateLaboratoryCommandHandler(
        ILaboratoryRepository laboratoryRepository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _laboratoryRepository = laboratoryRepository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<Guid> HandleAsync(
        CreateLaboratoryCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(command);

        var normalizedCode = command.Code?.Trim().ToUpperInvariant();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalizedCode))
            throw new ArgumentException("Laboratory code is required.");

        if (await _laboratoryRepository.ExistsByCodeAsync(
                normalizedCode,
                cancellationToken))
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                $"A laboratory with code '{normalizedCode}' already exists.");
        }

        var laboratory = Laboratory.Create(
            command.Name,
            normalizedCode,
            command.Description,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        await _laboratoryRepository.AddAsync(
            laboratory,
            cancellationToken);

        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return laboratory.Id;
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Laboratories\Handlers\GetLaboratoriesByUserIdQueryHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.DTOs;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Interfaces;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Queries;

namespace LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Handlers;

public sealed class GetLaboratoriesByUserIdQueryHandler
    : IQueryHandler<
        GetLaboratoriesByUserIdQuery,
        IReadOnlyCollection<LaboratoryDto>>
{
    private readonly ILaboratoryRepository _laboratoryRepository;

    public GetLaboratoriesByUserIdQueryHandler(
        ILaboratoryRepository laboratoryRepository)
    {
        _laboratoryRepository = laboratoryRepository;
    }

    public async Task<IReadOnlyCollection<LaboratoryDto>> HandleAsync(
        GetLaboratoriesByUserIdQuery query,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(query);

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(query.UserId))
            throw new ArgumentException("User identifier is required.");

        var laboratories = await _laboratoryRepository.GetByUserIdAsync(
            query.UserId.Trim(),
            cancellationToken);

        return laboratories
            .Select(laboratory => new LaboratoryDto(
                laboratory.Id,
                laboratory.Name,
                laboratory.Code,
                laboratory.Description,
                laboratory.Status,
                laboratory.CreatedAt,
                laboratory.UpdatedAt,
                laboratory.Members.Count))
            .ToArray();
    }
}

using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Interfaces;

    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(query);

            throw new ArgumentException("Laboratory code is required.");

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Laboratories\Handlers\GetLaboratoryByIdQueryHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.DTOs;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Interfaces;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Queries;

namespace LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Handlers;

public sealed class GetLaboratoryByIdQueryHandler
    : IQueryHandler<GetLaboratoryByIdQuery, LaboratoryDetailsDto?>
{
    private readonly ILaboratoryRepository _laboratoryRepository;

    public GetLaboratoryByIdQueryHandler(
        ILaboratoryRepository laboratoryRepository)
    {
        _laboratoryRepository = laboratoryRepository;
    }

    public async Task<LaboratoryDetailsDto?> HandleAsync(
        GetLaboratoryByIdQuery query,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(query);

        if (query.LaboratoryId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new ArgumentException("Laboratory identifier is required.");

        var laboratory = await _laboratoryRepository.GetWithMembersAsync(
            query.LaboratoryId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (laboratory is null)
            return null;

        var members = laboratory.Members
            .Select(member => new LaboratoryMemberDto(
                member.Id,
                member.UserId,
                member.Role,
                member.JoinedAt))
            .ToArray();

        return new LaboratoryDetailsDto(
            laboratory.Id,
            laboratory.Name,
            laboratory.Code,
            laboratory.Description,
            laboratory.Status,
            laboratory.CreatedAt,
            laboratory.UpdatedAt,
            members);
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Laboratories\Handlers\UpdateLaboratoryInformationCommandHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Interfaces;

namespace LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Handlers;

public sealed class UpdateLaboratoryInformationCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<UpdateLaboratoryInformationCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly ILaboratoryRepository _laboratoryRepository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public UpdateLaboratoryInformationCommandHandler(
        ILaboratoryRepository laboratoryRepository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _laboratoryRepository = laboratoryRepository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        UpdateLaboratoryInformationCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(command);

        if (command.LaboratoryId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new ArgumentException("Laboratory identifier is required.");

        var normalizedCode = command.Code?.Trim().ToUpperInvariant();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalizedCode))
            throw new ArgumentException("Laboratory code is required.");

        var laboratory = await _laboratoryRepository.GetByIdAsync(
            command.LaboratoryId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (laboratory is null)
            throw new KeyNotFoundException("Laboratory was not found.");

        var codeExists = await _laboratoryRepository.ExistsByCodeAsync(
            normalizedCode,
            cancellationToken);

        if (codeExists &&
            !string.Equals(
                laboratory.Code,
                normalizedCode,
                StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                $"A laboratory with code '{normalizedCode}' already exists.");
        }

        laboratory.UpdateInformation(
            command.Name,
            normalizedCode,
            command.Description,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Protocols.Abstractions;
using LIMS.Application.Protocols.Commands;

namespace LIMS.Application.Protocols.Handlers;

public sealed class ActivateProtocolCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<ActivateProtocolCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IProtocolRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public ActivateProtocolCommandHandler(
        IProtocolRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        ActivateProtocolCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var protocol = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.ProtocolId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (protocol is null)
            return false;

        protocol.Activate(_dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(protocol);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Protocols\Handlers\AddProtocolStepCommandHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Protocols.Abstractions;
using LIMS.Application.Protocols.Commands;

namespace LIMS.Application.Protocols.Handlers;

public sealed class AddProtocolStepCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<AddProtocolStepCommand, Guid>
{
    private readonly IProtocolRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public AddProtocolStepCommandHandler(
        IProtocolRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<Guid> HandleAsync(
        AddProtocolStepCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var protocol = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.ProtocolId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (protocol is null)
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                $"Protocol '{command.ProtocolId}' was not found.");

        var stepId = protocol.AddStep(
            command.Sequence,
            command.Title,
            command.Instruction,
            command.Type,
            command.IsRequired,
            command.TargetValue,
            command.TargetUnit,
            command.MinimumValue,
            command.MaximumValue,
            command.Notes,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(protocol);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return stepId;
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Protocols\Handlers\CreateProtocolCommandHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Abstractions;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Interfaces;
using LIMS.Application.Protocols.Abstractions;
using LIMS.Application.Protocols.Commands;
using LIMS.Domain.Protocols;

namespace LIMS.Application.Protocols.Handlers;

public sealed class CreateProtocolCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<CreateProtocolCommand, Guid>
{
    private readonly IProtocolRepository _protocolRepository;
    private readonly ILaboratoryRepository _laboratoryRepository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public CreateProtocolCommandHandler(
        IProtocolRepository protocolRepository,
        ILaboratoryRepository laboratoryRepository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _protocolRepository = protocolRepository;
        _laboratoryRepository = laboratoryRepository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<Guid> HandleAsync(
        CreateProtocolCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var laboratory = await _laboratoryRepository.GetByIdAsync(
            command.LaboratoryId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (laboratory is null)
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                $"Laboratory '{command.LaboratoryId}' was not found.");

        if (await _protocolRepository.ExistsByCodeAsync(
                command.LaboratoryId,
                command.ProtocolCode,
                cancellationToken))
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                $"Protocol code '{command.ProtocolCode}' already exists in this laboratory.");
        }

        var protocol = Protocol.Create(
            command.LaboratoryId,
            command.ProtocolCode,
            command.Name,
            command.Description,
            command.Version,
            command.CreatedByUserId,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        await _protocolRepository.AddAsync(protocol, cancellationToken);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return protocol.Id;
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Protocols\Handlers\DeprecateProtocolCommandHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Protocols.Abstractions;
using LIMS.Application.Protocols.Commands;

namespace LIMS.Application.Protocols.Handlers;

public sealed class DeprecateProtocolCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<DeprecateProtocolCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IProtocolRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public DeprecateProtocolCommandHandler(
        IProtocolRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        DeprecateProtocolCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var protocol = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.ProtocolId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (protocol is null)
            return false;

        protocol.Deprecate(_dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(protocol);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Protocols.Abstractions;
using LIMS.Application.Protocols.DTOs;
using LIMS.Application.Protocols.Queries;

namespace LIMS.Application.Protocols.Handlers;

public sealed class GetProtocolsByLaboratoryIdQueryHandler
    : IQueryHandler<
        GetProtocolsByLaboratoryIdQuery,
        IReadOnlyCollection<ProtocolDto>>
{
    private readonly IProtocolRepository _repository;

    public GetProtocolsByLaboratoryIdQueryHandler(
        IProtocolRepository repository)
    {
        _repository = repository;
    }

    public async Task<IReadOnlyCollection<ProtocolDto>> HandleAsync(
        GetProtocolsByLaboratoryIdQuery query,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var protocols = await _repository.GetByLaboratoryIdAsync(
            query.LaboratoryId,
            cancellationToken);

        return protocols.Select(x => x.ToDto()).ToArray();
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Protocols\Handlers\ProtocolMapping.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Protocols.DTOs;
using LIMS.Domain.Protocols;

namespace LIMS.Application.Protocols.Handlers;

internal static class ProtocolMapping
{
    public static ProtocolDto ToDto(this Protocol protocol)
    {
        return new ProtocolDto(
            protocol.Id,
            protocol.LaboratoryId,
            protocol.ProtocolCode,
            protocol.Name,
            protocol.Description,
            protocol.Version,
            protocol.Status,
            protocol.CreatedByUserId,
            protocol.CreatedAt,
            protocol.UpdatedAt,
            protocol.ActivatedAt,
            protocol.DeprecatedAt,
            protocol.ArchivedAt);
    }

    public static ProtocolDetailsDto ToDetailsDto(this Protocol protocol)
    {
        return new ProtocolDetailsDto(
            protocol.Id,
            protocol.LaboratoryId,
            protocol.ProtocolCode,
            protocol.Name,
            protocol.Description,
            protocol.Version,
            protocol.Status,
            protocol.CreatedByUserId,
            protocol.CreatedAt,
            protocol.UpdatedAt,
            protocol.ActivatedAt,
            protocol.DeprecatedAt,
            protocol.ArchivedAt,
            protocol.Steps
                .OrderBy(x => x.Sequence)
                .Select(x => new ProtocolStepDto(
                    x.Id,
                    x.ProtocolId,
                    x.Sequence,
                    x.Title,
                    x.Instruction,
                    x.Type,
                    x.IsRequired,
                    x.TargetValue,
                    x.TargetUnit,
                    x.MinimumValue,
                    x.MaximumValue,
                    x.Notes))
                .ToArray());
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Protocols\Handlers\RemoveProtocolStepCommandHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Protocols.Abstractions;
using LIMS.Application.Protocols.Commands;

namespace LIMS.Application.Protocols.Handlers;

public sealed class RemoveProtocolStepCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<RemoveProtocolStepCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IProtocolRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public RemoveProtocolStepCommandHandler(
        IProtocolRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        RemoveProtocolStepCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var protocol = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.ProtocolId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (protocol is null)
            return false;

        protocol.RemoveStep(
            command.StepId,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(protocol);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;

using LIMS.Application.Protocols.Abstractions;

public sealed class UpdateProtocolStepCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<UpdateProtocolStepCommand, bool>

    private readonly IProtocolRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public UpdateProtocolStepCommandHandler(
        IProtocolRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        UpdateProtocolStepCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)

        if (protocol is null)
            return false;

            command.StepId,
            command.Sequence,
            command.Title,

            command.Type,
            command.IsRequired,
            command.TargetValue,
            command.TargetUnit,

            command.MaximumValue,
            command.Notes,

FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Protocols\Validators\AddProtocolStepCommandValidator.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Validation;
using LIMS.Application.Protocols.Commands;
using LIMS.Domain.Protocols.Enums;

namespace LIMS.Application.Protocols.Validators;

public sealed class AddProtocolStepCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<AddProtocolStepCommand>
{
    public AddProtocolStepCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.ProtocolId)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty, "ProtocolId is required.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Sequence)
            .Must(x => x > 0, "Sequence must be greater than zero.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Title)
            .NotEmpty("Title is required.")
            .MaximumLength(200, "Title cannot exceed 200 characters.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Instruction)
            .NotEmpty("Instruction is required.")
            .MaximumLength(4000, "Instruction cannot exceed 4000 characters.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Type)
            .Must(x => Enum.IsDefined(x), "Protocol step type is invalid.");

        RuleFor(x => x.TargetUnit)
            .MaximumLength(50, "TargetUnit cannot exceed 50 characters.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Notes)
            .MaximumLength(1000, "Notes cannot exceed 1000 characters.");

        RuleFor(x => x.MinimumValue)
            .Must((command, value) =>
                !value.HasValue ||
                !command.MaximumValue.HasValue ||
                value.Value <= command.MaximumValue.Value,
                "MinimumValue cannot exceed MaximumValue.");

        RuleFor(x => x.TargetValue)
            .Must((command, value) =>
                !value.HasValue ||
                (!command.MinimumValue.HasValue ||
                 value.Value >= command.MinimumValue.Value) &&
                (!command.MaximumValue.HasValue ||
                 value.Value <= command.MaximumValue.Value),
                "TargetValue must be within the configured range.");
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Requests\DependencyInjection.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.CQRS;
using LIMS.Application.Requests.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Requests.Handlers;
using LIMS.Application.Requests.Queries;
using LIMS.Application.Requests.Validators;
using LIMS.Application.Validation;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;

namespace LIMS.Application.Requests;

public static class DependencyInjection
{
    public static IServiceCollection AddRequestsModule(
        this IServiceCollection services)
    {
        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<CreateSampleRequestCommand, Guid>,
            CreateSampleRequestCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<UpdateSampleRequestCommand, bool>,
            UpdateSampleRequestCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<SubmitSampleRequestCommand, bool>,
            SubmitSampleRequestCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<StartSampleRequestReviewCommand, bool>,
            StartSampleRequestReviewCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<ApproveSampleRequestCommand, bool>,
            ApproveSampleRequestCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<RejectSampleRequestCommand, bool>,
            RejectSampleRequestCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<CancelSampleRequestCommand, bool>,
            CancelSampleRequestCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<FulfillSampleRequestCommand, bool>,
            FulfillSampleRequestCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IQueryHandler<GetSampleRequestByIdQuery, DTOs.SampleRequestDto?>,
            GetSampleRequestByIdQueryHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IQueryHandler<GetSampleRequestByCodeQuery, DTOs.SampleRequestDto?>,
            GetSampleRequestByCodeQueryHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IQueryHandler<
                GetSampleRequestsByRequesterQuery,
                IReadOnlyCollection<DTOs.SampleRequestDto>>,
            GetSampleRequestsByRequesterQueryHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IQueryHandler<
                GetSampleRequestsByLaboratoryQuery,
                IReadOnlyCollection<DTOs.SampleRequestDto>>,
            GetSampleRequestsByLaboratoryQueryHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IQueryHandler<
                GetSampleRequestsByStatusQuery,
                IReadOnlyCollection<DTOs.SampleRequestDto>>,
            GetSampleRequestsByStatusQueryHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IQueryHandler<
                GetPendingSampleRequestsQuery,
                IReadOnlyCollection<DTOs.SampleRequestDto>>,
            GetPendingSampleRequestsQueryHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<CreateSampleRequestCommand>,
            CreateSampleRequestCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<UpdateSampleRequestCommand>,
            UpdateSampleRequestCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<SubmitSampleRequestCommand>,
            SubmitSampleRequestCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<StartSampleRequestReviewCommand>,
            StartSampleRequestReviewCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<ApproveSampleRequestCommand>,
            ApproveSampleRequestCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<RejectSampleRequestCommand>,
            RejectSampleRequestCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<CancelSampleRequestCommand>,
            CancelSampleRequestCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<FulfillSampleRequestCommand>,
            FulfillSampleRequestCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<GetSampleRequestByIdQuery>,
            GetSampleRequestByIdQueryValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<GetSampleRequestByCodeQuery>,
            GetSampleRequestByCodeQueryValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<GetSampleRequestsByRequesterQuery>,
            GetSampleRequestsByRequesterQueryValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<GetSampleRequestsByLaboratoryQuery>,
            GetSampleRequestsByLaboratoryQueryValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<GetSampleRequestsByStatusQuery>,
            GetSampleRequestsByStatusQueryValidator>();

        return services;
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Requests\DTOs\SampleRequestDto.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Requests.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Samples.Enums;

namespace LIMS.Application.Requests.DTOs;

public sealed record SampleRequestDto(
    Guid Id,
    string RequestCode,
    string Title,
    string? Description,
    string SampleName,
    string? SampleDescription,
    SampleType RequestedSampleType,
    string? SampleSource,
    string RequestedByUserId,
    string? AssignedReviewerUserId,
    Guid? LaboratoryId,
    Guid? CreatedSampleId,
    SampleRequestPriority Priority,
    SampleRequestStatus Status,
    string? ReviewComments,
    string? RejectionReason,
    string? CancellationReason,
    DateTime CreatedAt,
    DateTime UpdatedAt,
    DateTime? SubmittedAt,
    DateTime? ReviewStartedAt,
    DateTime? ApprovedAt,
    DateTime? RejectedAt,
    DateTime? CancelledAt,
    DateTime? FulfilledAt);


====================================================================================================

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Requests.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Requests.Repositories;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Text;

namespace LIMS.Application.Requests.Handlers;

internal class ApproveSampleRequestCommandHandler
{
}
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.CQRS;
using LIMS.Application.Requests.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Requests.Repositories;

namespace LIMS.Application.Requests.Handlers;

public sealed class ApproveSampleRequestCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<ApproveSampleRequestCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly ISampleRequestRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public ApproveSampleRequestCommandHandler(
        ISampleRequestRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        ApproveSampleRequestCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var request = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.RequestId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (request is null)
            return false;

        request.Approve(
            command.ApprovedByUserId,
            command.LaboratoryId,
            command.Comments,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(request);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Requests\Handlers\CreateSampleRequestCommandHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.CQRS;
using LIMS.Application.Requests.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Requests.Repositories;
using LIMS.Domain.Requests;

namespace LIMS.Application.Requests.Handlers;

public sealed class CreateSampleRequestCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<CreateSampleRequestCommand, Guid>
{
    private readonly ISampleRequestRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public CreateSampleRequestCommandHandler(
        ISampleRequestRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<Guid> HandleAsync(
        CreateSampleRequestCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var exists = await _repository.ExistsByCodeAsync(
            command.RequestCode,
            cancellationToken);

        if (exists)
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                $"A sample request with code '{command.RequestCode}' already exists.");

        var request = SampleRequest.Create(
            command.RequestCode,
            command.Title,
            command.Description,
            command.SampleName,
            command.SampleDescription,
            command.RequestedSampleType,
            command.SampleSource,
            command.RequestedByUserId,
            command.Priority,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        await _repository.AddAsync(request, cancellationToken);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return request.Id;
    }
}

        GetSampleRequestByIdQuery query,

        GetSampleRequestsByRequesterQuery,

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.CQRS;
using LIMS.Application.Requests.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Requests.Repositories;

namespace LIMS.Application.Requests.Handlers;

public sealed class UpdateSampleRequestCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<UpdateSampleRequestCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly ISampleRequestRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public UpdateSampleRequestCommandHandler(
        ISampleRequestRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        UpdateSampleRequestCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var request = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.RequestId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (request is null)
            return false;

        request.UpdateInformation(
            command.Title,
            command.Description,
            command.SampleName,
            command.SampleDescription,
            command.RequestedSampleType,
            command.SampleSource,
            command.Priority,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(request);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Requests\Mapping\SampleRequestMapping.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Requests.DTOs;
using LIMS.Domain.Requests;

namespace LIMS.Application.Requests.Mapping;

public static class SampleRequestMapping
{
    public static SampleRequestDto ToDto(this SampleRequest request)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(request);

        return new SampleRequestDto(
            request.Id,
            request.RequestCode,
            request.Title,
            request.Description,
            request.SampleName,
            request.SampleDescription,
            request.RequestedSampleType,
            request.SampleSource,
            request.RequestedByUserId,
            request.AssignedReviewerUserId,
            request.LaboratoryId,
            request.CreatedSampleId,
            request.Priority,
            request.Status,
            request.ReviewComments,
            request.RejectionReason,
            request.CancellationReason,
            request.CreatedAt,
            request.UpdatedAt,
            request.SubmittedAt,
            request.ReviewStartedAt,
            request.ApprovedAt,
            request.RejectedAt,
            request.CancelledAt,
            request.FulfilledAt);
    }
}

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Domain.Requests;
using LIMS.Domain.Requests.Enums;

namespace LIMS.Application.Requests.Repositories;

public interface ISampleRequestRepository
    : IRepository<SampleRequest, Guid>
{
    Task<bool> ExistsByCodeAsync(
        string requestCode,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<SampleRequest?> GetByCodeAsync(
        string requestCode,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<SampleRequest?> GetWithDetailsAsync(
        Guid requestId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<IReadOnlyCollection<SampleRequest>> GetByRequesterAsync(
        string requestedByUserId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<IReadOnlyCollection<SampleRequest>> GetByLaboratoryIdAsync(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<IReadOnlyCollection<SampleRequest>> GetByStatusAsync(
        SampleRequestStatus status,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<IReadOnlyCollection<SampleRequest>> GetPendingReviewAsync(
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);
}

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Validation;
using LIMS.Application.Requests.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Validation;

namespace LIMS.Application.Requests.Validators;

public sealed class ApproveSampleRequestCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<ApproveSampleRequestCommand>
{
    public ApproveSampleRequestCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.RequestId)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty);

        RuleFor(x => x.ApprovedByUserId)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(150);

        RuleFor(x => x.LaboratoryId)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty);

        RuleFor(x => x.Comments)
            .MaximumLength(2000);
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Requests\Validators\CreateSampleRequestCommandValidator.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Validation;
using LIMS.Application.Requests.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Validation;

namespace LIMS.Application.Requests.Validators;

public sealed class CreateSampleRequestCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<CreateSampleRequestCommand>
{
    public CreateSampleRequestCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.RequestCode)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(50)
            .Matches("^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$");

        RuleFor(x => x.Title)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(200);

        RuleFor(x => x.Description)
            .MaximumLength(4000);

        RuleFor(x => x.SampleName)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(200);

        RuleFor(x => x.SampleDescription)
            .MaximumLength(2000);

        RuleFor(x => x.SampleSource)
            .MaximumLength(250);

        RuleFor(x => x.RequestedByUserId)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(150);

        RuleFor(x => x.RequestedSampleType)
            .Must(x => Enum.IsDefined(x));

        RuleFor(x => x.Priority)
            .Must(x => Enum.IsDefined(x));
    }
}

public sealed class GetSampleRequestsByLaboratoryQueryValidator
    : AbstractValidator<GetSampleRequestsByLaboratoryQuery>
{
    public GetSampleRequestsByLaboratoryQueryValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.LaboratoryId)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty);
    }
}

public sealed class GetSampleRequestsByStatusQueryValidator
    : AbstractValidator<GetSampleRequestsByStatusQuery>
{
    public GetSampleRequestsByStatusQueryValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.Status)
            .Must(x => Enum.IsDefined(x));
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Requests\Validators\UpdateSampleRequestCommandValidator.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Validation;
using LIMS.Application.Requests.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Validation;

namespace LIMS.Application.Requests.Validators;

public sealed class UpdateSampleRequestCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<UpdateSampleRequestCommand>
{
    public UpdateSampleRequestCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.RequestId)
            .Must(x => x != Guid.Empty);

        RuleFor(x => x.Title)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(200);

        RuleFor(x => x.Description)
            .MaximumLength(4000);

        RuleFor(x => x.SampleName)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(200);

        RuleFor(x => x.SampleDescription)
            .MaximumLength(2000);

        RuleFor(x => x.SampleSource)
            .MaximumLength(250);

        RuleFor(x => x.RequestedSampleType)
            .Must(x => Enum.IsDefined(x));

        RuleFor(x => x.Priority)
            .Must(x => Enum.IsDefined(x));
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Results\DependencyInjection.cs

using LIMS.Application.Requests.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Requests.Handlers;
using LIMS.Application.Requests.Queries;
using LIMS.Application.Requests.Validators;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;

namespace LIMS.Application.Requests;

public static class DependencyInjection
{
    public static IServiceCollection AddRequestsModule(
        this IServiceCollection services)
    {
        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<CreateSampleRequestCommand, Guid>,
            CreateSampleRequestCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<UpdateSampleRequestCommand, bool>,
            UpdateSampleRequestCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<SubmitSampleRequestCommand, bool>,
            SubmitSampleRequestCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<StartSampleRequestReviewCommand, bool>,
            StartSampleRequestReviewCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<ApproveSampleRequestCommand, bool>,
            ApproveSampleRequestCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<RejectSampleRequestCommand, bool>,
            RejectSampleRequestCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<CancelSampleRequestCommand, bool>,
            CancelSampleRequestCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            ICommandHandler<FulfillSampleRequestCommand, bool>,
            FulfillSampleRequestCommandHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IQueryHandler<GetSampleRequestByIdQuery, DTOs.SampleRequestDto?>,
            GetSampleRequestByIdQueryHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IQueryHandler<GetSampleRequestByCodeQuery, DTOs.SampleRequestDto?>,
            GetSampleRequestByCodeQueryHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IQueryHandler<
                GetSampleRequestsByRequesterQuery,
                IReadOnlyCollection<DTOs.SampleRequestDto>>,
            GetSampleRequestsByRequesterQueryHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IQueryHandler<
                GetSampleRequestsByLaboratoryQuery,
                IReadOnlyCollection<DTOs.SampleRequestDto>>,
            GetSampleRequestsByLaboratoryQueryHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IQueryHandler<
                GetSampleRequestsByStatusQuery,
                IReadOnlyCollection<DTOs.SampleRequestDto>>,
            GetSampleRequestsByStatusQueryHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IQueryHandler<
                GetPendingSampleRequestsQuery,
                IReadOnlyCollection<DTOs.SampleRequestDto>>,
            GetPendingSampleRequestsQueryHandler>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<CreateSampleRequestCommand>,
            CreateSampleRequestCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<UpdateSampleRequestCommand>,
            UpdateSampleRequestCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<SubmitSampleRequestCommand>,
            SubmitSampleRequestCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<StartSampleRequestReviewCommand>,
            StartSampleRequestReviewCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<ApproveSampleRequestCommand>,
            ApproveSampleRequestCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<RejectSampleRequestCommand>,
            RejectSampleRequestCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<CancelSampleRequestCommand>,
            CancelSampleRequestCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<FulfillSampleRequestCommand>,
            FulfillSampleRequestCommandValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<GetSampleRequestByIdQuery>,
            GetSampleRequestByIdQueryValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<GetSampleRequestByCodeQuery>,
            GetSampleRequestByCodeQueryValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<GetSampleRequestsByRequesterQuery>,
            GetSampleRequestsByRequesterQueryValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<GetSampleRequestsByLaboratoryQuery>,
            GetSampleRequestsByLaboratoryQueryValidator>();

        services.AddScoped<
            IValidator<GetSampleRequestsByStatusQuery>,
            GetSampleRequestsByStatusQueryValidator>();

        return services;
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Results\Handlers\AddResultEntryCommandHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;

namespace LIMS.Application.Results.Handlers;

public sealed class AddResultEntryCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<AddResultEntryCommand, Guid>
{
    private readonly IAnalysisResultRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public AddResultEntryCommandHandler(
        IAnalysisResultRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<Guid> HandleAsync(
        AddResultEntryCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var result = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.ResultId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (result is null)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Analysis result was not found.");

        result.AddEntry(
            command.ParameterName,
            command.ValueType,
            command.Value,
            command.NumericValue,
            command.Unit,
            command.Uncertainty,
            command.Method,
            command.Notes,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        var entryId = result.Entries.Last().Id;

        _repository.Update(result);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return entryId;
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Results\Handlers\ApproveAnalysisResultCommandHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Repositories;

namespace LIMS.Application.Results.Handlers;

public sealed class ApproveAnalysisResultCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<ApproveAnalysisResultCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IAnalysisResultRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public ApproveAnalysisResultCommandHandler(
        IAnalysisResultRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        ApproveAnalysisResultCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var result = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.ResultId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (result is null)
            return false;

        result.Approve(
            command.ReviewerUserId,
            command.Comments,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(result);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Results\Handlers\ArchiveAnalysisResultCommandHandler.cs

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Repositories;

namespace LIMS.Application.Results.Handlers;

public sealed class ArchiveAnalysisResultCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<ArchiveAnalysisResultCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IAnalysisResultRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public ArchiveAnalysisResultCommandHandler(
        IAnalysisResultRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        ArchiveAnalysisResultCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)

        if (result is null)
            return false;

        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Results\Handlers\CreateAnalysisResultCommandHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Experiments;
using LIMS.Application.Experiments.Abstractions;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories;
using LIMS.Application.Laboratories.Interfaces;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Repositories;
using LIMS.Application.Samples;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.Interfaces;
using LIMS.Domain.Results;

namespace LIMS.Application.Results.Handlers;

public sealed class CreateAnalysisResultCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<CreateAnalysisResultCommand, Guid>
{
    private readonly IAnalysisResultRepository _resultRepository;
    private readonly ILaboratoryRepository _laboratoryRepository;
    private readonly IExperimentRepository _experimentRepository;
    private readonly ISampleRepository _sampleRepository;
    private readonly IInstrumentRepository _instrumentRepository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public CreateAnalysisResultCommandHandler(
        IAnalysisResultRepository resultRepository,
        ILaboratoryRepository laboratoryRepository,
        IExperimentRepository experimentRepository,
        ISampleRepository sampleRepository,
        IInstrumentRepository instrumentRepository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _resultRepository = resultRepository;
        _laboratoryRepository = laboratoryRepository;
        _experimentRepository = experimentRepository;
        _sampleRepository = sampleRepository;
        _instrumentRepository = instrumentRepository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<Guid> HandleAsync(
        CreateAnalysisResultCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var laboratory = await _laboratoryRepository.GetByIdAsync(
            command.LaboratoryId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (laboratory is null)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Laboratory was not found.");

        var experiment = await _experimentRepository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.ExperimentId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (experiment is null)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Experiment was not found.");

        if (experiment.LaboratoryId != command.LaboratoryId)
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The experiment does not belong to the specified laboratory.");

        if (experiment.SampleId != command.SampleId)
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The experiment is not associated with the specified sample.");

        var sample = await _sampleRepository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.SampleId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (sample is null)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Sample was not found.");

        if (sample.LaboratoryId != command.LaboratoryId)
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The sample does not belong to the specified laboratory.");

        if (command.InstrumentId.HasValue)
        {
            var instrument = await _instrumentRepository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
                command.InstrumentId.Value,
                cancellationToken);

            if (instrument is null)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Instrument was not found.");

            if (instrument.LaboratoryId != command.LaboratoryId)
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "The instrument does not belong to the specified laboratory.");
        }

        var codeExists = await _resultRepository.ExistsByCodeAsync(
            command.LaboratoryId,
            command.ResultCode,
            cancellationToken);

        if (codeExists)
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "An analysis result with the specified code already exists.");

        var result = AnalysisResult.Create(
            command.LaboratoryId,
            command.ExperimentId,
            command.SampleId,
            command.InstrumentId,
            command.ResultCode,
            command.Title,
            command.Description,
            command.CreatedByUserId,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        await _resultRepository.AddAsync(result, cancellationToken);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return result.Id;
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Results\Handlers\Queries\GetAnalysisResultByCodeQueryHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Results.DTOs;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Mapping;

namespace LIMS.Application.Results.Handlers.Queries;

public sealed class GetAnalysisResultByCodeQueryHandler
    : IQueryHandler<GetAnalysisResultByCodeQuery, AnalysisResultDto?>
{
    private readonly IAnalysisResultRepository _repository;

    public GetAnalysisResultByCodeQueryHandler(
        IAnalysisResultRepository repository)
    {
        _repository = repository;
    }

    public async Task<AnalysisResultDto?> HandleAsync(
        GetAnalysisResultByCodeQuery query,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var result = await _repository.GetByCodeAsync(
            query.LaboratoryId,
            query.ResultCode,
            cancellationToken);

        return result is null
            ? null
            : AnalysisResultMapping.ToDto(result);
    }
}

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Results.DTOs;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Mapping;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Queries;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Repositories;

public sealed class GetAnalysisResultsByExperimentIdQueryHandler
    : IQueryHandler<
        GetAnalysisResultsByExperimentIdQuery,
        IReadOnlyCollection<AnalysisResultDto>>
{
    private readonly IAnalysisResultRepository _repository;

    public GetAnalysisResultsByExperimentIdQueryHandler(
        IAnalysisResultRepository repository)
    {
        _repository = repository;
    }

    public async Task<IReadOnlyCollection<AnalysisResultDto>> HandleAsync(

            query.ExperimentId,
            cancellationToken);

        return results
            .Select(AnalysisResultMapping.ToDto)
            .ToArray();
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Results\Handlers\Queries\GetAnalysisResultsByStatusQueryHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Results.DTOs;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Mapping;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Queries;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Repositories;

namespace LIMS.Application.Results.Handlers.Queries;

public sealed class GetAnalysisResultsByStatusQueryHandler
    : IQueryHandler<
        GetAnalysisResultsByStatusQuery,
        IReadOnlyCollection<AnalysisResultDto>>
{
    private readonly IAnalysisResultRepository _repository;

    public GetAnalysisResultsByStatusQueryHandler(
        IAnalysisResultRepository repository)
    {
        _repository = repository;
    }

    public async Task<IReadOnlyCollection<AnalysisResultDto>> HandleAsync(
        GetAnalysisResultsByStatusQuery query,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var results = await _repository.GetByStatusAsync(
            query.LaboratoryId,
            query.Status,
            cancellationToken);

        return results
            .Select(AnalysisResultMapping.ToDto)
            .ToArray();
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Results\Handlers\RejectAnalysisResultCommandHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Repositories;

namespace LIMS.Application.Results.Handlers;

public sealed class RejectAnalysisResultCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<RejectAnalysisResultCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IAnalysisResultRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public RejectAnalysisResultCommandHandler(
        IAnalysisResultRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        RejectAnalysisResultCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var result = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.ResultId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (result is null)
            return false;

        result.Reject(
            command.ReviewerUserId,
            command.Reason,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(result);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}
