using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Repositories;

namespace LIMS.Application.Results.Handlers;

public sealed class RemoveResultEntryCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<RemoveResultEntryCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IAnalysisResultRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public RemoveResultEntryCommandHandler(
        IAnalysisResultRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        RemoveResultEntryCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var result = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.ResultId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (result is null)
            return false;

        result.RemoveEntry(
            command.EntryId,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(result);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Results\Handlers\UpdateAnalysisResultInformationCommandHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Instruments;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Repositories;

namespace LIMS.Application.Results.Handlers;

public sealed class UpdateAnalysisResultInformationCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<UpdateAnalysisResultInformationCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IAnalysisResultRepository _repository;
    private readonly IInstrumentRepository _instrumentRepository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public UpdateAnalysisResultInformationCommandHandler(
        IAnalysisResultRepository repository,
        IInstrumentRepository instrumentRepository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _instrumentRepository = instrumentRepository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        UpdateAnalysisResultInformationCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var result = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.ResultId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (result is null)
            return false;

        if (command.InstrumentId.HasValue)
        {
            var instrument = await _instrumentRepository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
                command.InstrumentId.Value,
                cancellationToken);

            if (instrument is null)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Instrument was not found.");

            if (instrument.LaboratoryId != result.LaboratoryId)
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "The instrument does not belong to the result laboratory.");
        }

        result.UpdateInformation(
            command.Title,
            command.Description,
            command.InstrumentId,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(result);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Results\Handlers\UpdateResultEntryCommandHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Repositories;

namespace LIMS.Application.Results.Handlers;

public sealed class UpdateResultEntryCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<UpdateResultEntryCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly IAnalysisResultRepository _repository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public UpdateResultEntryCommandHandler(
        IAnalysisResultRepository repository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        UpdateResultEntryCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var result = await _repository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            command.ResultId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (result is null)
            return false;

        result.UpdateEntry(
            command.EntryId,
            command.ParameterName,
            command.ValueType,
            command.Value,
            command.NumericValue,
            command.Unit,
            command.Uncertainty,
            command.Method,
            command.Notes,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        _repository.Update(result);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Results\Mapping\AnalysisResultMapping.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Results.DTOs;
using LIMS.Domain.Results;

namespace LIMS.Application.Results.Mapping;

public static class AnalysisResultMapping
{
    public static AnalysisResultDto ToDto(AnalysisResult result)
    {
        return new AnalysisResultDto(
            result.Id,
            result.LaboratoryId,
            result.ExperimentId,
            result.SampleId,
            result.InstrumentId,
            result.ResultCode,
            result.Title,
            result.Description,
            result.Status,
            result.CreatedByUserId,
            result.SubmittedByUserId,
            result.ReviewedByUserId,
            result.ReviewComments,
            result.RejectionReason,
            result.ArchivedByUserId,
            result.CreatedAt,
            result.UpdatedAt,
            result.SubmittedAt,
            result.ReviewedAt,
            result.ArchivedAt);
    }

    public static AnalysisResultDetailsDto ToDetailsDto(
        AnalysisResult result)
    {
        return new AnalysisResultDetailsDto(
            result.Id,
            result.LaboratoryId,
            result.ExperimentId,
            result.SampleId,
            result.InstrumentId,
            result.ResultCode,
            result.Title,
            result.Description,
            result.Status,
            result.CreatedByUserId,
            result.SubmittedByUserId,
            result.ReviewedByUserId,
            result.ReviewComments,
            result.RejectionReason,
            result.ArchivedByUserId,
            result.CreatedAt,
            result.UpdatedAt,
            result.SubmittedAt,
            result.ReviewedAt,
            result.ArchivedAt,
            result.Entries.Select(entry => new ResultEntryDto(
                entry.Id,
                entry.ResultId,
                entry.ParameterName,
                entry.ValueType,
                entry.Value,
                entry.NumericValue,
                entry.Unit,
                entry.Uncertainty,
                entry.Method,
                entry.Notes)).ToArray());
    }
}

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Domain.Results;
using LIMS.Domain.Results.Enums;

namespace LIMS.Application.Results.Repositories;

public interface IAnalysisResultRepository : IRepository<AnalysisResult, Guid>
{
    Task<bool> ExistsByCodeAsync(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string resultCode,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<AnalysisResult?> GetWithDetailsAsync(
        Guid resultId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<AnalysisResult?> GetByCodeAsync(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string resultCode,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<IReadOnlyCollection<AnalysisResult>> GetByLaboratoryIdAsync(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<IReadOnlyCollection<AnalysisResult>> GetByExperimentIdAsync(
        Guid experimentId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<IReadOnlyCollection<AnalysisResult>> GetBySampleIdAsync(
        Guid sampleId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<IReadOnlyCollection<AnalysisResult>> GetByStatusAsync(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        ResultStatus status,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Results\Validators\AddResultEntryCommandValidator.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Validation;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Validation;
using LIMS.Domain.Results.Enums;

namespace LIMS.Application.Results.Validators;

public sealed class AddResultEntryCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<AddResultEntryCommand>
{
    public AddResultEntryCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.ResultId)
            .NotEmpty();

        RuleFor(x => x.ParameterName)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(150);

        RuleFor(x => x.ValueType)
            .Must(Enum.IsDefined)
            .WithMessage("Invalid result value type.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Value)
            .MaximumLength(4000);

        RuleFor(x => x.Unit)
            .MaximumLength(50);

        RuleFor(x => x.Uncertainty)
            .Must(x => !x.HasValue || x.Value >= 0)
            .WithMessage("Uncertainty cannot be negative.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Method)
            .MaximumLength(200);

        RuleFor(x => x.Notes)
            .MaximumLength(2000);

        RuleFor(x => x)
            .Must(x => IsValueConsistent(
                x.ValueType,
                x.Value,
                x.NumericValue))
            .WithMessage("The supplied value does not match the selected value type.");
    }

    private static bool IsValueConsistent(
        ResultValueType valueType,
        string? value,
        decimal? numericValue)
    {
        return valueType switch
        {
            ResultValueType.Numeric => numericValue.HasValue,
            ResultValueType.Text => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value),
            ResultValueType.Boolean =>
                string.Equals(value, "true", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                string.Equals(value, "false", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase),
            ResultValueType.Date =>
                DateOnly.TryParse(value, out _),
            _ => false
        };
    }
}

using LIMS.Application.Validation;

namespace LIMS.Application.Results.Validators;

public sealed class CreateAnalysisResultCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<CreateAnalysisResultCommand>
{
    public CreateAnalysisResultCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.LaboratoryId)
            .NotEmpty();

        RuleFor(x => x.ExperimentId)
            .NotEmpty();

        RuleFor(x => x.SampleId)
            .NotEmpty();

        RuleFor(x => x.InstrumentId)
            .Must(x => !x.HasValue || x.Value != Guid.Empty)
            .WithMessage("InstrumentId must be a valid identifier when provided.");

        RuleFor(x => x.ResultCode)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(50)
            .Matches("^[A-Z0-9_-]+$")
            .WithMessage("Result code must contain only uppercase letters, numbers, hyphens or underscores.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Title)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(200);

        RuleFor(x => x.Description)
            .MaximumLength(4000);

        RuleFor(x => x.CreatedByUserId)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(150);
    }
}

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Validation;
using LIMS.Application.Results.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Validation;
using LIMS.Domain.Results.Enums;

namespace LIMS.Application.Results.Validators;

public sealed class UpdateResultEntryCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<UpdateResultEntryCommand>
{
    public UpdateResultEntryCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.ResultId)
            .NotEmpty();

        RuleFor(x => x.EntryId)
            .NotEmpty();

        RuleFor(x => x.ParameterName)
            .NotEmpty()
            .MaximumLength(150);

        RuleFor(x => x.ValueType)
            .Must(Enum.IsDefined)
            .WithMessage("Invalid result value type.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Value)
            .MaximumLength(4000);

        RuleFor(x => x.Unit)
            .MaximumLength(50);

        RuleFor(x => x.Uncertainty)
            .Must(x => !x.HasValue || x.Value >= 0)
            .WithMessage("Uncertainty cannot be negative.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Method)
            .MaximumLength(200);

        RuleFor(x => x.Notes)
            .MaximumLength(2000);

        RuleFor(x => x)
            .Must(x => IsValueConsistent(
                x.ValueType,
                x.Value,
                x.NumericValue))
            .WithMessage("The supplied value does not match the selected value type.");
    }

    private static bool IsValueConsistent(
        ResultValueType valueType,
        string? value,
        decimal? numericValue)
    {
        return valueType switch
        {
            ResultValueType.Numeric => numericValue.HasValue,
            ResultValueType.Text => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value),
            ResultValueType.Boolean =>
                string.Equals(value, "true", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                string.Equals(value, "false", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase),
            ResultValueType.Date =>
                DateOnly.TryParse(value, out _),
            _ => false
        };
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Samples\Handlers\ArchiveSampleCommandHandler.cs

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.Interfaces;

namespace LIMS.Application.Samples.Handlers;

public sealed class ArchiveSampleCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<ArchiveSampleCommand, bool>
{
    private readonly ISampleRepository _sampleRepository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public ArchiveSampleCommandHandler(
        ISampleRepository sampleRepository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _sampleRepository = sampleRepository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<bool> HandleAsync(
        ArchiveSampleCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var sample = await _sampleRepository.GetByIdAsync(
            command.SampleId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (sample is null)
            return false;

        sample.Archive(_dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return true;
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Samples\Handlers\GetSampleByCodeQueryHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.DTOs;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.Interfaces;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.Queries;

namespace LIMS.Application.Samples.Handlers;

public sealed class GetSampleByCodeQueryHandler
    : IQueryHandler<GetSampleByCodeQuery, SampleDto?>
{
    private readonly ISampleRepository _sampleRepository;

    public GetSampleByCodeQueryHandler(ISampleRepository sampleRepository)
    {
        _sampleRepository = sampleRepository;
    }

    public async Task<SampleDto?> HandleAsync(
        GetSampleByCodeQuery query,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var sample = await _sampleRepository.GetByCodeAsync(
            query.LaboratoryId,
            query.SampleCode,
            cancellationToken);

        if (sample is null)
            return null;

        return new SampleDto(
            sample.Id,
            sample.LaboratoryId,
            sample.SampleCode,
            sample.Name,
            sample.Description,
            sample.Type,
            sample.Status,
            sample.Priority,
            sample.Source,
            sample.AssignedToUserId,
            sample.RegisteredByUserId,
            sample.RegisteredAt,
            sample.ReceivedAt,
            sample.UpdatedAt,
            sample.ArchivedAt);
    }
}

====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.DTOs;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.Interfaces;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.Queries;

namespace LIMS.Application.Samples.Handlers;

public sealed class GetSampleByIdQueryHandler
    : IQueryHandler<GetSampleByIdQuery, SampleDetailsDto?>
{
    private readonly ISampleRepository _sampleRepository;

    public GetSampleByIdQueryHandler(ISampleRepository sampleRepository)
    {
        _sampleRepository = sampleRepository;
    }

    public async Task<SampleDetailsDto?> HandleAsync(
        GetSampleByIdQuery query,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var sample = await _sampleRepository.GetWithDetailsAsync(
            query.SampleId,
            cancellationToken);

        if (sample is null)
            return null;

        return new SampleDetailsDto(
            sample.Id,
            sample.LaboratoryId,
            sample.SampleCode,
            sample.Name,
            sample.Description,
            sample.Type,
            sample.Status,
            sample.Priority,
            sample.Source,
            sample.AssignedToUserId,
            sample.RegisteredByUserId,
            sample.RegisteredAt,
            sample.ReceivedAt,
            sample.UpdatedAt,
            sample.ArchivedAt);
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Samples\Handlers\GetSamplesByAssignedUserIdQueryHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.DTOs;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.Interfaces;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.Queries;

namespace LIMS.Application.Samples.Handlers;

public sealed class GetSamplesByAssignedUserIdQueryHandler
    : IQueryHandler<GetSamplesByAssignedUserIdQuery, IReadOnlyCollection<SampleDto>>
{
    private readonly ISampleRepository _sampleRepository;

    public GetSamplesByAssignedUserIdQueryHandler(ISampleRepository sampleRepository)
    {
        _sampleRepository = sampleRepository;
    }

    public async Task<IReadOnlyCollection<SampleDto>> HandleAsync(
        GetSamplesByAssignedUserIdQuery query,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var samples = await _sampleRepository.GetByAssignedUserIdAsync(
            query.UserId,
            cancellationToken);

        return samples.Select(sample => new SampleDto(
            sample.Id,
            sample.LaboratoryId,
            sample.SampleCode,
            sample.Name,
            sample.Description,
            sample.Type,
            sample.Status,
            sample.Priority,
            sample.Source,
            sample.AssignedToUserId,
            sample.RegisteredByUserId,
            sample.RegisteredAt,
            sample.ReceivedAt,
            sample.UpdatedAt,
            sample.ArchivedAt)).ToArray();
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Samples\Handlers\RegisterSampleCommandHandler.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.Commands;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.Interfaces;
using LIMS.Domain.Samples;
using LIMS.Domain.Samples.Exceptions;

namespace LIMS.Application.Samples.Handlers;

public sealed class RegisterSampleCommandHandler
    : ICommandHandler<RegisterSampleCommand, Guid>
{
    private readonly ISampleRepository _sampleRepository;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly IDateTimeProvider _dateTimeProvider;

    public RegisterSampleCommandHandler(
        ISampleRepository sampleRepository,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
        IDateTimeProvider dateTimeProvider)
    {
        _sampleRepository = sampleRepository;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        _dateTimeProvider = dateTimeProvider;
    }

    public async Task<Guid> HandleAsync(
        RegisterSampleCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var exists = await _sampleRepository.ExistsByCodeAsync(
            command.LaboratoryId,
            command.SampleCode,
            cancellationToken);

        if (exists)
        {
            throw new SampleDomainException(
                $"Sample code '{command.SampleCode}' already exists in this laboratory.");
        }

        var sample = Sample.Register(
            command.LaboratoryId,
            command.SampleCode,
            command.Name,
            command.Description,
            command.Type,
            command.Priority,
            command.Source,
            command.RegisteredByUserId,
            _dateTimeProvider.UtcNow);

        await _sampleRepository.AddAsync(sample, cancellationToken);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        return sample.Id;
    }
}

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Messaging;

FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Samples\Interfaces\ISampleRepository.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Domain.Samples;

namespace LIMS.Application.Samples.Interfaces;

public interface ISampleRepository : IRepository<Sample, Guid>
{
    Task<bool> ExistsByCodeAsync(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string sampleCode,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<Sample?> GetByCodeAsync(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string sampleCode,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<IReadOnlyCollection<Sample>> GetByLaboratoryIdAsync(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<IReadOnlyCollection<Sample>> GetByAssignedUserIdAsync(
        string userId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<Sample?> GetWithDetailsAsync(
        Guid sampleId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Samples\Validators\AssignSampleCommandValidator.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Validation;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.Commands;

namespace LIMS.Application.Samples.Validators;

public sealed class AssignSampleCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<AssignSampleCommand>
{
    public AssignSampleCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(
            command => command.SampleId,
            (command, failures) =>
            {
                if (command.SampleId == Guid.Empty)
                    failures.Add(new ValidationFailure(
                        nameof(command.SampleId),
                        "Sample identifier is required."));
            });

        RuleFor(
            command => command.UserId,
            (command, failures) =>
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(command.UserId))
                    failures.Add(new ValidationFailure(
                        nameof(command.UserId),
                        "User identifier is required."));
            });
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Samples\Validators\RegisterSampleCommandValidator.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Validation;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.Commands;

namespace LIMS.Application.Samples.Validators;

public sealed class RegisterSampleCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<RegisterSampleCommand>
{
    public RegisterSampleCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(
            command => command.LaboratoryId,
            (command, failures) =>
            {
                if (command.LaboratoryId == Guid.Empty)
                    failures.Add(new ValidationFailure(
                        nameof(command.LaboratoryId),
                        "Laboratory identifier is required."));
            });

        RuleFor(
            command => command.SampleCode,
            (command, failures) =>
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(command.SampleCode))
                    failures.Add(new ValidationFailure(
                        nameof(command.SampleCode),
                        "Sample code is required."));
                else if (command.SampleCode.Length > 50)
                    failures.Add(new ValidationFailure(
                        nameof(command.SampleCode),
                        "Sample code cannot exceed 50 characters."));
            });

        RuleFor(
            command => command.Name,
            (command, failures) =>
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(command.Name))
                    failures.Add(new ValidationFailure(
                        nameof(command.Name),
                        "Sample name is required."));
                else if (command.Name.Length > 200)
                    failures.Add(new ValidationFailure(
                        nameof(command.Name),
                        "Sample name cannot exceed 200 characters."));
            });

        RuleFor(
            command => command.Description,
            (command, failures) =>
            {
                if (command.Description?.Length > 2000)
                    failures.Add(new ValidationFailure(
                        nameof(command.Description),
                        "Description cannot exceed 2000 characters."));
            });

        RuleFor(
            command => command.Source,
            (command, failures) =>
            {
                if (command.Source?.Length > 250)
                    failures.Add(new ValidationFailure(
                        nameof(command.Source),
                        "Source cannot exceed 250 characters."));
            });

        RuleFor(
            command => command.RegisteredByUserId,
            (command, failures) =>
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(command.RegisteredByUserId))
                    failures.Add(new ValidationFailure(
                        nameof(command.RegisteredByUserId),
                        "Registering user identifier is required."));
            });

        RuleFor(
            command => command.Type,
            (command, failures) =>
            {
                if (!Enum.IsDefined(command.Type))
                    failures.Add(new ValidationFailure(
                        nameof(command.Type),
                        "Sample type is invalid."));
            });

        RuleFor(
            command => command.Priority,
            (command, failures) =>
            {
                if (!Enum.IsDefined(command.Priority))
                    failures.Add(new ValidationFailure(
                        nameof(command.Priority),
                        "Sample priority is invalid."));
            });
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Samples\Validators\RegisterSampleRequestValidator.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Contracts.Samples;

namespace LIMS.Application.Samples.Validators;

public static class SampleContractValidation
{
    public static bool IsValid(RegisterSampleRequest request)
    {
        return request.LaboratoryId != Guid.Empty
            && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.SampleCode)
            && request.SampleCode.Length <= 50
            && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.Name)
            && request.Name.Length <= 200
            && (request.Description?.Length ?? 0) <= 2000
            && (request.Source?.Length ?? 0) <= 250
            && Enum.IsDefined(request.Type)
            && Enum.IsDefined(request.Priority)
            && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.RegisteredByUserId);
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Application\Samples\Validators\RejectSampleCommandValidator.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Validation;
using LIMS.Application.Samples.Commands;

namespace LIMS.Application.Samples.Validators;

public sealed class RejectSampleCommandValidator
    : AbstractValidator<RejectSampleCommand>
{
    public RejectSampleCommandValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(
            command => command.SampleId,
            (command, failures) =>
            {
                if (command.SampleId == Guid.Empty)
                    failures.Add(new ValidationFailure(
                        nameof(command.SampleId),
                        "Sample identifier is required."));
            });

        RuleFor(
            command => command.Reason,
            (command, failures) =>
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(command.Reason))
                    failures.Add(new ValidationFailure(
                        nameof(command.Reason),
                        "Rejection reason is required."));
                else if (command.Reason.Length > 1000)
                    failures.Add(new ValidationFailure(
                        nameof(command.Reason),
                        "Rejection reason cannot exceed 1000 characters."));
            });
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Contracts\Instruments\Mappings\InstrumentContractMappings.cs

using LIMS.Domain.Instruments.Enums;

namespace LIMS.Contracts.Instruments.Mappings;

public static class InstrumentContractMappings
{
    public static InstrumentStatus ToDomain(this InstrumentStatusContract status)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(status))
        {
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(status), status, "Invalid instrument status.");
        }

        return (InstrumentStatus)(int)status;
    }

    public static InstrumentStatusContract ToContract(this InstrumentStatus status)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(status))
        {
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(status), status, "Invalid instrument status.");
        }

        return (InstrumentStatusContract)(int)status;
    }

    public static InstrumentType ToDomain(this InstrumentTypeContract type)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(type))
        {
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(type), type, "Invalid instrument type.");
        }

        return (InstrumentType)(int)type;
    }

    public static InstrumentTypeContract ToContract(this InstrumentType type)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(type))
        {
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(type), type, "Invalid instrument type.");
        }

        return (InstrumentTypeContract)(int)type;
    }

    public static MaintenanceType ToDomain(this MaintenanceTypeContract type)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(type))
        {
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(type), type, "Invalid maintenance type.");
        }

        return (MaintenanceType)(int)type;
    }

    public static MaintenanceTypeContract ToContract(this MaintenanceType type)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(type))
        {
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(type), type, "Invalid maintenance type.");
        }

        return (MaintenanceTypeContract)(int)type;
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Contracts\Inventory\Mappings\InventoryContractMappings.cs

using LIMS.Contracts.Inventory.Enums;
using LIMS.Contracts.Inventory.Responses;
using LIMS.Domain.Inventory.Enums;

namespace LIMS.Contracts.Inventory.Mappings;

public static class InventoryContractMappings
{
    public static InventoryItemType ToDomain(
        this InventoryItemTypeContract value)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(value))
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(value));

        return (InventoryItemType)(int)value;
    }

    public static InventoryItemTypeContract ToContract(
        this InventoryItemType value)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(value))
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(value));

        return (InventoryItemTypeContract)(int)value;
    }

    public static InventoryItemStatusContract ToContract(
        this InventoryItemStatus value)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(value))
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(value));

        return (InventoryItemStatusContract)(int)value;
    }

    public static InventoryLotStatusContract ToContract(
        this InventoryLotStatus value)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(value))
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(value));

        return (InventoryLotStatusContract)(int)value;
    }

    public static InventoryMovementTypeContract ToContract(
        this InventoryMovementType value)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(value))
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(value));

        return (InventoryMovementTypeContract)(int)value;
    }

    public static InventoryItemResponse ToResponse(
        this LIMS.Application.Inventory.DTOs.InventoryItemDto dto)
    {
        return new InventoryItemResponse(
            dto.Id,
            dto.LaboratoryId,
            dto.ItemCode,
            dto.Name,
            dto.Description,
            dto.Type.ToContract(),
            dto.Status.ToContract(),
            dto.CasNumber,
            dto.ChemicalFormula,
            dto.MolecularWeight,
            dto.HazardClassification,
            dto.SafetyDataSheetReference,
            dto.DefaultUnit,
            dto.MinimumStockLevel,
            dto.TotalAvailableQuantity,
            dto.CreatedAt,
            dto.UpdatedAt);
    }

    public static InventoryLotResponse ToResponse(
        this LIMS.Application.Inventory.DTOs.InventoryLotDto dto)
    {
        return new InventoryLotResponse(
            dto.Id,
            dto.LotNumber,
            dto.Supplier,
            dto.Manufacturer,
            dto.InitialQuantity,
            dto.AvailableQuantity,
            dto.Unit,
            dto.MinimumStockLevel,
            dto.ManufactureDate,
            dto.ExpiryDate,
            dto.StorageLocation,
            dto.StorageConditions,
            dto.CertificateOfAnalysisNumber,
            dto.Status.ToContract(),
            dto.ReceivedAt);
    }

    public static InventoryMovementResponse ToResponse(
        this LIMS.Application.Inventory.DTOs.InventoryMovementDto dto)
    {
        return new InventoryMovementResponse(
            dto.Id,
            dto.LotId,
            dto.Type.ToContract(),
            dto.Quantity,
            dto.BalanceAfterMovement,
            dto.Unit,
            dto.PerformedByUserId,
            dto.ExperimentId,
            dto.ReferenceNumber,
            dto.Notes,
            dto.OccurredAt);
    }

    public static InventoryItemDetailsResponse ToResponse(
        this LIMS.Application.Inventory.DTOs.InventoryItemDetailsDto dto)
    {
        return new InventoryItemDetailsResponse(
            dto.Item.ToResponse(),
            dto.Lots.Select(x => x.ToResponse()).ToArray(),
            dto.Movements.Select(x => x.ToResponse()).ToArray());
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Contracts\Protocols\ProtocolStatusContractExtensions.cs

using LIMS.Domain.Protocols.Enums;

namespace LIMS.Contracts.Protocols;

public static class ProtocolStatusContractExtensions
{
    public static ProtocolStatus ToDomain(this ProtocolStatusContract status)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(status))
        {
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                nameof(status),
                status,
                "The protocol status is not supported.");
        }

        return (ProtocolStatus)(int)status;
    }

    public static ProtocolStatusContract ToContract(this ProtocolStatus status)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(status))
        {
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                nameof(status),
                status,
                "The protocol status is not supported.");
        }

        return (ProtocolStatusContract)(int)status;
    }
}

using LIMS.Application.Requests.DTOs;
using LIMS.Contracts.Requests.Enums;
using LIMS.Contracts.Requests.Responses;
using LIMS.Contracts.Samples;
using LIMS.Domain.Requests.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Samples.Enums;

namespace LIMS.Contracts.Requests.Mapping;

public static class SampleRequestContractMappings
{
    public static SampleRequestResponse ToResponse(
        this SampleRequestDto request)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(request);

        return new SampleRequestResponse(
            request.Id,
            request.RequestCode,
            request.Title,
            request.Description,
            request.SampleName,
            request.SampleDescription,
            request.RequestedSampleType.ToContract(),
            request.SampleSource,
            request.RequestedByUserId,
            request.AssignedReviewerUserId,
            request.LaboratoryId,
            request.CreatedSampleId,
            request.Priority.ToContract(),
            request.Status.ToContract(),
            request.ReviewComments,
            request.RejectionReason,
            request.CancellationReason,
            request.CreatedAt,
            request.UpdatedAt,
            request.SubmittedAt,
            request.ReviewStartedAt,
            request.ApprovedAt,
            request.RejectedAt,
            request.CancelledAt,
            request.FulfilledAt);
    }

    public static SampleRequestStatusContract ToContract(
        this SampleRequestStatus status)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(status))
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(status));

        return (SampleRequestStatusContract)(int)status;
    }

    public static SampleRequestPriorityContract ToContract(
        this SampleRequestPriority priority)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(priority))
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(priority));

        return (SampleRequestPriorityContract)(int)priority;
    }

    public static SampleType ToDomain(
        this SampleTypeContract sampleType)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(sampleType))
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(sampleType));

        return (SampleType)(int)sampleType;
    }

    public static SampleRequestPriority ToDomain(
        this SampleRequestPriorityContract priority)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(priority))
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(priority));

        return (SampleRequestPriority)(int)priority;
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Contracts\Results\Mappings\ResultContractMappings.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Contracts.Results.Enums;
using LIMS.Contracts.Results.Responses;
using LIMS.Domain.Results.Enums;

namespace LIMS.Contracts.Results.Mappings;

public static class ResultContractMappings
{
    public static ResultStatus ToDomain(this ResultStatusContract status)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(status))
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(status));

        return (ResultStatus)(int)status;
    }

    public static ResultStatusContract ToContract(this ResultStatus status)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(status))
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(status));

        return (ResultStatusContract)(int)status;
    }

    public static ResultValueType ToDomain(this ResultValueTypeContract valueType)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(valueType))
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(valueType));

        return (ResultValueType)(int)valueType;
    }

    public static ResultValueTypeContract ToContract(this ResultValueType valueType)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(valueType))
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(valueType));

        return (ResultValueTypeContract)(int)valueType;
    }

    public static ResultEntryResponse ToResponse(
        this LIMS.Application.Results.DTOs.ResultEntryDto entry)
    {
        return new ResultEntryResponse(
            entry.Id,
            entry.ResultId,
            entry.ParameterName,
            entry.ValueType.ToContract(),
            entry.Value,
            entry.NumericValue,
            entry.Unit,
            entry.Uncertainty,
            entry.Method,
            entry.Notes);
    }

    public static AnalysisResultResponse ToResponse(
        this LIMS.Application.Results.DTOs.AnalysisResultDto result)
    {
        return new AnalysisResultResponse(
            result.Id,
            result.LaboratoryId,
            result.ExperimentId,
            result.SampleId,
            result.InstrumentId,
            result.ResultCode,
            result.Title,
            result.Description,
            result.Status.ToContract(),
            result.CreatedByUserId,
            result.SubmittedByUserId,
            result.ReviewedByUserId,
            result.ReviewComments,
            result.RejectionReason,
            result.ArchivedByUserId,
            result.CreatedAt,
            result.UpdatedAt,
            result.SubmittedAt,
            result.ReviewedAt,
            result.ArchivedAt);
    }

    public static AnalysisResultDetailsResponse ToDetailsResponse(
        this LIMS.Application.Results.DTOs.AnalysisResultDetailsDto result)
    {
        return new AnalysisResultDetailsResponse(
            result.ToResponse(),
            result.Entries.Select(x => x.ToResponse()).ToArray());
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Approvals\Approval.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;
using LIMS.Domain.Approvals.Entities;
using LIMS.Domain.Approvals.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Approvals.Events;
using LIMS.Domain.Approvals.Exceptions;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Approvals;

public sealed class Approval : AggregateRoot<Guid>
{
    private readonly List<ApprovalReviewer> _reviewers = [];
    private readonly List<ApprovalDecisionRecord> _decisions = [];

    public ApprovalSubjectType SubjectType { get; private set; }

    public Guid SubjectId { get; private set; }

    public string RequestedByUserId { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string? Title { get; private set; }

    public string? Description { get; private set; }

    public ApprovalStatus Status { get; private set; }

    public int RequiredApprovalCount { get; private set; }

    public DateTime CreatedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTime? ReviewStartedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTime? CompletedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTime? CancelledAt { get; private set; }

    public string? CancellationReason { get; private set; }

    public IReadOnlyCollection<ApprovalReviewer> Reviewers =>
        _reviewers.AsReadOnly();

    public IReadOnlyCollection<ApprovalDecisionRecord> Decisions =>
        _decisions.AsReadOnly();

    private Approval()
    {
    }

    private Approval(
        Guid id,
        ApprovalSubjectType subjectType,
        Guid subjectId,
        string requestedByUserId,
        string? title,
        string? description,
        int requiredApprovalCount,
        DateTime createdAt)
        : base(id)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(subjectType))
            throw new ApprovalDomainException("Approval subject type is invalid.");

        if (subjectId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new ApprovalDomainException("Approval subject ID is required.");

        if (requiredApprovalCount <= 0)
            throw new ApprovalDomainException(
                "Required approval count must be greater than zero.");

        SubjectType = subjectType;
        SubjectId = subjectId;
        RequestedByUserId = NormalizeRequired(
            requestedByUserId,
            nameof(requestedByUserId),
            150);

        Title = NormalizeOptional(title, 200);
        Description = NormalizeOptional(description, 4000);
        RequiredApprovalCount = requiredApprovalCount;
        Status = ApprovalStatus.Pending;
        CreatedAt = createdAt;
    }

    public static Approval Create(
        ApprovalSubjectType subjectType,
        Guid subjectId,
        string requestedByUserId,
        string? title,
        string? description,
        int requiredApprovalCount,
        DateTime createdAt)
    {
        var approval = new Approval(
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            subjectType,
            subjectId,
            requestedByUserId,
            title,
            description,
            requiredApprovalCount,
            createdAt);

        approval.AddDomainEvent(
            new ApprovalCreatedDomainEvent(
                approval.Id,
                subjectType,
                subjectId,
                approval.RequestedByUserId,
                createdAt));

        return approval;
    }

    public Guid AddReviewer(
        string reviewerUserId,
        int sequence,
        bool isRequired,
        DateTime assignedAt)
    {
        EnsurePending();

        var normalizedUserId = NormalizeRequired(
            reviewerUserId,
            nameof(reviewerUserId),
            150);

        if (string.Equals(
                normalizedUserId,
                RequestedByUserId,
                StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            throw new ApprovalDomainException(
                "The approval requester cannot review their own approval.");
        }

        if (_reviewers.Any(x => string.Equals(
                x.UserId,
                normalizedUserId,
                StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
        {
            throw new ApprovalDomainException(
                "The reviewer is already assigned to this approval.");
        }

        if (_reviewers.Any(x => x.Sequence == sequence))
            throw new ApprovalDomainException(
                "A reviewer with this sequence already exists.");

        var reviewer = new ApprovalReviewer(
            Id,
            normalizedUserId,
            sequence,
            isRequired,
            assignedAt);

        _reviewers.Add(reviewer);
        _reviewers.Sort((x, y) => x.Sequence.CompareTo(y.Sequence));

        return reviewer.Id;
    }

    public void RemoveReviewer(Guid reviewerId)
    {
        EnsurePending();

        var reviewer = FindReviewer(reviewerId);
        _reviewers.Remove(reviewer);
    }

    public void StartReview(DateTime startedAt)
    {
        EnsurePending();

        if (_reviewers.Count == 0)
            throw new ApprovalDomainException(
                "At least one reviewer must be assigned.");

        if (_reviewers.Count(x => x.IsRequired) < RequiredApprovalCount)
            throw new ApprovalDomainException(
                "There are not enough required reviewers to satisfy the approval threshold.");

        ReviewStartedAt = startedAt;
        Status = ApprovalStatus.InReview;

        AddDomainEvent(
            new ApprovalReviewStartedDomainEvent(
                Id,
                string.Join(",", _reviewers.Select(x => x.UserId)),
                startedAt,
                startedAt));
    }

    public void RecordDecision(
        string reviewerUserId,
        ApprovalDecision decision,
        string? comments,
        DateTime decidedAt)
    {
        if (Status != ApprovalStatus.InReview)
            throw new ApprovalDomainException(
                "Approval is not currently under review.");

        if (!Enum.IsDefined(decision))
            throw new ApprovalDomainException("Approval decision is invalid.");

        var normalizedUserId = NormalizeRequired(
            reviewerUserId,
            nameof(reviewerUserId),
            150);

        var reviewer = _reviewers.FirstOrDefault(x =>
            string.Equals(
                x.UserId,
                normalizedUserId,
                StringComparison.Ordinal));

        if (reviewer is null)
            throw new ApprovalDomainException(
                "User is not assigned as a reviewer for this approval.");

        if (reviewer.HasActed)
            throw new ApprovalDomainException(
                "Reviewer has already submitted a decision.");

        if (decision is ApprovalDecision.Reject
            or ApprovalDecision.ReturnForRevision
            && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(comments))
        {
            throw new ApprovalDomainException(
                "Comments are required for rejection or return decisions.");
        }

        var record = new ApprovalDecisionRecord(
            Id,
            normalizedUserId,
            decision,
            comments,
            decidedAt);

        reviewer.MarkActed(decidedAt);
        _decisions.Add(record);

        AddDomainEvent(
            new ApprovalDecisionRecordedDomainEvent(
                Id,
                decision,
                normalizedUserId,
                comments,
                decidedAt,
                decidedAt));

        if (decision == ApprovalDecision.Reject)
        {
            Status = ApprovalStatus.Rejected;
            CompletedAt = decidedAt;
            return;
        }

        if (decision == ApprovalDecision.ReturnForRevision)
        {
            Status = ApprovalStatus.Returned;
            CompletedAt = decidedAt;
            return;
        }

        var approvals = _decisions.Count(x =>
            x.Decision == ApprovalDecision.Approve);

        var pendingRequiredReviewers = _reviewers.Any(x =>
            x.IsRequired
            && !x.HasActed);

        if (approvals >= RequiredApprovalCount
            && !pendingRequiredReviewers)
        {
            Status = ApprovalStatus.Approved;
            CompletedAt = decidedAt;
        }
    }

    public void Cancel(
        string cancelledByUserId,
        string? reason,
        DateTime cancelledAt)
    {
        if (Status is ApprovalStatus.Approved
            or ApprovalStatus.Rejected
            or ApprovalStatus.Cancelled)
        {
            throw new ApprovalDomainException(
                "A completed approval cannot be cancelled.");
        }

        var userId = NormalizeRequired(
            cancelledByUserId,
            nameof(cancelledByUserId),
            150);

        if (!string.Equals(
                userId,
                RequestedByUserId,
                StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            throw new ApprovalDomainException(
                "Only the requester can cancel this approval.");
        }

        CancellationReason = NormalizeOptional(reason, 2000);
        CancelledAt = cancelledAt;
        Status = ApprovalStatus.Cancelled;

        AddDomainEvent(
            new ApprovalCancelledDomainEvent(
                Id,
                userId,
                CancellationReason,
                cancelledAt,
                cancelledAt));
    }

    private ApprovalReviewer FindReviewer(Guid reviewerId)
    {
        if (reviewerId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new ApprovalDomainException("Reviewer ID is required.");

        return _reviewers.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Id == reviewerId)
            ?? throw new ApprovalDomainException(
                $"Reviewer '{reviewerId}' was not found.");
    }

    private void EnsurePending()
    {
        if (Status != ApprovalStatus.Pending)
            throw new ApprovalDomainException(
                "Reviewers can only be managed while approval is pending.");
    }

    private static string NormalizeRequired(
        string? value,
        string fieldName,
        int maxLength)
    {
        var normalized = value?.Trim();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalized))
            throw new ApprovalDomainException($"{fieldName} is required.");

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new ApprovalDomainException(
                $"{fieldName} cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }

    private static string? NormalizeOptional(string? value, int maxLength)
    {
        var normalized = value?.Trim();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalized))
            return null;

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new ApprovalDomainException(
                $"Value cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }
}

====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;
using LIMS.Domain.Approvals.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Approvals.Exceptions;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Approvals.Entities;

public sealed class ApprovalDecisionRecord : Entity<Guid>
{
    public Guid ApprovalId { get; private set; }

    public string ReviewerUserId { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public ApprovalDecision Decision { get; private set; }

    public string? Comments { get; private set; }

    public DateTime DecidedAt { get; private set; }

    private ApprovalDecisionRecord()
    {
    }

    internal ApprovalDecisionRecord(
        Guid approvalId,
        string reviewerUserId,
        ApprovalDecision decision,
        string? comments,
        DateTime decidedAt)
        : base(Guid.NewGuid())
    {
        if (approvalId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new ApprovalDomainException("Approval ID is required.");

        if (!Enum.IsDefined(decision))
            throw new ApprovalDomainException("Approval decision is invalid.");

        ApprovalId = approvalId;
        ReviewerUserId = NormalizeRequired(
            reviewerUserId,
            nameof(reviewerUserId),
            150);

        Decision = decision;
        Comments = NormalizeOptional(comments, 4000);
        DecidedAt = decidedAt;
    }

    private static string NormalizeRequired(
        string? value,
        string fieldName,
        int maxLength)
    {
        var normalized = value?.Trim();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalized))
            throw new ApprovalDomainException($"{fieldName} is required.");

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new ApprovalDomainException(
                $"{fieldName} cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }

    private static string? NormalizeOptional(string? value, int maxLength)
    {
        var normalized = value?.Trim();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalized))
            return null;

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new ApprovalDomainException(
                $"Value cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Approvals\Entities\ApprovalReviewer.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;
using LIMS.Domain.Approvals.Exceptions;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Approvals.Entities;

public sealed class ApprovalReviewer : Entity<Guid>
{
    public Guid ApprovalId { get; private set; }

    public string UserId { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public int Sequence { get; private set; }

    public bool IsRequired { get; private set; }

    public bool HasActed { get; private set; }

    public DateTime AssignedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTime? ActedAt { get; private set; }

    private ApprovalReviewer()
    {
    }

    internal ApprovalReviewer(
        Guid approvalId,
        string userId,
        int sequence,
        bool isRequired,
        DateTime assignedAt)
        : base(Guid.NewGuid())
    {
        if (approvalId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new ApprovalDomainException("Approval ID is required.");

        if (sequence <= 0)
            throw new ApprovalDomainException("Reviewer sequence must be greater than zero.");

        ApprovalId = approvalId;
        UserId = NormalizeRequired(userId, nameof(userId), 150);
        Sequence = sequence;
        IsRequired = isRequired;
        AssignedAt = assignedAt;
    }

    internal void MarkActed(DateTime actedAt)
    {
        if (HasActed)
            throw new ApprovalDomainException(
                "Reviewer has already submitted a decision.");

        HasActed = true;
        ActedAt = actedAt;
    }

    private static string NormalizeRequired(
        string? value,
        string fieldName,
        int maxLength)
    {
        var normalized = value?.Trim();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalized))
            throw new ApprovalDomainException($"{fieldName} is required.");

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new ApprovalDomainException(
                $"{fieldName} cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Common\AggregateRoot.cs
====================================================================================================
namespace LIMS.Domain.Common;

public abstract class AggregateRoot<TId> : Entity<TId>
{
    private readonly List<IDomainEvent> _domainEvents = [];

    public IReadOnlyCollection<IDomainEvent> DomainEvents => _domainEvents.AsReadOnly();

    protected AggregateRoot() { }

    protected AggregateRoot(TId id) : base(id) { }

    protected void AddDomainEvent(IDomainEvent domainEvent)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(domainEvent);
        _domainEvents.Add(domainEvent);
    }

    public void ClearDomainEvents()
    {
        _domainEvents.Clear();
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Common\DomainException.cs
====================================================================================================
namespace LIMS.Domain.Common;

public abstract class DomainException : Exception
{
    protected DomainException(string message) : base(message) { }
}

namespace LIMS.Domain.Common;

public abstract class Entity<TId>
{
    public TId Id { get; protected set; } = default!;

    protected Entity() { }

    protected Entity(TId id)
    {
        Id = id;
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Common\ValueObject.cs
====================================================================================================
namespace LIMS.Domain.Common;

public abstract class ValueObject
{
    protected abstract IEnumerable<object?> GetEqualityComponents();

    public override bool Equals(object? obj)
    {
        if (obj is not ValueObject other || GetType() != obj.GetType())
            return false;

        return GetEqualityComponents().SequenceEqual(other.GetEqualityComponents());
    }

    public override int GetHashCode()
    {
        return GetEqualityComponents()
            .Aggregate(1, (current, item) =>
                HashCode.Combine(current, item));
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Experiments\Experiment.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;
using LIMS.Domain.Experiments.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Experiments.Events;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Experiments;

public sealed class Experiment : AggregateRoot<Guid>
{
    public Guid LaboratoryId { get; private set; }

    public Guid SampleId { get; private set; }

    public string ExperimentCode { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string Title { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string? Description { get; private set; }

    public ExperimentStatus Status { get; private set; }

    public string CreatedByUserId { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string? AssignedToUserId { get; private set; }

    public DateTimeOffset CreatedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTimeOffset? UpdatedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTimeOffset? StartedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTimeOffset? CompletedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTimeOffset? ArchivedAt { get; private set; }

    private Experiment()
    {
    }

    private Experiment(
        Guid id,
        Guid laboratoryId,
        Guid sampleId,
        string experimentCode,
        string title,
        string? description,
        string createdByUserId,
        DateTimeOffset createdAt) : base(id)
    {
        LaboratoryId = laboratoryId;
        SampleId = sampleId;
        ExperimentCode = NormalizeCode(experimentCode);
        Title = NormalizeRequiredText(title, nameof(title), 200);
        Description = NormalizeOptionalText(description, 2000);
        CreatedByUserId = NormalizeRequiredText(
            createdByUserId,
            nameof(createdByUserId),
            150);

        Status = ExperimentStatus.Planned;
        CreatedAt = createdAt;
    }

    public static Experiment Create(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        Guid sampleId,
        string experimentCode,
        string title,
        string? description,
        string createdByUserId,
        DateTimeOffset createdAt)
    {
        if (laboratoryId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new ExperimentDomainException("Laboratory identifier is required.");

        if (sampleId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new ExperimentDomainException("Sample identifier is required.");

        var experiment = new Experiment(
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            laboratoryId,
            sampleId,
            experimentCode,
            title,
            description,
            createdByUserId,
            createdAt);

        experiment.AddDomainEvent(new ExperimentCreatedDomainEvent(
            experiment.Id,
            experiment.LaboratoryId,
            experiment.SampleId,
            experiment.ExperimentCode,
            createdAt));

        return experiment;
    }

    public void UpdateInformation(
        string title,
        string? description,
        DateTimeOffset updatedAt)
    {
        EnsureEditable();

        Title = NormalizeRequiredText(title, nameof(title), 200);
        Description = NormalizeOptionalText(description, 2000);
        UpdatedAt = updatedAt;
    }

    public void AssignTo(string userId, DateTimeOffset assignedAt)
    {
        EnsureNotTerminal();

        AssignedToUserId = NormalizeRequiredText(
            userId,
            nameof(userId),
            150);

        UpdatedAt = assignedAt;
    }

    public void Unassign(DateTimeOffset updatedAt)
    {
        EnsureNotTerminal();

        AssignedToUserId = null;
        UpdatedAt = updatedAt;
    }

    public void Start(DateTimeOffset startedAt)
    {
        if (Status != ExperimentStatus.Planned)
        {
            throw new ExperimentDomainException(
                "Only planned experiments can be started.");
        }

        Status = ExperimentStatus.InProgress;
        StartedAt = startedAt;
        UpdatedAt = startedAt;

        AddDomainEvent(new ExperimentStartedDomainEvent(
            Id,
            SampleId,
            startedAt,
            startedAt));
    }

    public void Complete(DateTimeOffset completedAt)
    {
        if (Status != ExperimentStatus.InProgress)
        {
            throw new ExperimentDomainException(
                "Only in-progress experiments can be completed.");
        }

        if (StartedAt.HasValue && completedAt < StartedAt.Value)
        {
            throw new ExperimentDomainException(
                "Completion time cannot be earlier than start time.");
        }

        Status = ExperimentStatus.Completed;
        CompletedAt = completedAt;
        UpdatedAt = completedAt;

        AddDomainEvent(new ExperimentCompletedDomainEvent(
            Id,
            SampleId,
            completedAt,
            completedAt));
    }

    public void Cancel(string reason, DateTimeOffset cancelledAt)
    {
        if (Status is ExperimentStatus.Completed
            or ExperimentStatus.Cancelled
            or ExperimentStatus.Archived)
        {
            throw new ExperimentDomainException(
                "This experiment cannot be cancelled in its current status.");
        }

        var normalizedReason = NormalizeRequiredText(
            reason,
            nameof(reason),
            1000);

        Status = ExperimentStatus.Cancelled;
        UpdatedAt = cancelledAt;

        AddDomainEvent(new ExperimentCancelledDomainEvent(
            Id,
            SampleId,
            normalizedReason,
            cancelledAt));
    }

    public void Archive(DateTimeOffset archivedAt)
    {
        if (Status is not (ExperimentStatus.Completed or ExperimentStatus.Cancelled))
        {
            throw new ExperimentDomainException(
                "Only completed or cancelled experiments can be archived.");
        }

        Status = ExperimentStatus.Archived;
        ArchivedAt = archivedAt;
        UpdatedAt = archivedAt;

        AddDomainEvent(new ExperimentArchivedDomainEvent(
            Id,
            archivedAt));
    }

    private void EnsureEditable()
    {
        if (Status != ExperimentStatus.Planned)
        {
            throw new ExperimentDomainException(
                "Only planned experiments can be modified.");
        }
    }

    private void EnsureNotTerminal()
    {
        if (Status is ExperimentStatus.Completed
            or ExperimentStatus.Cancelled
            or ExperimentStatus.Archived)
        {
            throw new ExperimentDomainException(
                "This experiment cannot be modified in its current status.");
        }
    }

    private static string NormalizeCode(string value)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
            throw new ExperimentDomainException("Experiment code is required.");

        var normalized = value.Trim().ToUpperInvariant();

        if (normalized.Length > 50)
            throw new ExperimentDomainException(
                "Experiment code cannot exceed 50 characters.");

        if (normalized.Any(character =>
                !char.IsLetterOrDigit(character)
                && character is not '-' and not '_'))
        {
            throw new ExperimentDomainException(
                "Experiment code can contain only letters, numbers, hyphens and underscores.");
        }

        return normalized;
    }

    private static string NormalizeRequiredText(
        string value,
        string parameterName,
        int maxLength)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
        {
            throw new ExperimentDomainException(
                $"{parameterName} is required.");
        }

        var normalized = value.Trim();

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
        {
            throw new ExperimentDomainException(
                $"{parameterName} cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");
        }

        return normalized;
    }

    private static string? NormalizeOptionalText(
        string? value,
        int maxLength)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
            return null;

        var normalized = value.Trim();

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
        {
            throw new ExperimentDomainException(
                $"Description cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");
        }

        return normalized;
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Files\ValueObjects\FileChecksum.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Files.ValueObjects;

public sealed class FileChecksum : ValueObject
{
    public string Algorithm { get; }
    public string Value { get; }

    private FileChecksum(string algorithm, string value)
    {
        Algorithm = algorithm;
        Value = value;
    }

    public static FileChecksum CreateSha256(string value)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
            throw new FileDomainException("SHA-256 checksum is required.");

        var normalized = value.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();

        if (normalized.Length != 64 || normalized.Any(c => !Uri.IsHexDigit(c)))
            throw new FileDomainException("SHA-256 checksum must contain exactly 64 hexadecimal characters.");

        return new FileChecksum("SHA256", normalized);
    }

    protected override IEnumerable<object?> GetEqualityComponents()
    {
        yield return Algorithm;
        yield return Value;
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Files\ValueObjects\ObjectStorageReference.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Files.ValueObjects;

public sealed class ObjectStorageReference : ValueObject
{
    public string Provider { get; }
    public string Bucket { get; }
    public string ObjectKey { get; }

    private ObjectStorageReference(
        string provider,
        string bucket,
        string objectKey)
    {
        Provider = provider;
        Bucket = bucket;
        ObjectKey = objectKey;
    }

    public static ObjectStorageReference Create(
        string provider,
        string bucket,
        string objectKey)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(provider))
            throw new FileDomainException("Storage provider is required.");

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(bucket))
            throw new FileDomainException("Storage bucket is required.");

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(objectKey))
            throw new FileDomainException("Storage object key is required.");

        if (provider.Length > 100)
            throw new FileDomainException("Storage provider cannot exceed 100 characters.");

        if (bucket.Length > 255)
            throw new FileDomainException("Storage bucket cannot exceed 255 characters.");

        if (objectKey.Length > 1000)
            throw new FileDomainException("Storage object key cannot exceed 1000 characters.");

        return new ObjectStorageReference(
            provider.Trim(),
            bucket.Trim(),
            objectKey.Trim());
    }

    protected override IEnumerable<object?> GetEqualityComponents()
    {
        yield return Provider;
        yield return Bucket;
        yield return ObjectKey;
    }
}
