====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Instruments\Instrument.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;
using LIMS.Domain.Instruments.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Instruments.Events;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Instruments;

public sealed class Instrument : AggregateRoot<Guid>
{
    private readonly List<InstrumentMaintenance> _maintenanceRecords = [];

    private Instrument()
    {
    }

    private Instrument(Guid id)
        : base(id)
    {
    }

    public Guid LaboratoryId { get; private set; }

    public string InstrumentCode { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string Name { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string? Manufacturer { get; private set; }

    public string? Model { get; private set; }

    public string? SerialNumber { get; private set; }

    public InstrumentType Type { get; private set; }

    public InstrumentStatus Status { get; private set; }

    public string? Location { get; private set; }

    public string? Description { get; private set; }

    public DateTimeOffset RegisteredAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTimeOffset UpdatedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTimeOffset? RetiredAt { get; private set; }

    public IReadOnlyCollection<InstrumentMaintenance> MaintenanceRecords =>
        _maintenanceRecords.AsReadOnly();

    public static Instrument Register(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string instrumentCode,
        string name,
        InstrumentType type,
        string? manufacturer,
        string? model,
        string? serialNumber,
        string? location,
        string? description,
        DateTimeOffset registeredAt)
    {
        if (laboratoryId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("LaboratoryId is required.");

        var normalizedCode = NormalizeCode(instrumentCode);
        var normalizedName = NormalizeRequiredText(name, 200, "Name");

        if (!Enum.IsDefined(type))
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("Instrument type is invalid.");

        var instrument = new Instrument(Guid.NewGuid())
        {
            LaboratoryId = laboratoryId,
            InstrumentCode = normalizedCode,
            Name = normalizedName,
            Type = type,
            Manufacturer = NormalizeOptionalText(manufacturer, 150, "Manufacturer"),
            Model = NormalizeOptionalText(model, 150, "Model"),
            SerialNumber = NormalizeOptionalText(serialNumber, 150, "SerialNumber"),
            Location = NormalizeOptionalText(location, 250, "Location"),
            Description = NormalizeOptionalText(description, 2000, "Description"),
            Status = InstrumentStatus.Available,
            RegisteredAt = registeredAt,
            UpdatedAt = registeredAt
        };

        instrument.AddDomainEvent(
            new InstrumentRegisteredDomainEvent(
                instrument.Id,
                laboratoryId,
                normalizedCode,
                registeredAt));

        return instrument;
    }

    public void UpdateInformation(
        string name,
        InstrumentType type,
        string? manufacturer,
        string? model,
        string? serialNumber,
        string? location,
        string? description,
        DateTimeOffset updatedAt)
    {
        EnsureNotRetired();

        if (!Enum.IsDefined(type))
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("Instrument type is invalid.");

        Name = NormalizeRequiredText(name, 200, "Name");
        Type = type;
        Manufacturer = NormalizeOptionalText(manufacturer, 150, "Manufacturer");
        Model = NormalizeOptionalText(model, 150, "Model");
        SerialNumber = NormalizeOptionalText(serialNumber, 150, "SerialNumber");
        Location = NormalizeOptionalText(location, 250, "Location");
        Description = NormalizeOptionalText(description, 2000, "Description");
        UpdatedAt = updatedAt;
    }

    public void MarkInUse(DateTimeOffset occurredOn)
    {
        EnsureNotRetired();

        if (Status != InstrumentStatus.Available)
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("Only available instruments can be marked as in use.");

        ChangeStatus(InstrumentStatus.InUse, occurredOn);
    }

    public void MarkAvailable(DateTimeOffset occurredOn)
    {
        EnsureNotRetired();

        if (Status != InstrumentStatus.InUse)
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("Only instruments in use can be marked as available.");

        ChangeStatus(InstrumentStatus.Available, occurredOn);
    }

    public void ScheduleMaintenance(
        MaintenanceType type,
        string description,
        DateTimeOffset scheduledAt,
        string scheduledByUserId,
        DateTimeOffset occurredOn)
    {
        EnsureNotRetired();

        if (Status == InstrumentStatus.InUse)
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("Maintenance cannot be scheduled while the instrument is in use.");

        if (_maintenanceRecords.Any(x => !x.IsCompleted))
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("An active maintenance record already exists.");

        var maintenance = new InstrumentMaintenance(
            Id,
            type,
            description,
            scheduledAt,
            scheduledByUserId);

        _maintenanceRecords.Add(maintenance);

        ChangeStatus(InstrumentStatus.UnderMaintenance, occurredOn);

        AddDomainEvent(
            new InstrumentMaintenanceScheduledDomainEvent(
                Id,
                maintenance.Id,
                type,
                scheduledAt,
                occurredOn));

        UpdatedAt = occurredOn;
    }

    public void CompleteMaintenance(
        Guid maintenanceId,
        string completedByUserId,
        string? completionNotes,
        DateTimeOffset completedAt)
    {
        var maintenance = _maintenanceRecords.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Id == maintenanceId)
            ?? throw new InstrumentDomainException("Maintenance record was not found.");

        maintenance.Complete(
            completedByUserId,
            completionNotes,
            completedAt);

        AddDomainEvent(
            new InstrumentMaintenanceCompletedDomainEvent(
                Id,
                maintenanceId,
                completedAt,
                completedAt));

        if (!_maintenanceRecords.Any(x => !x.IsCompleted))
            ChangeStatus(InstrumentStatus.Available, completedAt);

        UpdatedAt = completedAt;
    }

    public void MarkOutOfService(string reason, DateTimeOffset occurredOn)
    {
        EnsureNotRetired();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(reason))
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("A reason is required.");

        if (reason.Trim().Length > 1000)
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("Reason cannot exceed 1000 characters.");

        if (Status == InstrumentStatus.OutOfService)
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("Instrument is already out of service.");

        ChangeStatus(InstrumentStatus.OutOfService, occurredOn);
        UpdatedAt = occurredOn;
    }

    public void RestoreService(DateTimeOffset occurredOn)
    {
        EnsureNotRetired();

        if (Status != InstrumentStatus.OutOfService)
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("Only out-of-service instruments can be restored.");

        if (_maintenanceRecords.Any(x => !x.IsCompleted))
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("Active maintenance must be completed before restoring the instrument.");

        ChangeStatus(InstrumentStatus.Available, occurredOn);
        UpdatedAt = occurredOn;
    }

    public void Retire(DateTimeOffset retiredAt)
    {
        if (Status == InstrumentStatus.Retired)
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("Instrument is already retired.");

        if (Status == InstrumentStatus.InUse)
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("An instrument in use cannot be retired.");

        if (_maintenanceRecords.Any(x => !x.IsCompleted))
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("Active maintenance must be completed before retiring the instrument.");

        RetiredAt = retiredAt;
        ChangeStatus(InstrumentStatus.Retired, retiredAt);
        UpdatedAt = retiredAt;
    }

    private void ChangeStatus(
        InstrumentStatus newStatus,
        DateTimeOffset occurredOn)
    {
        if (Status == newStatus)
            return;

        var previousStatus = Status;
        Status = newStatus;

        AddDomainEvent(
            new InstrumentStatusChangedDomainEvent(
                Id,
                previousStatus,
                newStatus,
                occurredOn));
    }

    private void EnsureNotRetired()
    {
        if (Status == InstrumentStatus.Retired)
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("Retired instruments cannot be modified.");
    }

    private static string NormalizeCode(string value)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("InstrumentCode is required.");

        var normalized = value.Trim().ToUpperInvariant();

        if (normalized.Length > 50)
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("InstrumentCode cannot exceed 50 characters.");

        if (normalized.Any(c =>
                !char.IsAsciiLetterOrDigit(c) &&
                c != '-' &&
                c != '_'))
        {
            throw new InstrumentDomainException(
                "InstrumentCode can contain only letters, numbers, hyphens and underscores.");
        }

        return normalized;
    }

    private static string NormalizeRequiredText(
        string value,
        int maxLength,
        string fieldName)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
            throw new InstrumentDomainException($"{fieldName} is required.");

        var normalized = value.Trim();

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new InstrumentDomainException($"{fieldName} cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }

    private static string? NormalizeOptionalText(
        string? value,
        int maxLength,
        string fieldName)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
            return null;

        var normalized = value.Trim();

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new InstrumentDomainException($"{fieldName} cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Instruments\InstrumentMaintenance.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;
using LIMS.Domain.Instruments.Enums;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Instruments;

public sealed class InstrumentMaintenance : Entity<Guid>
{
    private InstrumentMaintenance()
    {
    }

    internal InstrumentMaintenance(
        Guid instrumentId,
        MaintenanceType type,
        string description,
        DateTimeOffset scheduledAt,
        string scheduledByUserId)
        : base(Guid.NewGuid())
    {
        if (instrumentId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("InstrumentId is required.");

        if (!Enum.IsDefined(type))
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("Maintenance type is invalid.");

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(description))
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("Maintenance description is required.");

        if (description.Trim().Length > 2000)
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("Maintenance description cannot exceed 2000 characters.");

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(scheduledByUserId))
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("ScheduledByUserId is required.");

        if (scheduledByUserId.Trim().Length > 150)
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("ScheduledByUserId cannot exceed 150 characters.");

        InstrumentId = instrumentId;
        Type = type;
        Description = description.Trim();
        ScheduledAt = scheduledAt;
        ScheduledByUserId = scheduledByUserId.Trim();
    }

    public Guid InstrumentId { get; private set; }

    public MaintenanceType Type { get; private set; }

    public string Description { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public DateTimeOffset ScheduledAt { get; private set; }

    public string ScheduledByUserId { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public DateTimeOffset? CompletedAt { get; private set; }

    public string? CompletedByUserId { get; private set; }

    public string? CompletionNotes { get; private set; }

    public bool IsCompleted => CompletedAt.HasValue;

    internal void Complete(
        string completedByUserId,
        string? completionNotes,
        DateTimeOffset completedAt)
    {
        if (IsCompleted)
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("Maintenance has already been completed.");

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(completedByUserId))
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("CompletedByUserId is required.");

        if (completedByUserId.Trim().Length > 150)
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("CompletedByUserId cannot exceed 150 characters.");

        if (completionNotes?.Trim().Length > 2000)
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("CompletionNotes cannot exceed 2000 characters.");

        if (completedAt < ScheduledAt)
            throw new InstrumentDomainException("Maintenance cannot be completed before its scheduled time.");

        CompletedAt = completedAt;
        CompletedByUserId = completedByUserId.Trim();
        CompletionNotes = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(completionNotes)
            ? null
            : completionNotes.Trim();
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Laboratories\Entities\LaboratoryMember.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Laboratories.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Laboratories.Exceptions;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Laboratories.Entities;

public sealed class LaboratoryMember
{
    public Guid Id { get; private set; }

    public Guid LaboratoryId { get; private set; }

    public string UserId { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public LaboratoryMemberRole Role { get; private set; }

    public DateTimeOffset JoinedAt { get; private set; }

    private LaboratoryMember()
    {
    }

    internal LaboratoryMember(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string userId,
        LaboratoryMemberRole role,
        DateTimeOffset joinedAt)
    {
        if (laboratoryId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new LaboratoryDomainException("Laboratory identifier is required.");

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(userId))
            throw new LaboratoryDomainException("User identifier is required.");

        if (!Enum.IsDefined(role))
            throw new LaboratoryDomainException("Invalid laboratory member role.");

        Id = Guid.NewGuid();
        LaboratoryId = laboratoryId;
        UserId = userId.Trim();
        Role = role;
        JoinedAt = joinedAt;
    }

    internal void ChangeRole(LaboratoryMemberRole role)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(role))
            throw new LaboratoryDomainException("Invalid laboratory member role.");

        Role = role;
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Laboratories\Laboratory.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;
using LIMS.Domain.Laboratories.Entities;
using LIMS.Domain.Laboratories.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Laboratories.Events;
using LIMS.Domain.Laboratories.Exceptions;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Laboratories;

public sealed class Laboratory : AggregateRoot<Guid>
{
    private readonly List<LaboratoryMember> _members = [];

    public string Name { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string Code { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string? Description { get; private set; }

    public LaboratoryStatus Status { get; private set; }

    public DateTimeOffset CreatedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTimeOffset? UpdatedAt { get; private set; }

    public IReadOnlyCollection<LaboratoryMember> Members =>
        _members.AsReadOnly();

    private Laboratory()
    {
    }

    public static Laboratory Create(
        string name,
        string code,
        string? description,
        DateTimeOffset createdAt)
    {
        var laboratory = new Laboratory
        {
            Id = Guid.NewGuid(),
            Name = NormalizeRequired(name, nameof(name), 150),
            Code = NormalizeCode(code),
            Description = NormalizeOptional(description, 1000),
            Status = LaboratoryStatus.Active,
            CreatedAt = createdAt
        };

        laboratory.AddDomainEvent(
            new LaboratoryCreatedDomainEvent(
                laboratory.Id,
                laboratory.Name,
                createdAt));

        return laboratory;
    }

    public void UpdateInformation(
        string name,
        string code,
        string? description,
        DateTimeOffset updatedAt)
    {
        EnsureNotArchived();

        Name = NormalizeRequired(name, nameof(name), 150);
        Code = NormalizeCode(code);
        Description = NormalizeOptional(description, 1000);
        UpdatedAt = updatedAt;
    }

    public void AddMember(
        string userId,
        LaboratoryMemberRole role,
        DateTimeOffset joinedAt)
    {
        EnsureActive();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(userId))
            throw new LaboratoryDomainException("User identifier is required.");

        var normalizedUserId = userId.Trim();

        if (_members.Any(x =>
                string.Equals(
                    x.UserId,
                    normalizedUserId,
                    StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
        {
            throw new LaboratoryDomainException(
                "The user is already a member of this laboratory.");
        }

        var member = new LaboratoryMember(
            Id,
            normalizedUserId,
            role,
            joinedAt);

        _members.Add(member);

        AddDomainEvent(
            new LaboratoryMemberAddedDomainEvent(
                Id,
                member.UserId,
                member.Role,
                joinedAt));

        UpdatedAt = joinedAt;
    }

    public void ChangeMemberRole(
        string userId,
        LaboratoryMemberRole role,
        DateTimeOffset updatedAt)
    {
        EnsureActive();

        var member = FindMember(userId);
        member.ChangeRole(role);

        UpdatedAt = updatedAt;
    }

    public void RemoveMember(
        string userId,
        DateTimeOffset removedAt)
    {
        EnsureNotArchived();

        var member = FindMember(userId);

        _members.Remove(member);

        AddDomainEvent(
            new LaboratoryMemberRemovedDomainEvent(
                Id,
                member.UserId,
                removedAt));

        UpdatedAt = removedAt;
    }

    public void ChangeStatus(
        LaboratoryStatus newStatus,
        DateTimeOffset changedAt)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(newStatus))
            throw new LaboratoryDomainException("Invalid laboratory status.");

        if (Status == LaboratoryStatus.Archived)
            throw new LaboratoryDomainException(
                "An archived laboratory cannot change its status.");

        if (Status == newStatus)
            return;

        var previousStatus = Status;

        Status = newStatus;
        UpdatedAt = changedAt;

        AddDomainEvent(
            new LaboratoryStatusChangedDomainEvent(
                Id,
                previousStatus,
                newStatus,
                changedAt));
    }

    private LaboratoryMember FindMember(string userId)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(userId))
            throw new LaboratoryDomainException("User identifier is required.");

        return _members.FirstOrDefault(x =>
                   string.Equals(
                       x.UserId,
                       userId.Trim(),
                       StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
               ?? throw new LaboratoryDomainException(
                   "The specified user is not a member of this laboratory.");
    }

    private void EnsureActive()
    {
        if (Status != LaboratoryStatus.Active)
            throw new LaboratoryDomainException(
                "Only active laboratories can perform this operation.");
    }

    private void EnsureNotArchived()
    {
        if (Status == LaboratoryStatus.Archived)
            throw new LaboratoryDomainException(
                "Archived laboratories cannot be modified.");
    }

    private static string NormalizeRequired(
        string value,
        string fieldName,
        int maxLength)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
            throw new LaboratoryDomainException(
                $"{fieldName} is required.");

        var normalized = value.Trim();

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new LaboratoryDomainException(
                $"{fieldName} cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }

    private static string NormalizeCode(string code)
    {
        var normalized = NormalizeRequired(code, nameof(code), 30)
            .ToUpperInvariant();

        if (normalized.Any(character =>
                !char.IsLetterOrDigit(character) &&
                character != '-' &&
                character != '_'))
        {
            throw new LaboratoryDomainException(
                "Laboratory code can contain only letters, numbers, hyphens and underscores.");
        }

        return normalized;
    }

    private static string? NormalizeOptional(
        string? value,
        int maxLength)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
            return null;

        var normalized = value.Trim();

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new LaboratoryDomainException(
                $"Description cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Protocols\Protocol.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;
using LIMS.Domain.Protocols.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Protocols.Events;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Protocols;

public sealed class Protocol : AggregateRoot<Guid>
{
    private readonly List<ProtocolStep> _steps = [];

    public Guid LaboratoryId { get; private set; }

    public string ProtocolCode { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string Name { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string? Description { get; private set; }

    public string? Version { get; private set; }

    public ProtocolStatus Status { get; private set; }

    public string CreatedByUserId { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public DateTimeOffset CreatedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTimeOffset UpdatedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTimeOffset? ActivatedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTimeOffset? DeprecatedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTimeOffset? ArchivedAt { get; private set; }

    public IReadOnlyCollection<ProtocolStep> Steps => _steps.AsReadOnly();

    private Protocol()
    {
    }

    private Protocol(
        Guid id,
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string protocolCode,
        string name,
        string? description,
        string? version,
        string createdByUserId,
        DateTimeOffset createdAt)
        : base(id)
    {
        if (laboratoryId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new ProtocolDomainException("LaboratoryId is required.");

        LaboratoryId = laboratoryId;
        ProtocolCode = NormalizeCode(protocolCode);
        Name = NormalizeRequiredText(name, 200, "Protocol name");
        Description = NormalizeOptionalText(description, 4000, "Description");
        Version = NormalizeOptionalText(version, 50, "Version");
        CreatedByUserId = NormalizeRequiredText(
            createdByUserId,
            150,
            "CreatedByUserId");

        Status = ProtocolStatus.Draft;
        CreatedAt = createdAt;
        UpdatedAt = createdAt;

        AddDomainEvent(new ProtocolCreatedDomainEvent(
            Id,
            LaboratoryId,
            ProtocolCode,
            createdAt));
    }

    public static Protocol Create(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string protocolCode,
        string name,
        string? description,
        string? version,
        string createdByUserId,
        DateTimeOffset createdAt)
    {
        return new Protocol(
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            laboratoryId,
            protocolCode,
            name,
            description,
            version,
            createdByUserId,
            createdAt);
    }

    public void UpdateInformation(
        string name,
        string? description,
        string? version,
        DateTimeOffset updatedAt)
    {
        EnsureDraft();

        Name = NormalizeRequiredText(name, 200, "Protocol name");
        Description = NormalizeOptionalText(description, 4000, "Description");
        Version = NormalizeOptionalText(version, 50, "Version");
        UpdatedAt = updatedAt;

        AddDomainEvent(new ProtocolUpdatedDomainEvent(Id, updatedAt));
    }

    public Guid AddStep(
        int sequence,
        string title,
        string instruction,
        ProtocolStepType type,
        bool isRequired,
        decimal? targetValue,
        string? targetUnit,
        decimal? minimumValue,
        decimal? maximumValue,
        string? notes,
        DateTimeOffset occurredAt)
    {
        EnsureDraft();

        if (_steps.Any(x => x.Sequence == sequence))
            throw new ProtocolDomainException(
                $"A step with sequence '{sequence}' already exists.");

        var step = new ProtocolStep(
            Id,
            sequence,
            title,
            instruction,
            type,
            isRequired,
            targetValue,
            targetUnit,
            minimumValue,
            maximumValue,
            notes);

        _steps.Add(step);
        _steps.Sort((left, right) => left.Sequence.CompareTo(right.Sequence));
        UpdatedAt = occurredAt;

        AddDomainEvent(new ProtocolStepAddedDomainEvent(
            Id,
            step.Id,
            step.Sequence,
            occurredAt));

        return step.Id;
    }

    public void UpdateStep(
        Guid stepId,
        int sequence,
        string title,
        string instruction,
        ProtocolStepType type,
        bool isRequired,
        decimal? targetValue,
        string? targetUnit,
        decimal? minimumValue,
        decimal? maximumValue,
        string? notes,
        DateTimeOffset occurredAt)
    {
        EnsureDraft();

        var step = GetStep(stepId);

        if (_steps.Any(x => x.Id != stepId && x.Sequence == sequence))
            throw new ProtocolDomainException(
                $"A step with sequence '{sequence}' already exists.");

        step.Update(
            sequence,
            title,
            instruction,
            type,
            isRequired,
            targetValue,
            targetUnit,
            minimumValue,
            maximumValue,
            notes);

        _steps.Sort((left, right) => left.Sequence.CompareTo(right.Sequence));
        UpdatedAt = occurredAt;

        AddDomainEvent(new ProtocolStepUpdatedDomainEvent(
            Id,
            step.Id,
            occurredAt));
    }

    public void RemoveStep(Guid stepId, DateTimeOffset occurredAt)
    {
        EnsureDraft();

        var step = GetStep(stepId);

        _steps.Remove(step);
        UpdatedAt = occurredAt;

        AddDomainEvent(new ProtocolStepRemovedDomainEvent(
            Id,
            stepId,
            occurredAt));
    }

    public void Activate(DateTimeOffset activatedAt)
    {
        if (Status != ProtocolStatus.Draft)
            throw new ProtocolDomainException(
                "Only draft protocols can be activated.");

        if (_steps.Count == 0)
            throw new ProtocolDomainException(
                "A protocol must contain at least one step before activation.");

        Status = ProtocolStatus.Active;
        ActivatedAt = activatedAt;
        UpdatedAt = activatedAt;

        AddDomainEvent(new ProtocolStatusChangedDomainEvent(
            Id,
            ProtocolStatus.Draft,
            ProtocolStatus.Active,
            activatedAt));
    }

    public void Deprecate(DateTimeOffset deprecatedAt)
    {
        if (Status != ProtocolStatus.Active)
            throw new ProtocolDomainException(
                "Only active protocols can be deprecated.");

        Status = ProtocolStatus.Deprecated;
        DeprecatedAt = deprecatedAt;
        UpdatedAt = deprecatedAt;

        AddDomainEvent(new ProtocolStatusChangedDomainEvent(
            Id,
            ProtocolStatus.Active,
            ProtocolStatus.Deprecated,
            deprecatedAt));
    }

    public void Archive(DateTimeOffset archivedAt)
    {
        if (Status != ProtocolStatus.Deprecated)
            throw new ProtocolDomainException(
                "Only deprecated protocols can be archived.");

        Status = ProtocolStatus.Archived;
        ArchivedAt = archivedAt;
        UpdatedAt = archivedAt;

        AddDomainEvent(new ProtocolStatusChangedDomainEvent(
            Id,
            ProtocolStatus.Deprecated,
            ProtocolStatus.Archived,
            archivedAt));
    }

    private ProtocolStep GetStep(Guid stepId)
    {
        if (stepId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new ProtocolDomainException("StepId is required.");

        return _steps.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Id == stepId)
            ?? throw new ProtocolDomainException(
                $"Protocol step '{stepId}' was not found.");
    }

    private void EnsureDraft()
    {
        if (Status != ProtocolStatus.Draft)
            throw new ProtocolDomainException(
                "Only draft protocols can be modified.");
    }

    private static string NormalizeCode(string? value)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
            throw new ProtocolDomainException("Protocol code is required.");

        var normalized = value.Trim().ToUpperInvariant();

        if (normalized.Length > 50)
            throw new ProtocolDomainException(
                "Protocol code cannot exceed 50 characters.");

        if (normalized.Any(c =>
                !char.IsAsciiLetterOrDigit(c) &&
                c != '-' &&
                c != '_'))
        {
            throw new ProtocolDomainException(
                "Protocol code can contain only letters, numbers, hyphens and underscores.");
        }

        return normalized;
    }

    private static string NormalizeRequiredText(
        string? value,
        int maxLength,
        string fieldName)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
            throw new ProtocolDomainException($"{fieldName} is required.");

        var normalized = value.Trim();

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new ProtocolDomainException(
                $"{fieldName} cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }

    private static string? NormalizeOptionalText(
        string? value,
        int maxLength,
        string fieldName)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
            return null;

        var normalized = value.Trim();

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new ProtocolDomainException(
                $"{fieldName} cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Protocols\ProtocolStep.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;
using LIMS.Domain.Protocols.Enums;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Protocols;

public sealed class ProtocolStep : Entity<Guid>
{
    public Guid ProtocolId { get; private set; }

    public int Sequence { get; private set; }

    public string Title { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string Instruction { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public ProtocolStepType Type { get; private set; }

    public bool IsRequired { get; private set; }

    public decimal? TargetValue { get; private set; }

    public string? TargetUnit { get; private set; }

    public decimal? MinimumValue { get; private set; }

    public decimal? MaximumValue { get; private set; }

    public string? Notes { get; private set; }

    private ProtocolStep()
    {
    }

    internal ProtocolStep(
        Guid protocolId,
        int sequence,
        string title,
        string instruction,
        ProtocolStepType type,
        bool isRequired,
        decimal? targetValue,
        string? targetUnit,
        decimal? minimumValue,
        decimal? maximumValue,
        string? notes)
        : base(Guid.NewGuid())
    {
        if (protocolId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new ProtocolDomainException("ProtocolId is required.");

        ProtocolId = protocolId;
        Update(
            sequence,
            title,
            instruction,
            type,
            isRequired,
            targetValue,
            targetUnit,
            minimumValue,
            maximumValue,
            notes);
    }

    internal void Update(
        int sequence,
        string title,
        string instruction,
        ProtocolStepType type,
        bool isRequired,
        decimal? targetValue,
        string? targetUnit,
        decimal? minimumValue,
        decimal? maximumValue,
        string? notes)
    {
        if (sequence <= 0)
            throw new ProtocolDomainException("Step sequence must be greater than zero.");

        Title = NormalizeRequiredText(title, 200, "Step title");
        Instruction = NormalizeRequiredText(instruction, 4000, "Step instruction");

        if (!Enum.IsDefined(type))
            throw new ProtocolDomainException("Invalid protocol step type.");

        if (minimumValue.HasValue &&
            maximumValue.HasValue &&
            minimumValue.Value > maximumValue.Value)
        {
            throw new ProtocolDomainException(
                "Minimum value cannot be greater than maximum value.");
        }

        if (targetValue.HasValue &&
            minimumValue.HasValue &&
            targetValue.Value < minimumValue.Value)
        {
            throw new ProtocolDomainException(
                "Target value cannot be lower than minimum value.");
        }

        if (targetValue.HasValue &&
            maximumValue.HasValue &&
            targetValue.Value > maximumValue.Value)
        {
            throw new ProtocolDomainException(
                "Target value cannot be greater than maximum value.");
        }

        Sequence = sequence;
        Type = type;
        IsRequired = isRequired;
        TargetValue = targetValue;
        TargetUnit = NormalizeOptionalText(targetUnit, 50, "Target unit");
        MinimumValue = minimumValue;
        MaximumValue = maximumValue;
        Notes = NormalizeOptionalText(notes, 1000, "Step notes");
    }

    private static string NormalizeRequiredText(
        string? value,
        int maxLength,
        string fieldName)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
            throw new ProtocolDomainException($"{fieldName} is required.");

        var normalized = value.Trim();

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new ProtocolDomainException(
                $"{fieldName} cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }

    private static string? NormalizeOptionalText(
        string? value,
        int maxLength,
        string fieldName)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
            return null;

        var normalized = value.Trim();

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new ProtocolDomainException(
                $"{fieldName} cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Requests\Repositories\ISampleRequestRepository.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Persistence;
using LIMS.Domain.Requests;
using LIMS.Domain.Requests.Enums;

namespace LIMS.Application.Requests.Repositories;

public interface ISampleRequestRepository
    : IRepository<SampleRequest, Guid>
{
    Task<bool> ExistsByCodeAsync(
        string requestCode,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<SampleRequest?> GetByCodeAsync(
        string requestCode,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<SampleRequest?> GetWithDetailsAsync(
        Guid requestId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<IReadOnlyCollection<SampleRequest>> GetByRequesterAsync(
        string requestedByUserId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<IReadOnlyCollection<SampleRequest>> GetByLaboratoryIdAsync(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<IReadOnlyCollection<SampleRequest>> GetByStatusAsync(
        SampleRequestStatus status,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

    Task<IReadOnlyCollection<SampleRequest>> GetPendingReviewAsync(
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Requests\SampleRequest.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;
using LIMS.Domain.Requests.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Requests.Events;
using LIMS.Domain.Requests.Exceptions;
using LIMS.Domain.Samples.Enums;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Requests;

public sealed class SampleRequest : AggregateRoot<Guid>
{
    public string RequestCode { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string Title { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string? Description { get; private set; }

    public string SampleName { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string? SampleDescription { get; private set; }

    public SampleType RequestedSampleType { get; private set; }

    public string? SampleSource { get; private set; }

    public string RequestedByUserId { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string? AssignedReviewerUserId { get; private set; }

    public Guid? LaboratoryId { get; private set; }

    public Guid? CreatedSampleId { get; private set; }

    public SampleRequestPriority Priority { get; private set; }

    public SampleRequestStatus Status { get; private set; }

    public string? ReviewComments { get; private set; }

    public string? RejectionReason { get; private set; }

    public string? CancellationReason { get; private set; }

    public DateTime CreatedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTime UpdatedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTime? SubmittedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTime? ReviewStartedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTime? ApprovedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTime? RejectedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTime? CancelledAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTime? FulfilledAt { get; private set; }

    private SampleRequest()
    {
    }

    private SampleRequest(
        Guid id,
        string requestCode,
        string title,
        string? description,
        string sampleName,
        string? sampleDescription,
        SampleType requestedSampleType,
        string? sampleSource,
        string requestedByUserId,
        SampleRequestPriority priority,
        DateTime createdAt)
        : base(id)
    {
        RequestCode = NormalizeRequired(requestCode, nameof(requestCode), 50)
            .ToUpperInvariant();

        ValidateCode(RequestCode);

        Title = NormalizeRequired(title, nameof(title), 200);
        Description = NormalizeOptional(description, 4000);
        SampleName = NormalizeRequired(sampleName, nameof(sampleName), 200);
        SampleDescription = NormalizeOptional(sampleDescription, 2000);
        SampleSource = NormalizeOptional(sampleSource, 250);
        RequestedByUserId = NormalizeRequired(
            requestedByUserId,
            nameof(requestedByUserId),
            150);

        if (!Enum.IsDefined(requestedSampleType))
            throw new SampleRequestDomainException("Requested sample type is invalid.");

        if (!Enum.IsDefined(priority))
            throw new SampleRequestDomainException("Request priority is invalid.");

        RequestedSampleType = requestedSampleType;
        Priority = priority;
        Status = SampleRequestStatus.Draft;
        CreatedAt = createdAt;
        UpdatedAt = createdAt;
    }

    public static SampleRequest Create(
        string requestCode,
        string title,
        string? description,
        string sampleName,
        string? sampleDescription,
        SampleType requestedSampleType,
        string? sampleSource,
        string requestedByUserId,
        SampleRequestPriority priority,
        DateTime createdAt)
    {
        var request = new SampleRequest(
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            requestCode,
            title,
            description,
            sampleName,
            sampleDescription,
            requestedSampleType,
            sampleSource,
            requestedByUserId,
            priority,
            createdAt);

        request.AddDomainEvent(
            new SampleRequestCreatedDomainEvent(
                request.Id,
                request.RequestCode,
                request.RequestedByUserId,
                createdAt));

        return request;
    }

    public void UpdateInformation(
        string title,
        string? description,
        string sampleName,
        string? sampleDescription,
        SampleType requestedSampleType,
        string? sampleSource,
        SampleRequestPriority priority,
        DateTime updatedAt)
    {
        EnsureDraft();

        Title = NormalizeRequired(title, nameof(title), 200);
        Description = NormalizeOptional(description, 4000);
        SampleName = NormalizeRequired(sampleName, nameof(sampleName), 200);
        SampleDescription = NormalizeOptional(sampleDescription, 2000);
        SampleSource = NormalizeOptional(sampleSource, 250);

        if (!Enum.IsDefined(requestedSampleType))
            throw new SampleRequestDomainException("Requested sample type is invalid.");

        if (!Enum.IsDefined(priority))
            throw new SampleRequestDomainException("Request priority is invalid.");

        RequestedSampleType = requestedSampleType;
        Priority = priority;
        UpdatedAt = updatedAt;
    }

    public void Submit(
        string submittedByUserId,
        DateTime submittedAt)
    {
        EnsureDraft();

        var userId = NormalizeRequired(
            submittedByUserId,
            nameof(submittedByUserId),
            150);

        if (!string.Equals(
                userId,
                RequestedByUserId,
                StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            throw new SampleRequestDomainException(
                "Only the request creator can submit this request.");
        }

        Status = SampleRequestStatus.Submitted;
        SubmittedAt = submittedAt;
        UpdatedAt = submittedAt;

        AddDomainEvent(
            new SampleRequestSubmittedDomainEvent(
                Id,
                userId,
                submittedAt,
                submittedAt));
    }

    public void StartReview(
        string reviewerUserId,
        DateTime reviewedAt)
    {
        EnsureStatus(SampleRequestStatus.Submitted);

        var reviewer = NormalizeRequired(
            reviewerUserId,
            nameof(reviewerUserId),
            150);

        if (string.Equals(
                reviewer,
                RequestedByUserId,
                StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            throw new SampleRequestDomainException(
                "The request creator cannot review their own request.");
        }

        AssignedReviewerUserId = reviewer;
        ReviewStartedAt = reviewedAt;
        Status = SampleRequestStatus.UnderReview;
        UpdatedAt = reviewedAt;

        AddDomainEvent(
            new SampleRequestReviewStartedDomainEvent(
                Id,
                reviewer,
                reviewedAt,
                reviewedAt));
    }

    public void Approve(
        string approvedByUserId,
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string? comments,
        DateTime approvedAt)
    {
        EnsureStatus(SampleRequestStatus.UnderReview);

        var approver = NormalizeRequired(
            approvedByUserId,
            nameof(approvedByUserId),
            150);

        EnsureAssignedReviewer(approver);

        if (laboratoryId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new SampleRequestDomainException("Laboratory ID is required.");

        LaboratoryId = laboratoryId;
        ReviewComments = NormalizeOptional(comments, 2000);
        ApprovedAt = approvedAt;
        Status = SampleRequestStatus.Approved;
        UpdatedAt = approvedAt;

        AddDomainEvent(
            new SampleRequestApprovedDomainEvent(
                Id,
                approver,
                laboratoryId,
                approvedAt,
                approvedAt));
    }

    public void Reject(
        string rejectedByUserId,
        string reason,
        DateTime rejectedAt)
    {
        EnsureStatus(SampleRequestStatus.UnderReview);

        var reviewer = NormalizeRequired(
            rejectedByUserId,
            nameof(rejectedByUserId),
            150);

        EnsureAssignedReviewer(reviewer);

        RejectionReason = NormalizeRequired(reason, nameof(reason), 2000);
        RejectedAt = rejectedAt;
        Status = SampleRequestStatus.Rejected;
        UpdatedAt = rejectedAt;

        AddDomainEvent(
            new SampleRequestRejectedDomainEvent(
                Id,
                reviewer,
                RejectionReason,
                rejectedAt,
                rejectedAt));
    }

    public void Cancel(
        string cancelledByUserId,
        string? reason,
        DateTime cancelledAt)
    {
        var userId = NormalizeRequired(
            cancelledByUserId,
            nameof(cancelledByUserId),
            150);

        if (Status is SampleRequestStatus.Cancelled
            or SampleRequestStatus.Fulfilled)
        {
            throw new SampleRequestDomainException(
                "A cancelled or fulfilled request cannot be cancelled.");
        }

        if (Status == SampleRequestStatus.Approved)
        {
            throw new SampleRequestDomainException(
                "An approved request cannot be cancelled after laboratory assignment.");
        }

        if (!string.Equals(
                userId,
                RequestedByUserId,
                StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            throw new SampleRequestDomainException(
                "Only the request creator can cancel this request.");
        }

        CancellationReason = NormalizeOptional(reason, 2000);
        CancelledAt = cancelledAt;
        Status = SampleRequestStatus.Cancelled;
        UpdatedAt = cancelledAt;

        AddDomainEvent(
            new SampleRequestCancelledDomainEvent(
                Id,
                userId,
                CancellationReason,
                cancelledAt,
                cancelledAt));
    }

    public void Fulfill(
        Guid sampleId,
        DateTime fulfilledAt)
    {
        EnsureStatus(SampleRequestStatus.Approved);

        if (sampleId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new SampleRequestDomainException("Sample ID is required.");

        CreatedSampleId = sampleId;
        FulfilledAt = fulfilledAt;
        Status = SampleRequestStatus.Fulfilled;
        UpdatedAt = fulfilledAt;

        AddDomainEvent(
            new SampleRequestFulfilledDomainEvent(
                Id,
                sampleId,
                fulfilledAt,
                fulfilledAt));
    }

    private void EnsureDraft()
    {
        EnsureStatus(SampleRequestStatus.Draft);
    }

    private void EnsureStatus(SampleRequestStatus expectedStatus)
    {
        if (Status != expectedStatus)
        {
            throw new SampleRequestDomainException(
                $"Operation requires request status {expectedStatus}. Current status is {Status}.");
        }
    }

    private void EnsureAssignedReviewer(string userId)
    {
        if (!string.Equals(
                AssignedReviewerUserId,
                userId,
                StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            throw new SampleRequestDomainException(
                "Only the assigned reviewer can perform this operation.");
        }
    }

    private static string NormalizeRequired(
        string? value,
        string fieldName,
        int maxLength)
    {
        var normalized = value?.Trim();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalized))
            throw new SampleRequestDomainException($"{fieldName} is required.");

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
        {
            throw new SampleRequestDomainException(
                $"{fieldName} cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");
        }

        return normalized;
    }

    private static string? NormalizeOptional(
        string? value,
        int maxLength)
    {
        var normalized = value?.Trim();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalized))
            return null;

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
        {
            throw new SampleRequestDomainException(
                $"Value cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");
        }

        return normalized;
    }

    private static void ValidateCode(string code)
    {
        foreach (var character in code)
        {
            if (!char.IsAsciiLetterOrDigit(character)
                && character != '-'
                && character != '_')
            {
                throw new SampleRequestDomainException(
                    "Request code can contain only letters, numbers, hyphens and underscores.");
            }
        }
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Results\AnalysisResult.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;
using LIMS.Domain.Results.Entities;
using LIMS.Domain.Results.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Results.Events;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Results;

public sealed class AnalysisResult : AggregateRoot<Guid>
{
    private readonly List<ResultEntry> _entries = [];

    public Guid LaboratoryId { get; private set; }
    public Guid ExperimentId { get; private set; }
    public Guid SampleId { get; private set; }
    public Guid? InstrumentId { get; private set; }

    public string ResultCode { get; private set; } = string.Empty;
    public string Title { get; private set; } = string.Empty;
    public string? Description { get; private set; }

    public ResultStatus Status { get; private set; }

    public string CreatedByUserId { get; private set; } = string.Empty;
    public string? SubmittedByUserId { get; private set; }
    public string? ReviewedByUserId { get; private set; }
    public string? ReviewComments { get; private set; }
    public string? RejectionReason { get; private set; }
    public string? ArchivedByUserId { get; private set; }

    public DateTimeOffset CreatedAt { get; private set; }
    public DateTimeOffset UpdatedAt { get; private set; }
    public DateTimeOffset? SubmittedAt { get; private set; }
    public DateTimeOffset? ReviewedAt { get; private set; }
    public DateTimeOffset? ArchivedAt { get; private set; }

    public IReadOnlyCollection<ResultEntry> Entries => _entries.AsReadOnly();

    private AnalysisResult()
    {
    }

    private AnalysisResult(
        Guid id,
        Guid laboratoryId,
        Guid experimentId,
        Guid sampleId,
        Guid? instrumentId,
        string resultCode,
        string title,
        string? description,
        string createdByUserId,
        DateTimeOffset createdAt) : base(id)
    {
        LaboratoryId = laboratoryId;
        ExperimentId = experimentId;
        SampleId = sampleId;
        InstrumentId = instrumentId;
        ResultCode = resultCode;
        Title = title;
        Description = description;
        CreatedByUserId = createdByUserId;
        CreatedAt = createdAt;
        UpdatedAt = createdAt;
        Status = ResultStatus.Draft;
    }

    public static AnalysisResult Create(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        Guid experimentId,
        Guid sampleId,
        Guid? instrumentId,
        string resultCode,
        string title,
        string? description,
        string createdByUserId,
        DateTimeOffset createdAt)
    {
        if (laboratoryId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException("LaboratoryId is required.");

        if (experimentId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException("ExperimentId is required.");

        if (sampleId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException("SampleId is required.");

        if (instrumentId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException("InstrumentId cannot be empty.");

        resultCode = NormalizeRequired(resultCode, 50, nameof(resultCode));
        if (!IsValidCode(resultCode))
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                "Result code contains invalid characters.");

        title = NormalizeRequired(title, 200, nameof(title));
        description = NormalizeOptional(description, 4000);
        createdByUserId = NormalizeRequired(
            createdByUserId,
            150,
            nameof(createdByUserId));

        var result = new AnalysisResult(
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            laboratoryId,
            experimentId,
            sampleId,
            instrumentId,
            resultCode,
            title,
            description,
            createdByUserId,
            createdAt);

        result.AddDomainEvent(new AnalysisResultCreatedDomainEvent(
            result.Id,
            laboratoryId,
            experimentId,
            sampleId,
            resultCode,
            createdAt));

        return result;
    }

    public void UpdateInformation(
    string title,
    string? description,
    Guid? instrumentId,
    DateTimeOffset updatedAt)
    {
        EnsureEditable();

        if (instrumentId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                "InstrumentId cannot be empty.");

        Title = NormalizeRequired(title, 200, nameof(title));
        Description = NormalizeOptional(description, 4000);
        InstrumentId = instrumentId;
        UpdatedAt = updatedAt;
    }

    public Guid AddEntry(
        string parameterName,
        ResultValueType valueType,
        string? value,
        decimal? numericValue,
        string? unit,
        decimal? uncertainty,
        string? method,
        string? notes,
        DateTimeOffset occurredOn)
    {
        EnsureEditable();

        parameterName = NormalizeRequired(
            parameterName,
            150,
            nameof(parameterName));

        EnsureUniqueParameter(parameterName);

        var entry = new ResultEntry(
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            Id,
            parameterName,
            valueType,
            value,
            numericValue,
            unit,
            uncertainty,
            method,
            notes);

        _entries.Add(entry);
        UpdatedAt = occurredOn;

        AddDomainEvent(new ResultEntryAddedDomainEvent(
            Id,
            entry.Id,
            entry.ParameterName,
            occurredOn));

        return entry.Id;
    }

    public void UpdateEntry(
        Guid entryId,
        string parameterName,
        ResultValueType valueType,
        string? value,
        decimal? numericValue,
        string? unit,
        decimal? uncertainty,
        string? method,
        string? notes,
        DateTimeOffset occurredOn)
    {
        EnsureEditable();

        var entry = _entries.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Id == entryId);

        if (entry is null)
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException("Result entry was not found.");

        parameterName = NormalizeRequired(
            parameterName,
            150,
            nameof(parameterName));

        EnsureUniqueParameter(parameterName, entryId);

        entry.Update(
            parameterName,
            valueType,
            value,
            numericValue,
            unit,
            uncertainty,
            method,
            notes);

        UpdatedAt = occurredOn;

        AddDomainEvent(new ResultEntryUpdatedDomainEvent(
            Id,
            entry.Id,
            occurredOn));
    }

    public void RemoveEntry(Guid entryId, DateTimeOffset occurredOn)
    {
        EnsureEditable();

        var entry = _entries.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Id == entryId);

        if (entry is null)
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException("Result entry was not found.");

        _entries.Remove(entry);
        UpdatedAt = occurredOn;

        AddDomainEvent(new ResultEntryRemovedDomainEvent(
            Id,
            entry.Id,
            entry.ParameterName,
            occurredOn));
    }

    public void Submit(string submittedByUserId, DateTimeOffset submittedAt)
    {
        EnsureStatus(ResultStatus.Draft);

        if (_entries.Count == 0)
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                "At least one result entry is required before submission.");

        submittedByUserId = NormalizeRequired(
            submittedByUserId,
            150,
            nameof(submittedByUserId));

        SubmittedByUserId = submittedByUserId;
        SubmittedAt = submittedAt;
        Status = ResultStatus.Submitted;
        UpdatedAt = submittedAt;

        AddDomainEvent(new AnalysisResultSubmittedDomainEvent(
            Id,
            submittedByUserId,
            submittedAt,
            submittedAt));
    }

    public void StartReview(
        string reviewerUserId,
        DateTimeOffset startedAt)
    {
        EnsureStatus(ResultStatus.Submitted);

        reviewerUserId = NormalizeRequired(
            reviewerUserId,
            150,
            nameof(reviewerUserId));

        if (string.Equals(
            reviewerUserId,
            SubmittedByUserId,
            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                "The submitter cannot review their own result.");
        }

        ReviewedByUserId = reviewerUserId;
        Status = ResultStatus.UnderReview;
        UpdatedAt = startedAt;

        AddDomainEvent(new AnalysisResultReviewStartedDomainEvent(
            Id,
            reviewerUserId,
            startedAt,
            startedAt));
    }

    public void Approve(
        string reviewerUserId,
        string? comments,
        DateTimeOffset approvedAt)
    {
        EnsureStatus(ResultStatus.UnderReview);
        EnsureAssignedReviewer(reviewerUserId);

        ReviewComments = NormalizeOptional(comments, 2000);
        RejectionReason = null;
        ReviewedAt = approvedAt;
        Status = ResultStatus.Approved;
        UpdatedAt = approvedAt;

        AddDomainEvent(new AnalysisResultApprovedDomainEvent(
            Id,
            reviewerUserId,
            ReviewComments,
            approvedAt,
            approvedAt));
    }

    public void Reject(
        string reviewerUserId,
        string reason,
        DateTimeOffset rejectedAt)
    {
        EnsureStatus(ResultStatus.UnderReview);
        EnsureAssignedReviewer(reviewerUserId);

        RejectionReason = NormalizeRequired(
            reason,
            2000,
            nameof(reason));

        ReviewComments = null;
        ReviewedAt = rejectedAt;
        Status = ResultStatus.Rejected;
        UpdatedAt = rejectedAt;

        AddDomainEvent(new AnalysisResultRejectedDomainEvent(
            Id,
            reviewerUserId,
            RejectionReason,
            rejectedAt,
            rejectedAt));
    }

    public void ReturnToDraft(
        string requestedByUserId,
        DateTimeOffset returnedAt)
    {
        EnsureStatus(ResultStatus.Rejected);

        requestedByUserId = NormalizeRequired(
            requestedByUserId,
            150,
            nameof(requestedByUserId));

        if (!string.Equals(
            requestedByUserId,
            CreatedByUserId,
            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                "Only the result creator can return a rejected result to draft.");
        }

        Status = ResultStatus.Draft;
        SubmittedByUserId = null;
        SubmittedAt = null;
        ReviewedByUserId = null;
        ReviewedAt = null;
        ReviewComments = null;
        RejectionReason = null;
        UpdatedAt = returnedAt;
    }

    public void Archive(
        string archivedByUserId,
        DateTimeOffset archivedAt)
    {
        if (Status is not ResultStatus.Approved and not ResultStatus.Rejected)
        {
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                "Only approved or rejected results can be archived.");
        }

        archivedByUserId = NormalizeRequired(
            archivedByUserId,
            150,
            nameof(archivedByUserId));

        ArchivedByUserId = archivedByUserId;
        ArchivedAt = archivedAt;
        Status = ResultStatus.Archived;
        UpdatedAt = archivedAt;

        AddDomainEvent(new AnalysisResultArchivedDomainEvent(
            Id,
            archivedByUserId,
            archivedAt,
            archivedAt));
    }

    private void EnsureEditable()
    {
        EnsureStatus(ResultStatus.Draft);
    }

    private void EnsureStatus(ResultStatus expectedStatus)
    {
        if (Status != expectedStatus)
        {
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                $"Operation is not allowed while result status is {Status}.");
        }
    }

    private void EnsureAssignedReviewer(string reviewerUserId)
    {
        reviewerUserId = NormalizeRequired(
            reviewerUserId,
            150,
            nameof(reviewerUserId));

        if (!string.Equals(
            reviewerUserId,
            ReviewedByUserId,
            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                "Only the assigned reviewer can complete the review.");
        }
    }

    private void EnsureUniqueParameter(
        string parameterName,
        Guid? excludedEntryId = null)
    {
        var exists = _entries.Any(x =>
            x.Id != excludedEntryId &&
            string.Equals(
                x.ParameterName,
                parameterName,
                StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));

        if (exists)
        {
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                "A result entry with this parameter name already exists.");
        }
    }

    private static string NormalizeRequired(
        string? value,
        int maxLength,
        string parameterName)
    {
        var normalized = value?.Trim();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalized))
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                $"{parameterName} is required.");

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                $"{parameterName} cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }

    private static string? NormalizeOptional(
        string? value,
        int maxLength)
    {
        var normalized = value?.Trim();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalized))
            return null;

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                $"Value cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }

    private static bool IsValidCode(string value)
    {
        return value.All(c =>
            c is >= 'A' and <= 'Z' ||
            c is >= '0' and <= '9' ||
            c is '-' or '_');
    }
}

FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Results\AnalysisResultDomainException.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Results;

public sealed class AnalysisResultDomainException : DomainException
{
    public AnalysisResultDomainException(string message)
        : base(message)
    {
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Results\Entities\ResultEntry.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;
using LIMS.Domain.Results.Enums;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Results;

public sealed class ResultEntry : Entity<Guid>
{
    public Guid ResultId { get; private set; }
    public string ParameterName { get; private set; } = string.Empty;
    public ResultValueType ValueType { get; private set; }
    public string? Value { get; private set; }
    public decimal? NumericValue { get; private set; }
    public string? Unit { get; private set; }
    public decimal? Uncertainty { get; private set; }
    public string? Method { get; private set; }
    public string? Notes { get; private set; }

    private ResultEntry()
    {
    }

    internal ResultEntry(
        Guid id,
        Guid resultId,
        string parameterName,
        ResultValueType valueType,
        string? value,
        decimal? numericValue,
        string? unit,
        decimal? uncertainty,
        string? method,
        string? notes) : base(id)
    {
        if (resultId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException("ResultId is required.");

        ResultId = resultId;
        Apply(
            parameterName,
            valueType,
            value,
            numericValue,
            unit,
            uncertainty,
            method,
            notes);
    }

    internal void Update(
        string parameterName,
        ResultValueType valueType,
        string? value,
        decimal? numericValue,
        string? unit,
        decimal? uncertainty,
        string? method,
        string? notes)
    {
        Apply(
            parameterName,
            valueType,
            value,
            numericValue,
            unit,
            uncertainty,
            method,
            notes);
    }

    private void Apply(
        string parameterName,
        ResultValueType valueType,
        string? value,
        decimal? numericValue,
        string? unit,
        decimal? uncertainty,
        string? method,
        string? notes)
    {
        ParameterName = NormalizeRequired(
            parameterName,
            150,
            nameof(parameterName));

        if (!Enum.IsDefined(valueType))
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException("Invalid result value type.");

        if (uncertainty < 0)
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                "Uncertainty cannot be negative.");

        ValueType = valueType;
        Value = NormalizeOptional(value, 4000);
        NumericValue = numericValue;
        Unit = NormalizeOptional(unit, 50);
        Uncertainty = uncertainty;
        Method = NormalizeOptional(method, 200);
        Notes = NormalizeOptional(notes, 2000);

        ValidateValue();
    }

    private void ValidateValue()
    {
        switch (ValueType)
        {
            case ResultValueType.Numeric:
                if (!NumericValue.HasValue)
                    throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                        "NumericValue is required for numeric results.");
                break;

            case ResultValueType.Text:
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Value))
                    throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                        "Value is required for text results.");

                if (NumericValue.HasValue)
                    throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                        "NumericValue is not allowed for text results.");
                break;

            case ResultValueType.Boolean:
                if (!string.Equals(Value, "true", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                    !string.Equals(Value, "false", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                        "Boolean values must be true or false.");
                }

                if (NumericValue.HasValue)
                    throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                        "NumericValue is not allowed for boolean results.");
                break;

            case ResultValueType.Date:
                if (!DateOnly.TryParse(Value, out _))
                    throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                        "A valid date value is required.");

                if (NumericValue.HasValue)
                    throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                        "NumericValue is not allowed for date results.");
                break;

            default:
                throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                    "Unsupported result value type.");
        }
    }

    private static string NormalizeRequired(
        string? value,
        int maxLength,
        string parameterName)
    {
        var normalized = value?.Trim();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalized))
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                $"{parameterName} is required.");

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                $"{parameterName} cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }

    private static string? NormalizeOptional(string? value, int maxLength)
    {
        var normalized = value?.Trim();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalized))
            return null;

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new AnalysisResultDomainException(
                $"Value cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }
}
