====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Samples\Sample.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;
using LIMS.Domain.Samples.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Samples.Events;
using LIMS.Domain.Samples.Exceptions;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Samples;

public sealed class Sample : AggregateRoot<Guid>
{
    public Guid LaboratoryId { get; private set; }

    public string SampleCode { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string Name { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string? Description { get; private set; }

    public SampleType Type { get; private set; }

    public SampleStatus Status { get; private set; }

    public SamplePriority Priority { get; private set; }

    public string? Source { get; private set; }

    public string? AssignedToUserId { get; private set; }

    public string? RegisteredByUserId { get; private set; }

    public DateTimeOffset RegisteredAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTimeOffset? ReceivedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTimeOffset? UpdatedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTimeOffset? ArchivedAt { get; private set; }

    private Sample()
    {
    }

    public static Sample Register(
        Guid laboratoryId,
        string sampleCode,
        string name,
        SampleType type,
        SamplePriority priority,
        string? description,
        string? source,
        string? registeredByUserId,
        DateTimeOffset registeredAt)
    {
        if (laboratoryId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new SampleDomainException("Laboratory identifier is required.");

        if (!Enum.IsDefined(type))
            throw new SampleDomainException("Invalid sample type.");

        if (!Enum.IsDefined(priority))
            throw new SampleDomainException("Invalid sample priority.");

        var sample = new Sample
        {
            Id = Guid.NewGuid(),
            LaboratoryId = laboratoryId,
            SampleCode = NormalizeRequired(sampleCode, nameof(sampleCode), 50),
            Name = NormalizeRequired(name, nameof(name), 200),
            Type = type,
            Priority = priority,
            Description = NormalizeOptional(description, 2000),
            Source = NormalizeOptional(source, 250),
            RegisteredByUserId = NormalizeOptional(registeredByUserId, 150),
            Status = SampleStatus.Registered,
            RegisteredAt = registeredAt
        };

        sample.AddDomainEvent(
            new SampleRegisteredDomainEvent(
                sample.Id,
                sample.LaboratoryId,
                sample.SampleCode,
                registeredAt));

        return sample;
    }

    public void UpdateInformation(
        string name,
        SampleType type,
        SamplePriority priority,
        string? description,
        string? source,
        DateTimeOffset updatedAt)
    {
        EnsureNotArchived();

        if (Status is SampleStatus.UnderAnalysis or SampleStatus.AnalysisCompleted)
        {
            throw new SampleDomainException(
                "Sample information cannot be modified during or after analysis.");
        }

        if (!Enum.IsDefined(type))
            throw new SampleDomainException("Invalid sample type.");

        if (!Enum.IsDefined(priority))
            throw new SampleDomainException("Invalid sample priority.");

        Name = NormalizeRequired(name, nameof(name), 200);
        Type = type;
        Priority = priority;
        Description = NormalizeOptional(description, 2000);
        Source = NormalizeOptional(source, 250);
        UpdatedAt = updatedAt;
    }

    public void Receive(DateTimeOffset receivedAt)
    {
        EnsureNotArchived();

        if (Status != SampleStatus.Registered)
        {
            throw new SampleDomainException(
                "Only registered samples can be received.");
        }

        Status = SampleStatus.Received;
        ReceivedAt = receivedAt;
        UpdatedAt = receivedAt;

        AddStatusChangedEvent(
            SampleStatus.Registered,
            SampleStatus.Received,
            receivedAt);
    }

    public void StartPreparation(DateTimeOffset startedAt)
    {
        ChangeStatus(
            SampleStatus.Received,
            SampleStatus.InPreparation,
            startedAt);
    }

    public void MarkReadyForAnalysis(DateTimeOffset readyAt)
    {
        ChangeStatus(
            SampleStatus.InPreparation,
            SampleStatus.ReadyForAnalysis,
            readyAt);
    }

    public void StartAnalysis(DateTimeOffset startedAt)
    {
        ChangeStatus(
            SampleStatus.ReadyForAnalysis,
            SampleStatus.UnderAnalysis,
            startedAt);
    }

    public void CompleteAnalysis(DateTimeOffset completedAt)
    {
        ChangeStatus(
            SampleStatus.UnderAnalysis,
            SampleStatus.AnalysisCompleted,
            completedAt);
    }

    public void Reject(DateTimeOffset rejectedAt)
    {
        EnsureNotArchived();

        if (Status == SampleStatus.AnalysisCompleted)
        {
            throw new SampleDomainException(
                "A sample with completed analysis cannot be rejected.");
        }

        if (Status == SampleStatus.Rejected)
            return;

        var previousStatus = Status;

        Status = SampleStatus.Rejected;
        UpdatedAt = rejectedAt;

        AddStatusChangedEvent(
            previousStatus,
            SampleStatus.Rejected,
            rejectedAt);
    }

    public void AssignTo(
        string userId,
        DateTimeOffset assignedAt)
    {
        EnsureNotArchived();

        if (Status is SampleStatus.Rejected or SampleStatus.AnalysisCompleted)
        {
            throw new SampleDomainException(
                "This sample cannot be assigned in its current status.");
        }

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(userId))
            throw new SampleDomainException("User identifier is required.");

        var normalizedUserId = userId.Trim();

        if (string.Equals(
                AssignedToUserId,
                normalizedUserId,
                StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            return;
        }

        AssignedToUserId = normalizedUserId;
        UpdatedAt = assignedAt;

        AddDomainEvent(
            new SampleAssignedDomainEvent(
                Id,
                normalizedUserId,
                assignedAt));
    }

    public void Unassign(DateTimeOffset unassignedAt)
    {
        EnsureNotArchived();

        if (AssignedToUserId is null)
            return;

        AssignedToUserId = null;
        UpdatedAt = unassignedAt;
    }

    public void Archive(DateTimeOffset archivedAt)
    {
        if (Status == SampleStatus.Archived)
            return;

        if (Status is not SampleStatus.AnalysisCompleted
            and not SampleStatus.Rejected)
        {
            throw new SampleDomainException(
                "Only completed or rejected samples can be archived.");
        }

        var previousStatus = Status;

        Status = SampleStatus.Archived;
        ArchivedAt = archivedAt;
        UpdatedAt = archivedAt;

        AddStatusChangedEvent(
            previousStatus,
            SampleStatus.Archived,
            archivedAt);
    }

    private void ChangeStatus(
        SampleStatus expectedStatus,
        SampleStatus newStatus,
        DateTimeOffset changedAt)
    {
        EnsureNotArchived();

        if (Status != expectedStatus)
        {
            throw new SampleDomainException(
                $"Cannot change sample status from '{Status}' to '{newStatus}'.");
        }

        Status = newStatus;
        UpdatedAt = changedAt;

        AddStatusChangedEvent(
            expectedStatus,
            newStatus,
            changedAt);
    }

    private void AddStatusChangedEvent(
        SampleStatus previousStatus,
        SampleStatus newStatus,
        DateTimeOffset occurredOn)
    {
        AddDomainEvent(
            new SampleStatusChangedDomainEvent(
                Id,
                previousStatus,
                newStatus,
                occurredOn));
    }

    private void EnsureNotArchived()
    {
        if (Status == SampleStatus.Archived)
        {
            throw new SampleDomainException(
                "Archived samples cannot be modified.");
        }
    }

    private static string NormalizeRequired(
        string value,
        string fieldName,
        int maxLength)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
            throw new SampleDomainException($"{fieldName} is required.");

        var normalized = value.Trim();

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
        {
            throw new SampleDomainException(
                $"{fieldName} cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");
        }

        return normalized;
    }

    private static string? NormalizeOptional(
        string? value,
        int maxLength)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
            return null;

        var normalized = value.Trim();

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
        {
            throw new SampleDomainException(
                $"Value cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");
        }

        return normalized;
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Workflows\Entities\WorkflowStepDefinition.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;
using LIMS.Domain.Workflows.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Workflows.Exceptions;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Workflows.Entities;

public sealed class WorkflowStepDefinition : Entity<Guid>
{
    public Guid WorkflowDefinitionId { get; private set; }

    public int Sequence { get; private set; }

    public string Code { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string Name { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string? Description { get; private set; }

    public WorkflowStepType Type { get; private set; }

    public bool IsRequired { get; private set; }

    public bool AllowReassignment { get; private set; }

    public int? DueAfterHours { get; private set; }

    public string? RequiredRole { get; private set; }

    private WorkflowStepDefinition()
    {
    }

    internal WorkflowStepDefinition(
        Guid workflowDefinitionId,
        int sequence,
        string code,
        string name,
        string? description,
        WorkflowStepType type,
        bool isRequired,
        bool allowReassignment,
        int? dueAfterHours,
        string? requiredRole)
        : base(Guid.NewGuid())
    {
        if (workflowDefinitionId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException("Workflow definition ID is required.");

        if (sequence <= 0)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException("Step sequence must be greater than zero.");

        WorkflowDefinitionId = workflowDefinitionId;
        Sequence = sequence;
        Code = NormalizeRequired(code, nameof(code), 50).ToUpperInvariant();
        ValidateCode(Code);
        Name = NormalizeRequired(name, nameof(name), 150);
        Description = NormalizeOptional(description, 1000);

        if (!Enum.IsDefined(type))
            throw new WorkflowDomainException("Workflow step type is invalid.");

        if (dueAfterHours is <= 0)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException("Due-after hours must be greater than zero.");

        Type = type;
        IsRequired = isRequired;
        AllowReassignment = allowReassignment;
        DueAfterHours = dueAfterHours;
        RequiredRole = NormalizeOptional(requiredRole, 100);
    }

    internal void Update(
        int sequence,
        string code,
        string name,
        string? description,
        WorkflowStepType type,
        bool isRequired,
        bool allowReassignment,
        int? dueAfterHours,
        string? requiredRole)
    {
        if (sequence <= 0)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException("Step sequence must be greater than zero.");

        if (!Enum.IsDefined(type))
            throw new WorkflowDomainException("Workflow step type is invalid.");

        if (dueAfterHours is <= 0)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException("Due-after hours must be greater than zero.");

        Sequence = sequence;
        Code = NormalizeRequired(code, nameof(code), 50).ToUpperInvariant();
        ValidateCode(Code);
        Name = NormalizeRequired(name, nameof(name), 150);
        Description = NormalizeOptional(description, 1000);
        Type = type;
        IsRequired = isRequired;
        AllowReassignment = allowReassignment;
        DueAfterHours = dueAfterHours;
        RequiredRole = NormalizeOptional(requiredRole, 100);
    }

    private static string NormalizeRequired(
        string? value,
        string fieldName,
        int maxLength)
    {
        var normalized = value?.Trim();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalized))
            throw new WorkflowDomainException($"{fieldName} is required.");

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                $"{fieldName} cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }

    private static string? NormalizeOptional(string? value, int maxLength)
    {
        var normalized = value?.Trim();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalized))
            return null;

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                $"Value cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }

    private static void ValidateCode(string code)
    {
        if (code.Any(character =>
                !char.IsAsciiLetterOrDigit(character)
                && character != '-'
                && character != '_'))
        {
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                "Step code can contain only letters, numbers, hyphens and underscores.");
        }
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Workflows\Entities\WorkflowTask.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;
using LIMS.Domain.Workflows.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Workflows.Exceptions;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Workflows.Entities;

public sealed class WorkflowTask : Entity<Guid>
{
    public Guid WorkflowInstanceId { get; private set; }

    public Guid WorkflowStepDefinitionId { get; private set; }

    public int Sequence { get; private set; }

    public string Code { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string Name { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public WorkflowStepType Type { get; private set; }

    public bool IsRequired { get; private set; }

    public WorkflowTaskStatus Status { get; private set; }

    public string? AssignedToUserId { get; private set; }

    public string? CompletedByUserId { get; private set; }

    public string? RejectionReason { get; private set; }

    public string? CompletionComments { get; private set; }

    public DateTime CreatedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTime? AssignedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTime? StartedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTime? CompletedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTime? DueAt { get; private set; }

    private WorkflowTask()
    {
    }

    internal WorkflowTask(
        Guid workflowInstanceId,
        Guid workflowStepDefinitionId,
        int sequence,
        string code,
        string name,
        WorkflowStepType type,
        bool isRequired,
        DateTime createdAt,
        DateTime? dueAt)
        : base(Guid.NewGuid())
    {
        if (workflowInstanceId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException("Workflow instance ID is required.");

        if (workflowStepDefinitionId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException("Workflow step definition ID is required.");

        if (sequence <= 0)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException("Task sequence must be greater than zero.");

        if (!Enum.IsDefined(type))
            throw new WorkflowDomainException("Workflow task type is invalid.");

        WorkflowInstanceId = workflowInstanceId;
        WorkflowStepDefinitionId = workflowStepDefinitionId;
        Sequence = sequence;
        Code = NormalizeRequired(code, nameof(code), 50);
        Name = NormalizeRequired(name, nameof(name), 150);
        Type = type;
        IsRequired = isRequired;
        Status = WorkflowTaskStatus.Pending;
        CreatedAt = createdAt;
        DueAt = dueAt;
    }

    internal void Assign(
        string assignedToUserId,
        DateTime assignedAt)
    {
        if (Status is not WorkflowTaskStatus.Pending
            and not WorkflowTaskStatus.Assigned)
        {
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                "Only pending or assigned tasks can be assigned.");
        }

        AssignedToUserId = NormalizeRequired(
            assignedToUserId,
            nameof(assignedToUserId),
            150);

        AssignedAt = assignedAt;
        Status = WorkflowTaskStatus.Assigned;
    }

    internal void Start(
        string userId,
        DateTime startedAt)
    {
        EnsureAssignedUser(userId);

        if (Status != WorkflowTaskStatus.Assigned)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                "Only assigned tasks can be started.");

        StartedAt = startedAt;
        Status = WorkflowTaskStatus.InProgress;
    }

    internal void Complete(
        string userId,
        string? comments,
        DateTime completedAt)
    {
        EnsureAssignedUser(userId);

        if (Status is not WorkflowTaskStatus.Assigned
            and not WorkflowTaskStatus.InProgress)
        {
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                "Only assigned or in-progress tasks can be completed.");
        }

        CompletedByUserId = NormalizeRequired(
            userId,
            nameof(userId),
            150);

        CompletionComments = NormalizeOptional(comments, 2000);
        CompletedAt = completedAt;
        Status = WorkflowTaskStatus.Completed;
    }

    internal void Reject(
        string userId,
        string reason,
        DateTime rejectedAt)
    {
        EnsureAssignedUser(userId);

        if (Status is not WorkflowTaskStatus.Assigned
            and not WorkflowTaskStatus.InProgress)
        {
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                "Only assigned or in-progress tasks can be rejected.");
        }

        RejectionReason = NormalizeRequired(reason, nameof(reason), 2000);
        CompletedByUserId = NormalizeRequired(
            userId,
            nameof(userId),
            150);

        CompletedAt = rejectedAt;
        Status = WorkflowTaskStatus.Rejected;
    }

    internal void Skip(DateTime skippedAt)
    {
        if (IsRequired)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                "Required workflow tasks cannot be skipped.");

        if (Status is WorkflowTaskStatus.Completed
            or WorkflowTaskStatus.Rejected
            or WorkflowTaskStatus.Cancelled)
        {
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                "A terminal workflow task cannot be skipped.");
        }

        CompletedAt = skippedAt;
        Status = WorkflowTaskStatus.Skipped;
    }

    internal void Cancel(DateTime cancelledAt)
    {
        if (Status is WorkflowTaskStatus.Completed
            or WorkflowTaskStatus.Rejected
            or WorkflowTaskStatus.Skipped)
        {
            return;
        }

        CompletedAt = cancelledAt;
        Status = WorkflowTaskStatus.Cancelled;
    }

    private void EnsureAssignedUser(string userId)
    {
        var normalized = NormalizeRequired(userId, nameof(userId), 150);

        if (!string.Equals(
                AssignedToUserId,
                normalized,
                StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                "Only the assigned user can perform this task operation.");
        }
    }

    private static string NormalizeRequired(
        string? value,
        string fieldName,
        int maxLength)
    {
        var normalized = value?.Trim();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalized))
            throw new WorkflowDomainException($"{fieldName} is required.");

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                $"{fieldName} cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }

    private static string? NormalizeOptional(string? value, int maxLength)
    {
        var normalized = value?.Trim();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalized))
            return null;

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                $"Value cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }
}

====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Workflows.Exceptions;

public sealed class WorkflowDomainException : DomainException
{
    public WorkflowDomainException(string message)
        : base(message)
    {
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Workflows\WorkflowDefinition.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;
using LIMS.Domain.Workflows.Entities;
using LIMS.Domain.Workflows.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Workflows.Events;
using LIMS.Domain.Workflows.Exceptions;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Workflows;

public sealed class WorkflowDefinition : AggregateRoot<Guid>
{
    private readonly List<WorkflowStepDefinition> _steps = [];

    public string Code { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string Name { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string? Description { get; private set; }

    public string CreatedByUserId { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public WorkflowDefinitionStatus Status { get; private set; }

    public int Version { get; private set; }

    public DateTime CreatedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTime UpdatedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTime? ActivatedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTime? DeprecatedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTime? ArchivedAt { get; private set; }

    public IReadOnlyCollection<WorkflowStepDefinition> Steps => _steps.AsReadOnly();

    private WorkflowDefinition()
    {
    }

    private WorkflowDefinition(
        Guid id,
        string code,
        string name,
        string? description,
        string createdByUserId,
        int version,
        DateTime createdAt)
        : base(id)
    {
        Code = NormalizeRequired(code, nameof(code), 50).ToUpperInvariant();
        ValidateCode(Code);
        Name = NormalizeRequired(name, nameof(name), 200);
        Description = NormalizeOptional(description, 2000);
        CreatedByUserId = NormalizeRequired(
            createdByUserId,
            nameof(createdByUserId),
            150);

        if (version <= 0)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException("Workflow version must be greater than zero.");

        Version = version;
        Status = WorkflowDefinitionStatus.Draft;
        CreatedAt = createdAt;
        UpdatedAt = createdAt;
    }

    public static WorkflowDefinition Create(
        string code,
        string name,
        string? description,
        string createdByUserId,
        int version,
        DateTime createdAt)
    {
        var definition = new WorkflowDefinition(
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            code,
            name,
            description,
            createdByUserId,
            version,
            createdAt);

        definition.AddDomainEvent(
            new WorkflowDefinitionCreatedDomainEvent(
                definition.Id,
                definition.Code,
                definition.Name,
                createdAt));

        return definition;
    }

    public void UpdateInformation(
        string name,
        string? description,
        DateTime updatedAt)
    {
        EnsureDraft();

        Name = NormalizeRequired(name, nameof(name), 200);
        Description = NormalizeOptional(description, 2000);
        UpdatedAt = updatedAt;
    }

    public Guid AddStep(
        int sequence,
        string code,
        string name,
        string? description,
        WorkflowStepType type,
        bool isRequired,
        bool allowReassignment,
        int? dueAfterHours,
        string? requiredRole,
        DateTime updatedAt)
    {
        EnsureDraft();

        if (_steps.Any(x => x.Sequence == sequence))
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                $"A workflow step with sequence '{sequence}' already exists.");

        if (_steps.Any(x => string.Equals(
                x.Code,
                code?.Trim(),
                StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
        {
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                $"A workflow step with code '{code}' already exists.");
        }

        var step = new WorkflowStepDefinition(
            Id,
            sequence,
            code,
            name,
            description,
            type,
            isRequired,
            allowReassignment,
            dueAfterHours,
            requiredRole);

        _steps.Add(step);
        _steps.Sort((left, right) => left.Sequence.CompareTo(right.Sequence));
        UpdatedAt = updatedAt;

        return step.Id;
    }

    public void UpdateStep(
        Guid stepId,
        int sequence,
        string code,
        string name,
        string? description,
        WorkflowStepType type,
        bool isRequired,
        bool allowReassignment,
        int? dueAfterHours,
        string? requiredRole,
        DateTime updatedAt)
    {
        EnsureDraft();

        var step = FindStep(stepId);

        if (_steps.Any(x =>
                x.Id != stepId && x.Sequence == sequence))
        {
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                $"A workflow step with sequence '{sequence}' already exists.");
        }

        if (_steps.Any(x =>
                x.Id != stepId
                && string.Equals(
                    x.Code,
                    code?.Trim(),
                    StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
        {
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                $"A workflow step with code '{code}' already exists.");
        }

        step.Update(
            sequence,
            code,
            name,
            description,
            type,
            isRequired,
            allowReassignment,
            dueAfterHours,
            requiredRole);

        _steps.Sort((left, right) => left.Sequence.CompareTo(right.Sequence));
        UpdatedAt = updatedAt;
    }

    public void RemoveStep(Guid stepId, DateTime updatedAt)
    {
        EnsureDraft();

        var step = FindStep(stepId);
        _steps.Remove(step);
        UpdatedAt = updatedAt;
    }

    public void Activate(DateTime activatedAt)
    {
        EnsureDraft();

        if (_steps.Count == 0)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                "A workflow definition must contain at least one step before activation.");

        if (!_steps.Any(x => x.IsRequired))
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                "A workflow definition must contain at least one required step.");

        ActivatedAt = activatedAt;
        Status = WorkflowDefinitionStatus.Active;
        UpdatedAt = activatedAt;

        AddDomainEvent(
            new WorkflowDefinitionActivatedDomainEvent(
                Id,
                activatedAt,
                activatedAt));
    }

    public void Deprecate(DateTime deprecatedAt)
    {
        if (Status != WorkflowDefinitionStatus.Active)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                "Only active workflow definitions can be deprecated.");

        Status = WorkflowDefinitionStatus.Deprecated;
        DeprecatedAt = deprecatedAt;
        UpdatedAt = deprecatedAt;
    }

    public void Archive(DateTime archivedAt)
    {
        if (Status != WorkflowDefinitionStatus.Deprecated)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                "Only deprecated workflow definitions can be archived.");

        Status = WorkflowDefinitionStatus.Archived;
        ArchivedAt = archivedAt;
        UpdatedAt = archivedAt;
    }

    private WorkflowStepDefinition FindStep(Guid stepId)
    {
        if (stepId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException("Workflow step ID is required.");

        return _steps.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Id == stepId)
            ?? throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                $"Workflow step '{stepId}' was not found.");
    }

    private void EnsureDraft()
    {
        if (Status != WorkflowDefinitionStatus.Draft)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                "Only draft workflow definitions can be modified.");
    }

    private static string NormalizeRequired(
        string? value,
        string fieldName,
        int maxLength)
    {
        var normalized = value?.Trim();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalized))
            throw new WorkflowDomainException($"{fieldName} is required.");

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                $"{fieldName} cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }

    private static string? NormalizeOptional(string? value, int maxLength)
    {
        var normalized = value?.Trim();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalized))
            return null;

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                $"Value cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }

    private static void ValidateCode(string code)
    {
        if (code.Any(character =>
                !char.IsAsciiLetterOrDigit(character)
                && character != '-'
                && character != '_'))
        {
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                "Workflow code can contain only letters, numbers, hyphens and underscores.");
        }
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Domain\Workflows\WorkflowInstance.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;
using LIMS.Domain.Workflows.Entities;
using LIMS.Domain.Workflows.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Workflows.Events;
using LIMS.Domain.Workflows.Exceptions;

namespace LIMS.Domain.Workflows;

public sealed class WorkflowInstance : AggregateRoot<Guid>
{
    private readonly List<WorkflowTask> _tasks = [];

    public Guid WorkflowDefinitionId { get; private set; }

    public int WorkflowDefinitionVersion { get; private set; }

    public string WorkflowName { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public string ReferenceType { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public Guid ReferenceId { get; private set; }

    public string StartedByUserId { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public WorkflowInstanceStatus Status { get; private set; }

    public DateTime StartedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTime? CompletedAt { get; private set; }

    public DateTime? CancelledAt { get; private set; }

    public string? CancellationReason { get; private set; }

    public IReadOnlyCollection<WorkflowTask> Tasks => _tasks.AsReadOnly();

    private WorkflowInstance()
    {
    }

    private WorkflowInstance(
        Guid id,
        Guid workflowDefinitionId,
        int workflowDefinitionVersion,
        string workflowName,
        string referenceType,
        Guid referenceId,
        string startedByUserId,
        DateTime startedAt)
        : base(id)
    {
        if (workflowDefinitionId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException("Workflow definition ID is required.");

        if (workflowDefinitionVersion <= 0)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException("Workflow definition version is invalid.");

        if (referenceId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException("Workflow reference ID is required.");

        WorkflowDefinitionId = workflowDefinitionId;
        WorkflowDefinitionVersion = workflowDefinitionVersion;
        WorkflowName = NormalizeRequired(workflowName, nameof(workflowName), 200);
        ReferenceType = NormalizeRequired(referenceType, nameof(referenceType), 100);
        ReferenceId = referenceId;
        StartedByUserId = NormalizeRequired(
            startedByUserId,
            nameof(startedByUserId),
            150);

        StartedAt = startedAt;
        Status = WorkflowInstanceStatus.InProgress;
    }

    public static WorkflowInstance Start(
        WorkflowDefinition definition,
        string referenceType,
        Guid referenceId,
        string startedByUserId,
        DateTime startedAt)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(definition);

        if (definition.Status != WorkflowDefinitionStatus.Active)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                "Only active workflow definitions can be started.");

        if (definition.Steps.Count == 0)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                "Workflow definition has no steps.");

        var instance = new WorkflowInstance(
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            definition.Id,
            definition.Version,
            definition.Name,
            referenceType,
            referenceId,
            startedByUserId,
            startedAt);

        foreach (var step in definition.Steps.OrderBy(x => x.Sequence))
        {
            var dueAt = step.DueAfterHours.HasValue
                ? startedAt.AddHours(step.DueAfterHours.Value)
                : null;

            instance._tasks.Add(
                new WorkflowTask(
                    instance.Id,
                    step.Id,
                    step.Sequence,
                    step.Code,
                    step.Name,
                    step.Type,
                    step.IsRequired,
                    startedAt,
                    dueAt));
        }

        instance.AddDomainEvent(
            new WorkflowInstanceStartedDomainEvent(
                instance.Id,
                definition.Id,
                instance.StartedByUserId,
                startedAt,
                startedAt));

        return instance;
    }

    public void AssignTask(
        Guid taskId,
        string assignedToUserId,
        DateTime assignedAt)
    {
        EnsureInProgress();

        var task = FindTask(taskId);

        if (task.Status is WorkflowTaskStatus.Completed
            or WorkflowTaskStatus.Rejected
            or WorkflowTaskStatus.Skipped
            or WorkflowTaskStatus.Cancelled)
        {
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                "A terminal task cannot be reassigned.");
        }

        task.Assign(assignedToUserId, assignedAt);

        AddDomainEvent(
            new WorkflowTaskAssignedDomainEvent(
                Id,
                task.Id,
                assignedToUserId,
                assignedAt,
                assignedAt));
    }

    public void StartTask(
        Guid taskId,
        string userId,
        DateTime startedAt)
    {
        EnsureInProgress();

        var task = FindTask(taskId);
        EnsurePreviousRequiredTasksCompleted(task);

        task.Start(userId, startedAt);
    }

    public void CompleteTask(
        Guid taskId,
        string userId,
        string? comments,
        DateTime completedAt)
    {
        EnsureInProgress();

        var task = FindTask(taskId);
        EnsurePreviousRequiredTasksCompleted(task);

        task.Complete(userId, comments, completedAt);

        AddDomainEvent(
            new WorkflowTaskCompletedDomainEvent(
                Id,
                task.Id,
                userId,
                completedAt,
                completedAt));

        CompleteWorkflowIfFinished(completedAt);
    }

    public void RejectTask(
        Guid taskId,
        string userId,
        string reason,
        DateTime rejectedAt)
    {
        EnsureInProgress();

        var task = FindTask(taskId);
        EnsurePreviousRequiredTasksCompleted(task);

        task.Reject(userId, reason, rejectedAt);

        AddDomainEvent(
            new WorkflowTaskRejectedDomainEvent(
                Id,
                task.Id,
                userId,
                reason,
                rejectedAt,
                rejectedAt));

        Status = WorkflowInstanceStatus.Rejected;
        CancelRemainingTasks(rejectedAt);
    }

    public void SkipTask(Guid taskId, DateTime skippedAt)
    {
        EnsureInProgress();

        var task = FindTask(taskId);
        EnsurePreviousRequiredTasksCompleted(task);

        task.Skip(skippedAt);
        CompleteWorkflowIfFinished(skippedAt);
    }

    public void Cancel(
        string cancelledByUserId,
        string? reason,
        DateTime cancelledAt)
    {
        if (Status is WorkflowInstanceStatus.Completed
            or WorkflowInstanceStatus.Rejected
            or WorkflowInstanceStatus.Cancelled)
        {
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                "A terminal workflow instance cannot be cancelled.");
        }

        var userId = NormalizeRequired(
            cancelledByUserId,
            nameof(cancelledByUserId),
            150);

        CancellationReason = NormalizeOptional(reason, 2000);
        CancelledAt = cancelledAt;
        Status = WorkflowInstanceStatus.Cancelled;

        CancelRemainingTasks(cancelledAt);

        AddDomainEvent(
            new WorkflowInstanceCancelledDomainEvent(
                Id,
                userId,
                CancellationReason,
                cancelledAt,
                cancelledAt));
    }

    public void Suspend()
    {
        EnsureInProgress();
        Status = WorkflowInstanceStatus.Suspended;
    }

    public void Resume()
    {
        if (Status != WorkflowInstanceStatus.Suspended)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                "Only suspended workflows can be resumed.");

        Status = WorkflowInstanceStatus.InProgress;
    }

    private void CompleteWorkflowIfFinished(DateTime completedAt)
    {
        var requiredTasks = _tasks.Where(x => x.IsRequired);

        if (requiredTasks.Any(x =>
                x.Status != WorkflowTaskStatus.Completed
                && x.Status != WorkflowTaskStatus.Skipped))
        {
            return;
        }

        if (_tasks.Any(x =>
                x.Status is WorkflowTaskStatus.Pending
                    or WorkflowTaskStatus.Assigned
                    or WorkflowTaskStatus.InProgress))
        {
            return;
        }

        Status = WorkflowInstanceStatus.Completed;
        CompletedAt = completedAt;

        AddDomainEvent(
            new WorkflowInstanceCompletedDomainEvent(
                Id,
                completedAt,
                completedAt));
    }

    private void EnsurePreviousRequiredTasksCompleted(WorkflowTask task)
    {
        var previousRequiredTasks = _tasks.Where(x =>
            x.IsRequired && x.Sequence < task.Sequence);

        if (previousRequiredTasks.Any(x =>
                x.Status != WorkflowTaskStatus.Completed
                && x.Status != WorkflowTaskStatus.Skipped))
        {
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                "All previous required workflow tasks must be completed first.");
        }
    }

    private void CancelRemainingTasks(DateTime cancelledAt)
    {
        foreach (var task in _tasks)
            task.Cancel(cancelledAt);
    }

    private WorkflowTask FindTask(Guid taskId)
    {
        if (taskId == Guid.Empty)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException("Workflow task ID is required.");

        return _tasks.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Id == taskId)
            ?? throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                $"Workflow task '{taskId}' was not found.");
    }

    private void EnsureInProgress()
    {
        if (Status != WorkflowInstanceStatus.InProgress)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                "Workflow instance is not in progress.");
    }

    private static string NormalizeRequired(
        string? value,
        string fieldName,
        int maxLength)
    {
        var normalized = value?.Trim();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalized))
            throw new WorkflowDomainException($"{fieldName} is required.");

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                $"{fieldName} cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }

    private static string? NormalizeOptional(string? value, int maxLength)
    {
        var normalized = value?.Trim();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalized))
            return null;

        if (normalized.Length > maxLength)
            throw new WorkflowDomainException(
                $"Value cannot exceed {maxLength} characters.");

        return normalized;
    }
}

using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Identity;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Security;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Time;
using LIMS.Infrastructure.Identity.Authorization;
using LIMS.Infrastructure.Identity.Users;
using LIMS.Infrastructure.Time;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;

namespace LIMS.Infrastructure;

public static class DependencyInjection
{
    public static IServiceCollection AddInfrastructure(
        this IServiceCollection services)
    {
        services.AddHttpContextAccessor();

        services.AddScoped<ICurrentUser, CurrentUser>();
        services.AddScoped<IPermissionChecker, PermissionChecker>();

        services.AddSingleton<IDateTimeProvider, SystemDateTimeProvider>();

        return services;
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Infrastructure\Identity\Authentication\PermissionChecker.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Identity;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Security;

namespace LIMS.Infrastructure.Identity.Authorization;

public sealed class PermissionChecker : IPermissionChecker
{
    private readonly ICurrentUser _currentUser;

    public PermissionChecker(ICurrentUser currentUser)
    {
        _currentUser = currentUser;
    }

    public Task<bool> HasPermissionAsync(
        string permission,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        ArgumentException.ThrowIfNullOrWhiteSpace(permission);
        cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();

        var result = _currentUser.IsAuthenticated &&
                     _currentUser.Roles.Contains(
                         permission,
                         StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        return Task.FromResult(result);
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\LIMS.Infrastructure\Identity\Users\CurrentUser.cs
====================================================================================================
using System.Security.Claims;
using LIMS.Application.Abstractions.Identity;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http;

namespace LIMS.Infrastructure.Identity.Users;

public sealed class CurrentUser : ICurrentUser
{
    private readonly IHttpContextAccessor _httpContextAccessor;

    public CurrentUser(IHttpContextAccessor httpContextAccessor)
    {
        _httpContextAccessor = httpContextAccessor;
    }

    private ClaimsPrincipal? Principal =>
        _httpContextAccessor.HttpContext?.User;

    public string? UserId =>
        Principal?.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier)
        ?? Principal?.FindFirstValue("sub");

    public string? UserName =>
        Principal?.Identity?.Name
        ?? Principal?.FindFirstValue("preferred_username");

    public string? Email =>
        Principal?.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.Email)
        ?? Principal?.FindFirstValue("email");

    public bool IsAuthenticated =>
        Principal?.Identity?.IsAuthenticated ?? false;

    public IReadOnlyCollection<string> Roles =>
        Principal?.FindAll(ClaimTypes.Role)
            .Select(claim => claim.Value)
            .Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            .ToArray()
        ?? [];
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: tests\LIMS.UnitTests\Domain\Common\ValueObjectTests.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Common;

namespace LIMS.UnitTests.Domain.Common;

public sealed class ValueObjectTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void Equals_ShouldReturnTrueForSameComponents()
    {
        var first = new TestValueObject("Sample", 10);
        var second = new TestValueObject("Sample", 10);

        Assert.Equal(first, second);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Equals_ShouldReturnFalseForDifferentComponents()
    {
        var first = new TestValueObject("Sample", 10);
        var second = new TestValueObject("Sample", 20);

        Assert.NotEqual(first, second);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Equals_ShouldReturnFalseForDifferentTypes()
    {
        var first = new TestValueObject("Sample", 10);
        var second = new AnotherTestValueObject("Sample", 10);

        Assert.NotEqual(first, second);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GetHashCode_ShouldReturnSameValueForEqualObjects()
    {
        var first = new TestValueObject("Sample", 10);
        var second = new TestValueObject("Sample", 10);

        Assert.Equal(first.GetHashCode(), second.GetHashCode());
    }

    private sealed class TestValueObject(
        string name,
        int value) : ValueObject
    {
        protected override IEnumerable<object?> GetEqualityComponents()
        {
            yield return name;
            yield return value;
        }
    }

    private sealed class AnotherTestValueObject(
        string name,
        int value) : ValueObject
    {
        protected override IEnumerable<object?> GetEqualityComponents()
        {
            yield return name;
            yield return value;
        }
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: tests\LIMS.UnitTests\Domain\Experiments\ExperimentTests.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Experiments;
using LIMS.Domain.Experiments.Enums;

namespace LIMS.UnitTests.Domain.Experiments;

public sealed class ExperimentTests
{
    private static readonly DateTimeOffset Now =
        new(2026, 11, 6, 10, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero);

    private static Experiment CreateExperiment()
    {
        return Experiment.Create(
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            "EXP-001",
            "NMR Analysis",
            "Initial experiment description",
            "user-001",
            Now);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldInitializeExperiment()
    {
        var laboratoryId = Guid.NewGuid();
        var sampleId = Guid.NewGuid();

        var experiment = Experiment.Create(
            laboratoryId,
            sampleId,
            "exp-001",
            "NMR Analysis",
            "Description",
            "user-001",
            Now);

        Assert.NotEqual(Guid.Empty, experiment.Id);
        Assert.Equal(laboratoryId, experiment.LaboratoryId);
        Assert.Equal(sampleId, experiment.SampleId);
        Assert.Equal("EXP-001", experiment.ExperimentCode);
        Assert.Equal("NMR Analysis", experiment.Title);
        Assert.Equal("Description", experiment.Description);
        Assert.Equal(ExperimentStatus.Planned, experiment.Status);
        Assert.Equal("user-001", experiment.CreatedByUserId);
        Assert.Null(experiment.AssignedToUserId);
        Assert.Equal(Now, experiment.CreatedAt);
        Assert.Equal(Now, experiment.UpdatedAt);
        Assert.Null(experiment.StartedAt);
        Assert.Null(experiment.CompletedAt);
        Assert.Null(experiment.ArchivedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldNormalizeExperimentCode()
    {
        var experiment = Experiment.Create(
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            "  exp_001  ",
            "NMR Analysis",
            null,
            "user-001",
            Now);

        Assert.Equal("EXP_001", experiment.ExperimentCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldRaiseCreatedDomainEvent()
    {
        var laboratoryId = Guid.NewGuid();
        var sampleId = Guid.NewGuid();

        var experiment = Experiment.Create(
            laboratoryId,
            sampleId,
            "EXP-001",
            "NMR Analysis",
            null,
            "user-001",
            Now);

        var domainEvent = Assert.Single(experiment.DomainEvents);

        Assert.Equal(
            "ExperimentCreatedDomainEvent",
            domainEvent.GetType().Name);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldRejectEmptyLaboratoryId()
    {
        Assert.Throws<ExperimentDomainException>(() =>
            Experiment.Create(
                Guid.Empty,
                Guid.NewGuid(),
                "EXP-001",
                "NMR Analysis",
                null,
                "user-001",
                Now));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldRejectEmptySampleId()
    {
        Assert.Throws<ExperimentDomainException>(() =>
            Experiment.Create(
                Guid.NewGuid(),
                Guid.Empty,
                "EXP-001",
                "NMR Analysis",
                null,
                "user-001",
                Now));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldRejectInvalidExperimentCode()
    {
        Assert.Throws<ExperimentDomainException>(() =>
            Experiment.Create(
                Guid.NewGuid(),
                Guid.NewGuid(),
                "EXP 001",
                "NMR Analysis",
                null,
                "user-001",
                Now));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldRejectEmptyTitle()
    {
        Assert.Throws<ExperimentDomainException>(() =>
            Experiment.Create(
                Guid.NewGuid(),
                Guid.NewGuid(),
                "EXP-001",
                " ",
                null,
                "user-001",
                Now));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldRejectEmptyCreator()
    {
        Assert.Throws<ExperimentDomainException>(() =>
            Experiment.Create(
                Guid.NewGuid(),
                Guid.NewGuid(),
                "EXP-001",
                "NMR Analysis",
                null,
                " ",
                Now));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UpdateInformation_ShouldUpdateTitleAndDescription()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();
        var updatedAt = Now.AddHours(1);

        experiment.UpdateInformation(
            "Updated NMR Analysis",
            "Updated description",
            updatedAt);

        Assert.Equal("Updated NMR Analysis", experiment.Title);
        Assert.Equal("Updated description", experiment.Description);
        Assert.Equal(updatedAt, experiment.UpdatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UpdateInformation_ShouldRejectEmptyTitle()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();

        Assert.Throws<ExperimentDomainException>(() =>
            experiment.UpdateInformation(" ", null, Now.AddHours(1)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AssignTo_ShouldAssignUser()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();
        var updatedAt = Now.AddMinutes(10);

        experiment.AssignTo("user-002", updatedAt);

        Assert.Equal("user-002", experiment.AssignedToUserId);
        Assert.Equal(updatedAt, experiment.UpdatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Unassign_ShouldClearAssignedUser()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();

        experiment.AssignTo("user-002", Now.AddMinutes(10));
        experiment.Unassign(Now.AddMinutes(20));

        Assert.Null(experiment.AssignedToUserId);
        Assert.Equal(Now.AddMinutes(20), experiment.UpdatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Start_ShouldChangeStatusToInProgress()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();
        var startedAt = Now.AddHours(1);

        experiment.Start(startedAt);

        Assert.Equal(ExperimentStatus.InProgress, experiment.Status);
        Assert.Equal(startedAt, experiment.StartedAt);
        Assert.Equal(startedAt, experiment.UpdatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Complete_ShouldChangeStatusToCompleted()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();
        var startedAt = Now.AddHours(1);
        var completedAt = Now.AddHours(2);

        experiment.Start(startedAt);
        experiment.Complete(completedAt);

        Assert.Equal(ExperimentStatus.Completed, experiment.Status);
        Assert.Equal(startedAt, experiment.StartedAt);
        Assert.Equal(completedAt, experiment.CompletedAt);
        Assert.Equal(completedAt, experiment.UpdatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Complete_ShouldRejectCompletionBeforeStart()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();

        experiment.Start(Now.AddHours(2));

        Assert.Throws<ExperimentDomainException>(() =>
            experiment.Complete(Now.AddHours(1)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Cancel_ShouldChangeStatusToCancelled()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();
        var cancelledAt = Now.AddHours(1);

        experiment.Cancel("Equipment unavailable", cancelledAt);

        Assert.Equal(ExperimentStatus.Cancelled, experiment.Status);
        Assert.Equal(cancelledAt, experiment.UpdatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Cancel_ShouldRejectEmptyReason()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();

        Assert.Throws<ExperimentDomainException>(() =>
            experiment.Cancel(" ", Now.AddHours(1)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Archive_ShouldChangeCompletedExperimentToArchived()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();

        experiment.Start(Now.AddHours(1));
        experiment.Complete(Now.AddHours(2));
        experiment.Archive(Now.AddHours(3));

        Assert.Equal(ExperimentStatus.Archived, experiment.Status);
        Assert.Equal(Now.AddHours(3), experiment.ArchivedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Archive_ShouldChangeCancelledExperimentToArchived()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();

        experiment.Cancel("Cancelled by researcher", Now.AddHours(1));
        experiment.Archive(Now.AddHours(2));

        Assert.Equal(ExperimentStatus.Archived, experiment.Status);
        Assert.Equal(Now.AddHours(2), experiment.ArchivedAt);
    }
}

FILE: tests\LIMS.UnitTests\Domain\Experiments\ExperimentWorkflowTests.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Experiments;
using LIMS.Domain.Experiments.Enums;

namespace LIMS.UnitTests.Domain.Experiments;

public sealed class ExperimentWorkflowTests
{
    private static readonly DateTimeOffset Now =
        new(2026, 11, 6, 10, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero);

    private static Experiment CreateExperiment()
    {
        return Experiment.Create(
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            "EXP-001",
            "NMR Analysis",
            null,
            "user-001",
            Now);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Workflow_ShouldCompleteSuccessfully()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();

        experiment.AssignTo("researcher-001", Now.AddMinutes(5));
        experiment.Start(Now.AddMinutes(10));
        experiment.Complete(Now.AddMinutes(60));
        experiment.Archive(Now.AddMinutes(70));

        Assert.Equal(ExperimentStatus.Archived, experiment.Status);
        Assert.Equal("researcher-001", experiment.AssignedToUserId);
        Assert.Equal(Now.AddMinutes(10), experiment.StartedAt);
        Assert.Equal(Now.AddMinutes(60), experiment.CompletedAt);
        Assert.Equal(Now.AddMinutes(70), experiment.ArchivedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Start_ShouldRejectExperimentThatIsNotPlanned()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();

        experiment.Start(Now.AddMinutes(10));

        Assert.Throws<ExperimentDomainException>(() =>
            experiment.Start(Now.AddMinutes(20)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Complete_ShouldRejectExperimentThatIsNotInProgress()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();

        Assert.Throws<ExperimentDomainException>(() =>
            experiment.Complete(Now.AddMinutes(10)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Cancel_ShouldRejectCompletedExperiment()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();

        experiment.Start(Now.AddMinutes(10));
        experiment.Complete(Now.AddMinutes(20));

        Assert.Throws<ExperimentDomainException>(() =>
            experiment.Cancel("Cancellation reason", Now.AddMinutes(30)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Cancel_ShouldRejectAlreadyCancelledExperiment()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();

        experiment.Cancel("Initial cancellation", Now.AddMinutes(10));

        Assert.Throws<ExperimentDomainException>(() =>
            experiment.Cancel("Another cancellation", Now.AddMinutes(20)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Archive_ShouldRejectPlannedExperiment()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();

        Assert.Throws<ExperimentDomainException>(() =>
            experiment.Archive(Now.AddMinutes(10)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Archive_ShouldRejectInProgressExperiment()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();

        experiment.Start(Now.AddMinutes(10));

        Assert.Throws<ExperimentDomainException>(() =>
            experiment.Archive(Now.AddMinutes(20)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UpdateInformation_ShouldRejectInProgressExperiment()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();

        experiment.Start(Now.AddMinutes(10));

        Assert.Throws<ExperimentDomainException>(() =>
            experiment.UpdateInformation(
                "Updated title",
                null,
                Now.AddMinutes(20)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UpdateInformation_ShouldRejectCompletedExperiment()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();

        experiment.Start(Now.AddMinutes(10));
        experiment.Complete(Now.AddMinutes(20));

        Assert.Throws<ExperimentDomainException>(() =>
            experiment.UpdateInformation(
                "Updated title",
                null,
                Now.AddMinutes(30)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AssignTo_ShouldRejectTerminalExperiment()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();

        experiment.Cancel("Cancelled", Now.AddMinutes(10));

        Assert.Throws<ExperimentDomainException>(() =>
            experiment.AssignTo("researcher-001", Now.AddMinutes(20)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Unassign_ShouldRejectTerminalExperiment()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();

        experiment.AssignTo("researcher-001", Now.AddMinutes(10));
        experiment.Cancel("Cancelled", Now.AddMinutes(20));

        Assert.Throws<ExperimentDomainException>(() =>
            experiment.Unassign(Now.AddMinutes(30)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ArchivedExperiment_ShouldRejectFurtherCancellation()
    {
        var experiment = CreateExperiment();

        experiment.Cancel("Cancelled", Now.AddMinutes(10));
        experiment.Archive(Now.AddMinutes(20));

        Assert.Throws<ExperimentDomainException>(() =>
            experiment.Cancel("Another reason", Now.AddMinutes(30)));
    }
}

FILE: tests\LIMS.UnitTests\Domain\Instruments\InstrumentMaintenanceTests.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Instruments;
using LIMS.Domain.Instruments.Enums;

namespace LIMS.UnitTests.Domain.Instruments;

public sealed class InstrumentMaintenanceTests
{
    private static readonly Guid LaboratoryId =
        Guid.Parse("11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111");

    private static Instrument CreateInstrument()
    {
        return Instrument.Register(
            LaboratoryId,
            "NMR-001",
            "NMR Spectrometer",
            InstrumentType.NmrSpectrometer,
            DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,
            "Bruker",
            "AVANCE III",
            "SN-12345",
            "Chemistry Laboratory",
            null);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ScheduleMaintenance_ShouldCreateMaintenanceRecord()
    {
        var instrument = CreateInstrument();
        var scheduledAt = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddDays(1);

        instrument.ScheduleMaintenance(
            MaintenanceType.Preventive,
            "Annual preventive maintenance",
            scheduledAt,
            "user-001",
            DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        Assert.Equal(InstrumentStatus.UnderMaintenance, instrument.Status);
        Assert.Single(instrument.MaintenanceRecords);

        var maintenance = instrument.MaintenanceRecords.Single();

        Assert.Equal(MaintenanceType.Preventive, maintenance.Type);
        Assert.Equal("Annual preventive maintenance", maintenance.Description);
        Assert.Equal(scheduledAt, maintenance.ScheduledAt);
        Assert.Equal("user-001", maintenance.ScheduledByUserId);
        Assert.Null(maintenance.CompletedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ScheduleMaintenance_ShouldThrow_WhenInstrumentIsInUse()
    {
        var instrument = CreateInstrument();
        instrument.MarkInUse(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        Assert.Throws<InstrumentDomainException>(() =>
            instrument.ScheduleMaintenance(
                MaintenanceType.Preventive,
                "Maintenance",
                DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddDays(1),
                "user-001",
                DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ScheduleMaintenance_ShouldThrow_WhenActiveMaintenanceAlreadyExists()
    {
        var instrument = CreateInstrument();
        var now = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;

        instrument.ScheduleMaintenance(
            MaintenanceType.Preventive,
            "First maintenance",
            now.AddDays(1),
            "user-001",
            now);

        Assert.Throws<InstrumentDomainException>(() =>
            instrument.ScheduleMaintenance(
                MaintenanceType.Inspection,
                "Second maintenance",
                now.AddDays(2),
                "user-002",
                now.AddMinutes(1)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CompleteMaintenance_ShouldCompleteRecord()
    {
        var instrument = CreateInstrument();
        var now = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;

        instrument.ScheduleMaintenance(
            MaintenanceType.Calibration,
            "Calibration",
            now.AddDays(1),
            "user-001",
            now);

        var maintenanceId = instrument.MaintenanceRecords.Single().Id;
        var completedAt = now.AddDays(2);

        instrument.CompleteMaintenance(
            maintenanceId,
            "user-002",
            "Calibration completed successfully",
            completedAt);

        var maintenance = instrument.MaintenanceRecords.Single();

        Assert.Equal(completedAt, maintenance.CompletedAt);
        Assert.Equal("user-002", maintenance.CompletedByUserId);
        Assert.Equal(
            "Calibration completed successfully",
            maintenance.CompletionNotes);
        Assert.Equal(InstrumentStatus.Available, instrument.Status);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CompleteMaintenance_ShouldThrow_WhenMaintenanceDoesNotExist()
    {
        var instrument = CreateInstrument();

        Assert.Throws<InstrumentDomainException>(() =>
            instrument.CompleteMaintenance(
                Guid.NewGuid(),
                "user-001",
                null,
                DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CompleteMaintenance_ShouldThrow_WhenAlreadyCompleted()
    {
        var instrument = CreateInstrument();
        var now = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;

        instrument.ScheduleMaintenance(
            MaintenanceType.Preventive,
            "Maintenance",
            now.AddDays(1),
            "user-001",
            now);

        var maintenanceId = instrument.MaintenanceRecords.Single().Id;

        instrument.CompleteMaintenance(
            maintenanceId,
            "user-002",
            null,
            now.AddDays(2));

        Assert.Throws<InstrumentDomainException>(() =>
            instrument.CompleteMaintenance(
                maintenanceId,
                "user-003",
                null,
                now.AddDays(3)));
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: tests\LIMS.UnitTests\Domain\Instruments\InstrumentTests.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Instruments;
using LIMS.Domain.Instruments.Enums;

namespace LIMS.UnitTests.Domain.Instruments;

public sealed class InstrumentTests
{
    private static readonly Guid LaboratoryId =
        Guid.Parse("11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111");

    private static Instrument CreateInstrument(
        DateTimeOffset? registeredAt = null)
    {
        return Instrument.Register(
            LaboratoryId,
            "NMR-001",
            "NMR Spectrometer",
            InstrumentType.NmrSpectrometer,
            registeredAt ?? DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,
            "Bruker",
            "AVANCE III",
            "SN-12345",
            "Chemistry Laboratory",
            "300 MHz spectrometer");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Register_ShouldCreateAvailableInstrument()
    {
        var registeredAt = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;

        var instrument = CreateInstrument(registeredAt);

        Assert.NotEqual(Guid.Empty, instrument.Id);
        Assert.Equal(LaboratoryId, instrument.LaboratoryId);
        Assert.Equal("NMR-001", instrument.InstrumentCode);
        Assert.Equal("NMR Spectrometer", instrument.Name);
        Assert.Equal(InstrumentType.NmrSpectrometer, instrument.Type);
        Assert.Equal(InstrumentStatus.Available, instrument.Status);
        Assert.Equal(registeredAt, instrument.RegisteredAt);
        Assert.Single(instrument.DomainEvents);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Register_ShouldNormalizeInstrumentCode()
    {
        var instrument = Instrument.Register(
            LaboratoryId,
            "  nmr-001  ",
            "NMR Spectrometer",
            InstrumentType.NmrSpectrometer,
            DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null);

        Assert.Equal("NMR-001", instrument.InstrumentCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Register_ShouldThrow_WhenLaboratoryIdIsEmpty()
    {
        Assert.Throws<InstrumentDomainException>(() =>
            Instrument.Register(
                Guid.Empty,
                "NMR-001",
                "NMR Spectrometer",
                InstrumentType.NmrSpectrometer,
                DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Register_ShouldThrow_WhenCodeIsInvalid()
    {
        Assert.Throws<InstrumentDomainException>(() =>
            Instrument.Register(
                LaboratoryId,
                "NMR 001",
                "NMR Spectrometer",
                InstrumentType.NmrSpectrometer,
                DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UpdateInformation_ShouldUpdateInstrumentDetails()
    {
        var instrument = CreateInstrument();
        var updatedAt = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddMinutes(1);

        instrument.UpdateInformation(
            "Updated NMR",
            InstrumentType.NmrSpectrometer,
            "Bruker",
            "AVANCE NEO",
            "SN-99999",
            "Room 204",
            "Updated description",
            updatedAt);

        Assert.Equal("Updated NMR", instrument.Name);
        Assert.Equal("AVANCE NEO", instrument.Model);
        Assert.Equal("SN-99999", instrument.SerialNumber);
        Assert.Equal("Room 204", instrument.Location);
        Assert.Equal("Updated description", instrument.Description);
        Assert.Equal(updatedAt, instrument.UpdatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void MarkInUse_ShouldChangeStatusToInUse()
    {
        var instrument = CreateInstrument();
        var occurredOn = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddMinutes(1);

        instrument.MarkInUse(occurredOn);

        Assert.Equal(InstrumentStatus.InUse, instrument.Status);
        Assert.Equal(occurredOn, instrument.UpdatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void MarkAvailable_ShouldChangeStatusToAvailable()
    {
        var instrument = CreateInstrument();
        instrument.MarkInUse(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        var occurredOn = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddMinutes(1);
        instrument.MarkAvailable(occurredOn);

        Assert.Equal(InstrumentStatus.Available, instrument.Status);
        Assert.Equal(occurredOn, instrument.UpdatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void MarkInUse_ShouldThrow_WhenInstrumentIsNotAvailable()
    {
        var instrument = CreateInstrument();
        instrument.MarkInUse(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        Assert.Throws<InstrumentDomainException>(() =>
            instrument.MarkInUse(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddMinutes(1)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void MarkAvailable_ShouldThrow_WhenInstrumentIsNotInUse()
    {
        var instrument = CreateInstrument();

        Assert.Throws<InstrumentDomainException>(() =>
            instrument.MarkAvailable(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void MarkOutOfService_ShouldChangeStatus()
    {
        var instrument = CreateInstrument();
        var occurredOn = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddMinutes(1);

        instrument.MarkOutOfService(
            "Unexpected technical failure",
            occurredOn);

        Assert.Equal(InstrumentStatus.OutOfService, instrument.Status);
        Assert.Equal(occurredOn, instrument.UpdatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RestoreService_ShouldReturnInstrumentToAvailable()
    {
        var instrument = CreateInstrument();
        instrument.MarkOutOfService(
            "Unexpected technical failure",
            DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        instrument.RestoreService(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddMinutes(1));

        Assert.Equal(InstrumentStatus.Available, instrument.Status);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Retire_ShouldSetRetiredStatusAndTimestamp()
    {
        var instrument = CreateInstrument();
        var retiredAt = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddDays(1);

        instrument.Retire(retiredAt);

        Assert.Equal(InstrumentStatus.Retired, instrument.Status);
        Assert.Equal(retiredAt, instrument.RetiredAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Retire_ShouldThrow_WhenInstrumentIsInUse()
    {
        var instrument = CreateInstrument();
        instrument.MarkInUse(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        Assert.Throws<InstrumentDomainException>(() =>
            instrument.Retire(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddMinutes(1)));
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: tests\LIMS.UnitTests\Domain\Instruments\InstrumentWorkflowTests.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Instruments;
using LIMS.Domain.Instruments.Enums;

namespace LIMS.UnitTests.Domain.Instruments;

public sealed class InstrumentWorkflowTests
{
    private static readonly Guid LaboratoryId =
        Guid.Parse("11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111");

    private static Instrument CreateInstrument()
    {
        return Instrument.Register(
            LaboratoryId,
            "HPLC-001",
            "HPLC System",
            InstrumentType.Chromatograph,
            DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,
            "Agilent",
            "1260 Infinity",
            "HPLC-SN-001",
            "Analytical Laboratory",
            null);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CompleteLifecycle_ShouldRetireInstrument()
    {
        var instrument = CreateInstrument();
        var now = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;

        instrument.MarkInUse(now.AddMinutes(1));
        instrument.MarkAvailable(now.AddMinutes(2));

        instrument.ScheduleMaintenance(
            MaintenanceType.Preventive,
            "Scheduled maintenance",
            now.AddDays(1),
            "technician-001",
            now.AddMinutes(3));

        var maintenanceId = instrument.MaintenanceRecords.Single().Id;

        instrument.CompleteMaintenance(
            maintenanceId,
            "technician-001",
            "Maintenance completed",
            now.AddDays(2));

        instrument.Retire(now.AddDays(3));

        Assert.Equal(InstrumentStatus.Retired, instrument.Status);
        Assert.NotNull(instrument.RetiredAt);
        Assert.Single(instrument.MaintenanceRecords);
        Assert.NotNull(instrument.MaintenanceRecords.Single().CompletedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Retire_ShouldThrow_WhenActiveMaintenanceExists()
    {
        var instrument = CreateInstrument();
        var now = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;

        instrument.ScheduleMaintenance(
            MaintenanceType.Corrective,
            "Repair required",
            now.AddDays(1),
            "technician-001",
            now);

        Assert.Throws<InstrumentDomainException>(() =>
            instrument.Retire(now.AddDays(2)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RestoreService_ShouldThrow_WhenActiveMaintenanceExists()
    {
        var instrument = CreateInstrument();
        var now = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;

        instrument.MarkOutOfService(
            "Electrical fault",
            now);

        instrument.ScheduleMaintenance(
            MaintenanceType.Corrective,
            "Electrical repair",
            now.AddDays(1),
            "technician-001",
            now.AddMinutes(1));

        Assert.Throws<InstrumentDomainException>(() =>
            instrument.RestoreService(now.AddDays(2)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RetiredInstrument_ShouldNotBeUpdated()
    {
        var instrument = CreateInstrument();
        var now = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;

        instrument.Retire(now.AddDays(1));

        Assert.Throws<InstrumentDomainException>(() =>
            instrument.UpdateInformation(
                "Updated HPLC",
                InstrumentType.Chromatograph,
                "Agilent",
                "New Model",
                "NEW-SERIAL",
                "Room 10",
                null,
                now.AddDays(2)));
    }
}

using LIMS.Domain.Laboratories.Entities;
using LIMS.Domain.Laboratories.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Laboratories.Exceptions;

namespace LIMS.UnitTests.Domain.Laboratories;

public sealed class LaboratoryMemberTests
{
    private static readonly Guid LaboratoryId =
        Guid.Parse("aaaaaaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa");

    private static readonly DateTimeOffset JoinedAt =
        new(2026, 11, 6, 12, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero);

    [Fact]
    public void Constructor_ShouldCreateValidMember()
    {
        var member = CreateMember();

        Assert.NotEqual(Guid.Empty, member.Id);
        Assert.Equal(LaboratoryId, member.LaboratoryId);
        Assert.Equal("user-001", member.UserId);
        Assert.Equal(LaboratoryMemberRole.Researcher, member.Role);
        Assert.Equal(JoinedAt, member.JoinedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Constructor_ShouldRejectEmptyLaboratoryId()
    {
        Assert.Throws<LaboratoryDomainException>(() =>
            new LaboratoryMember(
                Guid.Empty,
                "user-001",
                LaboratoryMemberRole.Researcher,
                JoinedAt));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Constructor_ShouldRejectEmptyUserId()
    {
        Assert.Throws<LaboratoryDomainException>(() =>
            new LaboratoryMember(
                LaboratoryId,
                " ",
                LaboratoryMemberRole.Researcher,
                JoinedAt));
    }

    private static LaboratoryMember CreateMember()
    {
        return new LaboratoryMember(
            LaboratoryId,
            " user-001 ",
            LaboratoryMemberRole.Researcher,
            JoinedAt);
    }
}

FILE: tests\LIMS.UnitTests\Domain\Laboratories\LaboratoryTests.cs
