using LIMS.Domain.Laboratories;
using LIMS.Domain.Laboratories.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Laboratories.Events;
using LIMS.Domain.Laboratories.Exceptions;

namespace LIMS.UnitTests.Domain.Laboratories;

public sealed class LaboratoryTests
{
    private static readonly DateTimeOffset Now =
        new(2026, 11, 6, 12, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero);

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldCreateActiveLaboratory()
    {
        var laboratory = Laboratory.Create(
            " Analytical Chemistry ",
            " ac-001 ",
            " Main laboratory ",
            Now);

        Assert.NotEqual(Guid.Empty, laboratory.Id);
        Assert.Equal("Analytical Chemistry", laboratory.Name);
        Assert.Equal("AC-001", laboratory.Code);
        Assert.Equal("Main laboratory", laboratory.Description);
        Assert.Equal(LaboratoryStatus.Active, laboratory.Status);
        Assert.Equal(Now, laboratory.CreatedAt);

        var domainEvent = Assert.Single(laboratory.DomainEvents);
        Assert.IsType<LaboratoryCreatedDomainEvent>(domainEvent);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("")]
    [InlineData("   ")]
    public void Create_ShouldRejectEmptyName(string name)
    {
        Assert.Throws<LaboratoryDomainException>(() =>
            Laboratory.Create(name, "LAB-001", null, Now));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldRejectNameLongerThan150Characters()
    {
        var name = new string('A', 151);

        Assert.Throws<LaboratoryDomainException>(() =>
            Laboratory.Create(name, "LAB-001", null, Now));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldNormalizeLaboratoryCode()
    {
        var laboratory = Laboratory.Create(
            "Laboratory",
            " lab_001 ",
            null,
            Now);

        Assert.Equal("LAB_001", laboratory.Code);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("LAB 001")]
    [InlineData("LAB@001")]
    [InlineData("LAB.001")]
    public void Create_ShouldRejectInvalidCode(string code)
    {
        Assert.Throws<LaboratoryDomainException>(() =>
            Laboratory.Create("Laboratory", code, null, Now));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddMember_ShouldAddMemberAndRaiseDomainEvent()
    {
        var laboratory = CreateLaboratory();

        laboratory.AddMember(
            "user-001",
            LaboratoryMemberRole.Researcher,
            Now);

        var member = Assert.Single(laboratory.Members);

        Assert.Equal("user-001", member.UserId);
        Assert.Equal(LaboratoryMemberRole.Researcher, member.Role);
        Assert.Equal(Now, member.JoinedAt);

        Assert.Contains(
            laboratory.DomainEvents,
            domainEvent =>
                domainEvent is LaboratoryMemberAddedDomainEvent);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddMember_ShouldRejectDuplicateUser()
    {
        var laboratory = CreateLaboratory();

        laboratory.AddMember(
            "user-001",
            LaboratoryMemberRole.Researcher,
            Now);

        Assert.Throws<LaboratoryDomainException>(() =>
            laboratory.AddMember(
                "USER-001",
                LaboratoryMemberRole.Technician,
                Now));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddMember_ShouldRejectEmptyUserId()
    {
        var laboratory = CreateLaboratory();

        Assert.Throws<LaboratoryDomainException>(() =>
            laboratory.AddMember(
                " ",
                LaboratoryMemberRole.Researcher,
                Now));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ChangeMemberRole_ShouldUpdateMemberRole()
    {
        var laboratory = CreateLaboratory();

        laboratory.AddMember(
            "user-001",
            LaboratoryMemberRole.Researcher,
            Now);

        laboratory.ChangeMemberRole(
            "user-001",
            LaboratoryMemberRole.LaboratoryManager,
            Now.AddMinutes(5));

        var member = Assert.Single(laboratory.Members);

        Assert.Equal(
            LaboratoryMemberRole.LaboratoryManager,
            member.Role);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ChangeMemberRole_ShouldRejectUnknownMember()
    {
        var laboratory = CreateLaboratory();

        Assert.Throws<LaboratoryDomainException>(() =>
            laboratory.ChangeMemberRole(
                "unknown-user",
                LaboratoryMemberRole.Researcher,
                Now));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RemoveMember_ShouldRemoveMemberAndRaiseDomainEvent()
    {
        var laboratory = CreateLaboratory();

        laboratory.AddMember(
            "user-001",
            LaboratoryMemberRole.Researcher,
            Now);

        laboratory.RemoveMember("user-001", Now.AddMinutes(10));

        Assert.Empty(laboratory.Members);

        Assert.Contains(
            laboratory.DomainEvents,
            domainEvent =>
                domainEvent is LaboratoryMemberRemovedDomainEvent);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RemoveMember_ShouldRejectUnknownMember()
    {
        var laboratory = CreateLaboratory();

        Assert.Throws<LaboratoryDomainException>(() =>
            laboratory.RemoveMember("unknown-user", Now));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ChangeStatus_ShouldChangeStatusAndRaiseDomainEvent()
    {
        var laboratory = CreateLaboratory();

        laboratory.ChangeStatus(
            LaboratoryStatus.Inactive,
            Now.AddMinutes(10));

        Assert.Equal(
            LaboratoryStatus.Inactive,
            laboratory.Status);

        Assert.Contains(
            laboratory.DomainEvents,
            domainEvent =>
                domainEvent is LaboratoryStatusChangedDomainEvent);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ChangeStatus_ShouldNotRaiseEventWhenStatusIsUnchanged()
    {
        var laboratory = CreateLaboratory();
        var initialEventCount = laboratory.DomainEvents.Count;

        laboratory.ChangeStatus(
            LaboratoryStatus.Active,
            Now.AddMinutes(10));

        Assert.Equal(initialEventCount, laboratory.DomainEvents.Count);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void InactiveLaboratory_ShouldNotAcceptNewMembers()
    {
        var laboratory = CreateLaboratory();

        laboratory.ChangeStatus(
            LaboratoryStatus.Inactive,
            Now);

        Assert.Throws<LaboratoryDomainException>(() =>
            laboratory.AddMember(
                "user-001",
                LaboratoryMemberRole.Researcher,
                Now));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ArchivedLaboratory_ShouldNotAllowInformationUpdate()
    {
        var laboratory = CreateLaboratory();

        laboratory.ChangeStatus(
            LaboratoryStatus.Archived,
            Now);

        Assert.Throws<LaboratoryDomainException>(() =>
            laboratory.UpdateInformation(
                "Updated Laboratory",
                "LAB-002",
                null,
                Now.AddMinutes(10)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ArchivedLaboratory_ShouldNotAllowMemberRemoval()
    {
        var laboratory = CreateLaboratory();

        laboratory.AddMember(
            "user-001",
            LaboratoryMemberRole.Researcher,
            Now);

        laboratory.ChangeStatus(
            LaboratoryStatus.Archived,
            Now.AddMinutes(5));

        Assert.Throws<LaboratoryDomainException>(() =>
            laboratory.RemoveMember(
                "user-001",
                Now.AddMinutes(10)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UpdateInformation_ShouldUpdateLaboratoryFields()
    {
        var laboratory = CreateLaboratory();
        var updatedAt = Now.AddHours(1);

        laboratory.UpdateInformation(
            "Updated Laboratory",
            "LAB-002",
            "Updated description",
            updatedAt);

        Assert.Equal("Updated Laboratory", laboratory.Name);
        Assert.Equal("LAB-002", laboratory.Code);
        Assert.Equal("Updated description", laboratory.Description);
        Assert.Equal(updatedAt, laboratory.UpdatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ClearDomainEvents_ShouldRemoveAllEvents()
    {
        var laboratory = CreateLaboratory();

        Assert.NotEmpty(laboratory.DomainEvents);

        laboratory.ClearDomainEvents();

        Assert.Empty(laboratory.DomainEvents);
    }

    private static Laboratory CreateLaboratory()
    {
        return Laboratory.Create(
            "Analytical Chemistry",
            "AC-001",
            null,
            Now);
    }
}

using LIMS.Domain.Protocols;
using LIMS.Domain.Protocols.Enums;
using Xunit;

namespace LIMS.UnitTests.Domain.Protocols;

public sealed class ProtocolStepTests
{
    private static Protocol CreateProtocol()
    {
        return Protocol.Create(
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            "NMR-001",
            "NMR Protocol",
            null,
            "1.0",
            "user-001",
            DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddStep_ShouldAcceptValidMeasurementRange()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();

        protocol.AddStep(
            1,
            "Temperature measurement",
            "Measure sample temperature.",
            ProtocolStepType.Measurement,
            true,
            25m,
            "Â°C",
            20m,
            30m,
            null,
            DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        var step = Assert.Single(protocol.Steps);

        Assert.Equal(25m, step.TargetValue);
        Assert.Equal(20m, step.MinimumValue);
        Assert.Equal(30m, step.MaximumValue);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddStep_ShouldRejectMinimumGreaterThanMaximum()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();

        Assert.Throws<ProtocolDomainException>(() =>
            protocol.AddStep(
                1,
                "Temperature",
                "Measure temperature.",
                ProtocolStepType.Measurement,
                true,
                null,
                "Â°C",
                40m,
                20m,
                null,
                DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddStep_ShouldRejectTargetBelowMinimum()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();

        Assert.Throws<ProtocolDomainException>(() =>
            protocol.AddStep(
                1,
                "Temperature",
                "Measure temperature.",
                ProtocolStepType.Measurement,
                true,
                15m,
                "Â°C",
                20m,
                30m,
                null,
                DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddStep_ShouldRejectTargetAboveMaximum()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();

        Assert.Throws<ProtocolDomainException>(() =>
            protocol.AddStep(
                1,
                "Temperature",
                "Measure temperature.",
                ProtocolStepType.Measurement,
                true,
                35m,
                "Â°C",
                20m,
                30m,
                null,
                DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddStep_ShouldRejectInvalidSequence()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();

        Assert.Throws<ProtocolDomainException>(() =>
            protocol.AddStep(
                0,
                "Temperature",
                "Measure temperature.",
                ProtocolStepType.Measurement,
                true,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddStep_ShouldRejectEmptyTitle()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();

        Assert.Throws<ProtocolDomainException>(() =>
            protocol.AddStep(
                1,
                " ",
                "Measure temperature.",
                ProtocolStepType.Measurement,
                true,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddStep_ShouldRejectInvalidStepType()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();

        Assert.Throws<ProtocolDomainException>(() =>
            protocol.AddStep(
                1,
                "Temperature",
                "Measure temperature.",
                (ProtocolStepType)999,
                true,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
    }
}

FILE: tests\LIMS.UnitTests\Domain\Protocols\ProtocolTests.cs

using LIMS.Domain.Protocols;
using LIMS.Domain.Protocols.Enums;
using Xunit;

namespace LIMS.UnitTests.Domain.Protocols;

public sealed class ProtocolTests
{
    private static Protocol CreateProtocol()
    {
        return Protocol.Create(
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            "NMR-PROTOCOL-001",
            "NMR Analysis",
            "Standard NMR analysis protocol.",
            "1.0",
            "user-001",
            DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);
    }

    private static void AddValidStep(Protocol protocol)
    {
        protocol.AddStep(
            1,
            "Sample preparation",
            "Prepare the sample for NMR analysis.",
            ProtocolStepType.Preparation,
            true,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldInitializeDraftProtocol()
    {
        var laboratoryId = Guid.NewGuid();

        var protocol = Protocol.Create(
            laboratoryId,
            "NMR-001",
            "NMR Protocol",
            null,
            "1.0",
            "user-001",
            DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        Assert.NotEqual(Guid.Empty, protocol.Id);
        Assert.Equal(laboratoryId, protocol.LaboratoryId);
        Assert.Equal("NMR-001", protocol.ProtocolCode);
        Assert.Equal("NMR Protocol", protocol.Name);
        Assert.Equal(ProtocolStatus.Draft, protocol.Status);
        Assert.Empty(protocol.Steps);
        Assert.Single(protocol.DomainEvents);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldNormalizeProtocolCode()
    {
        var protocol = Protocol.Create(
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            " nmr-001 ",
            "NMR Protocol",
            null,
            "1.0",
            "user-001",
            DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        Assert.Equal("NMR-001", protocol.ProtocolCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldRejectEmptyLaboratoryId()
    {
        Assert.Throws<ProtocolDomainException>(() =>
            Protocol.Create(
                Guid.Empty,
                "NMR-001",
                "NMR Protocol",
                null,
                "1.0",
                "user-001",
                DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldRejectInvalidProtocolCode()
    {
        Assert.Throws<ProtocolDomainException>(() =>
            Protocol.Create(
                Guid.NewGuid(),
                "NMR 001!",
                "NMR Protocol",
                null,
                "1.0",
                "user-001",
                DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UpdateInformation_ShouldUpdateDraftProtocol()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();
        var updatedAt = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddMinutes(5);

        protocol.UpdateInformation(
            "Updated NMR Protocol",
            "Updated description.",
            "2.0",
            updatedAt);

        Assert.Equal("Updated NMR Protocol", protocol.Name);
        Assert.Equal("Updated description.", protocol.Description);
        Assert.Equal("2.0", protocol.Version);
        Assert.Equal(updatedAt, protocol.UpdatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddStep_ShouldAddStepToDraftProtocol()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();

        AddValidStep(protocol);

        var step = Assert.Single(protocol.Steps);

        Assert.Equal(1, step.Sequence);
        Assert.Equal("Sample preparation", step.Title);
        Assert.Equal(ProtocolStepType.Preparation, step.Type);
        Assert.True(step.IsRequired);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddStep_ShouldRejectDuplicateSequence()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();

        AddValidStep(protocol);

        Assert.Throws<ProtocolDomainException>(() =>
            protocol.AddStep(
                1,
                "Second step",
                "Another instruction.",
                ProtocolStepType.Instruction,
                true,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddStep_ShouldSortStepsBySequence()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();

        protocol.AddStep(
            3,
            "Third step",
            "Third instruction.",
            ProtocolStepType.Instruction,
            true,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        protocol.AddStep(
            1,
            "First step",
            "First instruction.",
            ProtocolStepType.Instruction,
            true,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        protocol.AddStep(
            2,
            "Second step",
            "Second instruction.",
            ProtocolStepType.Instruction,
            true,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { 1, 2, 3 }, protocol.Steps.Select(x => x.Sequence));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UpdateStep_ShouldUpdateExistingStep()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();
        AddValidStep(protocol);

        var step = protocol.Steps.Single();

        protocol.UpdateStep(
            step.Id,
            2,
            "Updated preparation",
            "Updated instruction.",
            ProtocolStepType.Instruction,
            false,
            25m,
            "Â°C",
            20m,
            30m,
            "Keep temperature controlled.",
            DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddMinutes(2));

        var updatedStep = protocol.Steps.Single();

        Assert.Equal(2, updatedStep.Sequence);
        Assert.Equal("Updated preparation", updatedStep.Title);
        Assert.Equal(ProtocolStepType.Instruction, updatedStep.Type);
        Assert.False(updatedStep.IsRequired);
        Assert.Equal(25m, updatedStep.TargetValue);
        Assert.Equal("Â°C", updatedStep.TargetUnit);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UpdateStep_ShouldRejectUnknownStep()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();

        Assert.Throws<ProtocolDomainException>(() =>
            protocol.UpdateStep(
                Guid.NewGuid(),
                1,
                "Unknown",
                "Instruction.",
                ProtocolStepType.Instruction,
                true,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RemoveStep_ShouldRemoveExistingStep()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();
        AddValidStep(protocol);

        var stepId = protocol.Steps.Single().Id;

        protocol.RemoveStep(stepId, DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        Assert.Empty(protocol.Steps);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Activate_ShouldRequireAtLeastOneStep()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();

        Assert.Throws<ProtocolDomainException>(() =>
            protocol.Activate(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Activate_ShouldActivateProtocolWithSteps()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();
        AddValidStep(protocol);

        var activatedAt = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddMinutes(1);

        protocol.Activate(activatedAt);

        Assert.Equal(ProtocolStatus.Active, protocol.Status);
        Assert.Equal(activatedAt, protocol.ActivatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UpdateInformation_ShouldRejectActiveProtocol()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();
        AddValidStep(protocol);
        protocol.Activate(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        Assert.Throws<ProtocolDomainException>(() =>
            protocol.UpdateInformation(
                "Changed name",
                null,
                "2.0",
                DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddStep_ShouldRejectActiveProtocol()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();
        AddValidStep(protocol);
        protocol.Activate(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        Assert.Throws<ProtocolDomainException>(() =>
            protocol.AddStep(
                2,
                "Another step",
                "Another instruction.",
                ProtocolStepType.Instruction,
                true,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Deprecate_ShouldChangeStatus()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();
        AddValidStep(protocol);
        protocol.Activate(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        var deprecatedAt = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddMinutes(2);

        protocol.Deprecate(deprecatedAt);

        Assert.Equal(ProtocolStatus.Deprecated, protocol.Status);
        Assert.Equal(deprecatedAt, protocol.DeprecatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Archive_ShouldRequireDeprecatedStatus()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();
        AddValidStep(protocol);
        protocol.Activate(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        Assert.Throws<ProtocolDomainException>(() =>
            protocol.Archive(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Archive_ShouldArchiveDeprecatedProtocol()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();
        AddValidStep(protocol);
        protocol.Activate(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);
        protocol.Deprecate(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddMinutes(1));

        var archivedAt = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddMinutes(2);

        protocol.Archive(archivedAt);

        Assert.Equal(ProtocolStatus.Archived, protocol.Status);
        Assert.Equal(archivedAt, protocol.ArchivedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Deprecate_ShouldRejectDraftProtocol()
    {
        var protocol = CreateProtocol();

        Assert.Throws<ProtocolDomainException>(() =>
            protocol.Deprecate(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: tests\LIMS.UnitTests\Domain\Protocols\ProtocolWorkflowTests.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Protocols;
using LIMS.Domain.Protocols.Enums;
using Xunit;

namespace LIMS.UnitTests.Domain.Protocols;

public sealed class ProtocolWorkflowTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void CompleteLifecycle_ShouldFollowExpectedTransitions()
    {
        var now = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;

        var protocol = Protocol.Create(
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            "NMR-LIFECYCLE-001",
            "NMR Lifecycle Protocol",
            "Lifecycle test.",
            "1.0",
            "user-001",
            now);

        protocol.AddStep(
            1,
            "Prepare sample",
            "Prepare sample for analysis.",
            ProtocolStepType.Preparation,
            true,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            now.AddMinutes(1));

        protocol.AddStep(
            2,
            "Measure sample",
            "Acquire NMR spectrum.",
            ProtocolStepType.Measurement,
            true,
            25m,
            "Â°C",
            20m,
            30m,
            null,
            now.AddMinutes(2));

        Assert.Equal(ProtocolStatus.Draft, protocol.Status);
        Assert.Equal(2, protocol.Steps.Count);

        protocol.Activate(now.AddMinutes(3));

        Assert.Equal(ProtocolStatus.Active, protocol.Status);

        protocol.Deprecate(now.AddMinutes(4));

        Assert.Equal(ProtocolStatus.Deprecated, protocol.Status);

        protocol.Archive(now.AddMinutes(5));

        Assert.Equal(ProtocolStatus.Archived, protocol.Status);
        Assert.NotNull(protocol.ActivatedAt);
        Assert.NotNull(protocol.DeprecatedAt);
        Assert.NotNull(protocol.ArchivedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ArchivedProtocol_ShouldNotBeReactivated()
    {
        var protocol = Protocol.Create(
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            "NMR-ARCHIVE-001",
            "Archived Protocol",
            null,
            "1.0",
            "user-001",
            DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        protocol.AddStep(
            1,
            "Prepare",
            "Prepare sample.",
            ProtocolStepType.Preparation,
            true,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        protocol.Activate(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);
        protocol.Deprecate(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);
        protocol.Archive(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        Assert.Throws<ProtocolDomainException>(() =>
            protocol.Activate(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
    }
}

using LIMS.Domain.Requests;
using LIMS.Domain.Requests.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Requests.Exceptions;
using LIMS.Domain.Samples.Enums;
using Xunit;

namespace LIMS.UnitTests.Requests;

public sealed class SampleRequestTests
{
    private static readonly DateTime CreatedAt =
        new(2026, 11, 6, 10, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc);

    private static SampleRequest CreateRequest()
    {
        return SampleRequest.Create(
            "REQ-001",
            "NMR Analysis Request",
            "Initial request",
            "Saffron Sample",
            "Saffron from batch A",
            SampleType.Powder,
            "Farm A",
            "requester-001",
            SampleRequestPriority.Normal,
            CreatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldInitializeDraftRequest()
    {
        var request = CreateRequest();

        Assert.NotEqual(Guid.Empty, request.Id);
        Assert.Equal("REQ-001", request.RequestCode);
        Assert.Equal("NMR Analysis Request", request.Title);
        Assert.Equal("Saffron Sample", request.SampleName);
        Assert.Equal(SampleType.Powder, request.RequestedSampleType);
        Assert.Equal(SampleRequestPriority.Normal, request.Priority);
        Assert.Equal(SampleRequestStatus.Draft, request.Status);
        Assert.Equal("requester-001", request.RequestedByUserId);
        Assert.Equal(CreatedAt, request.CreatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldNormalizeRequestCode()
    {
        var request = SampleRequest.Create(
            "  req-002  ",
            "Analysis",
            null,
            "Sample",
            null,
            SampleType.Solid,
            null,
            "requester-001",
            SampleRequestPriority.High,
            CreatedAt);

        Assert.Equal("REQ-002", request.RequestCode);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("")]
    [InlineData(" ")]
    [InlineData("invalid code")]
    [InlineData("req/001")]
    public void Create_ShouldRejectInvalidRequestCode(string code)
    {
        Assert.Throws<SampleRequestDomainException>(() =>
            SampleRequest.Create(
                code,
                "Analysis",
                null,
                "Sample",
                null,
                SampleType.Solid,
                null,
                "requester-001",
                SampleRequestPriority.Normal,
                CreatedAt));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldRejectEmptyTitle()
    {
        Assert.Throws<SampleRequestDomainException>(() =>
            SampleRequest.Create(
                "REQ-001",
                "",
                null,
                "Sample",
                null,
                SampleType.Solid,
                null,
                "requester-001",
                SampleRequestPriority.Normal,
                CreatedAt));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UpdateInformation_ShouldUpdateDraftRequest()
    {
        var request = CreateRequest();
        var updatedAt = CreatedAt.AddHours(1);

        request.UpdateInformation(
            "Updated title",
            "Updated description",
            "Updated sample",
            "Updated sample description",
            SampleType.Liquid,
            "Updated source",
            SampleRequestPriority.High,
            updatedAt);

        Assert.Equal("Updated title", request.Title);
        Assert.Equal("Updated description", request.Description);
        Assert.Equal("Updated sample", request.SampleName);
        Assert.Equal(SampleType.Liquid, request.RequestedSampleType);
        Assert.Equal(SampleRequestPriority.High, request.Priority);
        Assert.Equal(updatedAt, request.UpdatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Submit_ShouldChangeStatusToSubmitted()
    {
        var request = CreateRequest();

        request.Submit(
            "requester-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1));

        Assert.Equal(SampleRequestStatus.Submitted, request.Status);
        Assert.Equal("requester-001", request.RequestedByUserId);
        Assert.NotNull(request.SubmittedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Submit_ShouldRejectDifferentUser()
    {
        var request = CreateRequest();

        Assert.Throws<SampleRequestDomainException>(() =>
            request.Submit(
                "another-user",
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void StartReview_ShouldAssignReviewer()
    {
        var request = CreateSubmittedRequest();

        request.StartReview(
            "reviewer-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(2));

        Assert.Equal(SampleRequestStatus.UnderReview, request.Status);
        Assert.Equal("reviewer-001", request.AssignedReviewerUserId);
        Assert.NotNull(request.ReviewStartedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void StartReview_ShouldRejectRequesterAsReviewer()
    {
        var request = CreateSubmittedRequest();

        Assert.Throws<SampleRequestDomainException>(() =>
            request.StartReview(
                "requester-001",
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(2)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Approve_ShouldAssignLaboratory()
    {
        var request = CreateUnderReviewRequest();
        var laboratoryId = Guid.NewGuid();

        request.Approve(
            "reviewer-001",
            laboratoryId,
            "Approved",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(3));

        Assert.Equal(SampleRequestStatus.Approved, request.Status);
        Assert.Equal(laboratoryId, request.LaboratoryId);
        Assert.Equal("Approved", request.ReviewComments);
        Assert.NotNull(request.ApprovedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Approve_ShouldRejectDifferentReviewer()
    {
        var request = CreateUnderReviewRequest();

        Assert.Throws<SampleRequestDomainException>(() =>
            request.Approve(
                "reviewer-002",
                Guid.NewGuid(),
                null,
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(3)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Reject_ShouldRecordReason()
    {
        var request = CreateUnderReviewRequest();

        request.Reject(
            "reviewer-001",
            "Insufficient information",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(3));

        Assert.Equal(SampleRequestStatus.Rejected, request.Status);
        Assert.Equal(
            "Insufficient information",
            request.RejectionReason);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Cancel_ShouldAllowRequesterToCancelDraft()
    {
        var request = CreateRequest();

        request.Cancel(
            "requester-001",
            "No longer needed",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1));

        Assert.Equal(SampleRequestStatus.Cancelled, request.Status);
        Assert.Equal("No longer needed", request.CancellationReason);
        Assert.NotNull(request.CancelledAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Cancel_ShouldRejectDifferentUser()
    {
        var request = CreateRequest();

        Assert.Throws<SampleRequestDomainException>(() =>
            request.Cancel(
                "another-user",
                null,
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Cancel_ShouldRejectApprovedRequest()
    {
        var request = CreateApprovedRequest();

        Assert.Throws<SampleRequestDomainException>(() =>
            request.Cancel(
                "requester-001",
                "Cancel",
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(4)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Fulfill_ShouldLinkCreatedSample()
    {
        var request = CreateApprovedRequest();
        var sampleId = Guid.NewGuid();

        request.Fulfill(
            sampleId,
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(4));

        Assert.Equal(SampleRequestStatus.Fulfilled, request.Status);
        Assert.Equal(sampleId, request.CreatedSampleId);
        Assert.NotNull(request.FulfilledAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Fulfill_ShouldRejectEmptySampleId()
    {
        var request = CreateApprovedRequest();

        Assert.Throws<SampleRequestDomainException>(() =>
            request.Fulfill(
                Guid.Empty,
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(4)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UpdateInformation_ShouldRejectNonDraftRequest()
    {
        var request = CreateSubmittedRequest();

        Assert.Throws<SampleRequestDomainException>(() =>
            request.UpdateInformation(
                "Updated title",
                null,
                "Sample",
                null,
                SampleType.Solid,
                null,
                SampleRequestPriority.Normal,
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(3)));
    }

    private static SampleRequest CreateSubmittedRequest()
    {
        var request = CreateRequest();

        request.Submit(
            "requester-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1));

        return request;
    }

    private static SampleRequest CreateUnderReviewRequest()
    {
        var request = CreateSubmittedRequest();

        request.StartReview(
            "reviewer-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(2));

        return request;
    }

    private static SampleRequest CreateApprovedRequest()
    {
        var request = CreateUnderReviewRequest();

        request.Approve(
            "reviewer-001",
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            "Approved",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(3));

        return request;
    }
}

FILE: tests\LIMS.UnitTests\Domain\Requests\SampleRequestWorkflowTests.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Requests;
using LIMS.Domain.Requests.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Requests.Exceptions;
using LIMS.Domain.Samples.Enums;
using Xunit;

namespace LIMS.UnitTests.Requests;

public sealed class SampleRequestWorkflowTests
{
    private static readonly DateTime CreatedAt =
        new(2026, 11, 6, 10, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc);

    private static SampleRequest CreateRequest()
    {
        return SampleRequest.Create(
            "REQ-WORKFLOW-001",
            "NMR Analysis",
            null,
            "Saffron",
            null,
            SampleType.Powder,
            "Farm A",
            "requester-001",
            SampleRequestPriority.High,
            CreatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Workflow_ShouldSupportApprovalAndFulfillment()
    {
        var request = CreateRequest();
        var laboratoryId = Guid.NewGuid();
        var sampleId = Guid.NewGuid();

        request.Submit(
            "requester-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1));

        request.StartReview(
            "reviewer-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(2));

        request.Approve(
            "reviewer-001",
            laboratoryId,
            "Approved",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(3));

        request.Fulfill(
            sampleId,
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(4));

        Assert.Equal(SampleRequestStatus.Fulfilled, request.Status);
        Assert.Equal(laboratoryId, request.LaboratoryId);
        Assert.Equal(sampleId, request.CreatedSampleId);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Workflow_ShouldSupportRejection()
    {
        var request = CreateRequest();

        request.Submit(
            "requester-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1));

        request.StartReview(
            "reviewer-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(2));

        request.Reject(
            "reviewer-001",
            "Insufficient sample information",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(3));

        Assert.Equal(SampleRequestStatus.Rejected, request.Status);
        Assert.Equal(
            "Insufficient sample information",
            request.RejectionReason);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Workflow_ShouldNotAllowApprovalBeforeReview()
    {
        var request = CreateRequest();

        request.Submit(
            "requester-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1));

        Assert.Throws<SampleRequestDomainException>(() =>
            request.Approve(
                "reviewer-001",
                Guid.NewGuid(),
                null,
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(2)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Workflow_ShouldNotAllowFulfillmentBeforeApproval()
    {
        var request = CreateRequest();

        Assert.Throws<SampleRequestDomainException>(() =>
            request.Fulfill(
                Guid.NewGuid(),
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Workflow_ShouldNotAllowReviewAfterRejection()
    {
        var request = CreateRequest();

        request.Submit(
            "requester-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1));

        request.StartReview(
            "reviewer-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(2));

        request.Reject(
            "reviewer-001",
            "Invalid request",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(3));

        Assert.Throws<SampleRequestDomainException>(() =>
            request.StartReview(
                "reviewer-002",
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(4)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Workflow_ShouldNotAllowCancellationAfterFulfillment()
    {
        var request = CreateRequest();

        request.Submit(
            "requester-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1));

        request.StartReview(
            "reviewer-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(2));

        request.Approve(
            "reviewer-001",
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            null,
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(3));

        request.Fulfill(
            Guid.NewGuid(),
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(4));

        Assert.Throws<SampleRequestDomainException>(() =>
            request.Cancel(
                "requester-001",
                "Cancel",
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(5)));
    }
}

FILE: tests\LIMS.UnitTests\Domain\Results\AnalysisResultTests.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Results;
using LIMS.Domain.Results.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Results.Exceptions;
using Xunit;

namespace LIMS.UnitTests.Results;

public sealed class AnalysisResultTests
{
    private static readonly Guid LaboratoryId =
        Guid.NewGuid();

    private static readonly Guid ExperimentId =
        Guid.NewGuid();

    private static readonly Guid SampleId =
        Guid.NewGuid();

    private static readonly DateTime CreatedAt =
        new(2026, 11, 6, 10, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc);

    private static AnalysisResult CreateResult()
    {
        return AnalysisResult.Create(
            LaboratoryId,
            ExperimentId,
            SampleId,
            null,
            "RES-001",
            "NMR Analysis",
            "Initial analysis",
            "user-001",
            CreatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldInitializeResultInDraftStatus()
    {
        var result = CreateResult();

        Assert.NotEqual(Guid.Empty, result.Id);
        Assert.Equal(LaboratoryId, result.LaboratoryId);
        Assert.Equal(ExperimentId, result.ExperimentId);
        Assert.Equal(SampleId, result.SampleId);
        Assert.Equal("RES-001", result.ResultCode);
        Assert.Equal("NMR Analysis", result.Title);
        Assert.Equal(ResultStatus.Draft, result.Status);
        Assert.Empty(result.Entries);
        Assert.Equal(CreatedAt, result.CreatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldRejectEmptyLaboratoryId()
    {
        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            AnalysisResult.Create(
                Guid.Empty,
                ExperimentId,
                SampleId,
                null,
                "RES-001",
                "NMR Analysis",
                null,
                "user-001",
                CreatedAt));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldRejectEmptyExperimentId()
    {
        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            AnalysisResult.Create(
                LaboratoryId,
                Guid.Empty,
                SampleId,
                null,
                "RES-001",
                "NMR Analysis",
                null,
                "user-001",
                CreatedAt));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Create_ShouldRejectEmptySampleId()
    {
        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            AnalysisResult.Create(
                LaboratoryId,
                ExperimentId,
                Guid.Empty,
                null,
                "RES-001",
                "NMR Analysis",
                null,
                "user-001",
                CreatedAt));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("")]
    [InlineData(" ")]
    [InlineData("invalid code")]
    [InlineData("res-001")]
    public void Create_ShouldRejectInvalidResultCode(string resultCode)
    {
        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            AnalysisResult.Create(
                LaboratoryId,
                ExperimentId,
                SampleId,
                null,
                resultCode,
                "NMR Analysis",
                null,
                "user-001",
                CreatedAt));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UpdateInformation_ShouldUpdateDraftResult()
    {
        var result = CreateResult();
        var updatedAt = CreatedAt.AddHours(1);

        result.UpdateInformation(
            "Updated NMR Analysis",
            "Updated description",
            null,
            updatedAt);

        Assert.Equal("Updated NMR Analysis", result.Title);
        Assert.Equal("Updated description", result.Description);
        Assert.Equal(updatedAt, result.UpdatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddEntry_ShouldAddEntryAndReturnItsId()
    {
        var result = CreateResult();

        var entryId = result.AddEntry(
            "Chemical Shift",
            ResultValueType.Numeric,
            null,
            7.25m,
            "ppm",
            null,
            "NMR",
            null,
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1));

        Assert.NotEqual(Guid.Empty, entryId);
        Assert.Single(result.Entries);
        Assert.Equal(entryId, result.Entries.Single().Id);
        Assert.Equal("Chemical Shift", result.Entries.Single().ParameterName);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddEntry_ShouldRejectDuplicateParameterName()
    {
        var result = CreateResult();

        result.AddEntry(
            "Chemical Shift",
            ResultValueType.Numeric,
            null,
            7.25m,
            "ppm",
            null,
            null,
            null,
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1));

        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            result.AddEntry(
                "chemical shift",
                ResultValueType.Numeric,
                null,
                8.10m,
                "ppm",
                null,
                null,
                null,
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(2)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RemoveEntry_ShouldRemoveExistingEntry()
    {
        var result = CreateResult();

        var entryId = result.AddEntry(
            "Chemical Shift",
            ResultValueType.Numeric,
            null,
            7.25m,
            "ppm",
            null,
            null,
            null,
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1));

        result.RemoveEntry(
            entryId,
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(2));

        Assert.Empty(result.Entries);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Submit_ShouldRequireAtLeastOneEntry()
    {
        var result = CreateResult();

        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            result.Submit(
                "user-001",
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Submit_ShouldChangeStatusToSubmitted()
    {
        var result = CreateResult();

        result.AddEntry(
            "Chemical Shift",
            ResultValueType.Numeric,
            null,
            7.25m,
            "ppm",
            null,
            null,
            null,
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1));

        result.Submit(
            "user-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(2));

        Assert.Equal(ResultStatus.Submitted, result.Status);
        Assert.Equal("user-001", result.SubmittedByUserId);
        Assert.NotNull(result.SubmittedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void StartReview_ShouldAssignReviewer()
    {
        var result = CreateSubmittedResult();

        result.StartReview(
            "reviewer-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(3));

        Assert.Equal(ResultStatus.UnderReview, result.Status);
        Assert.Equal("reviewer-001", result.ReviewedByUserId);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void StartReview_ShouldRejectSubmitterAsReviewer()
    {
        var result = CreateSubmittedResult();

        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            result.StartReview(
                "user-001",
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(3)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Approve_ShouldApproveResultAssignedToReviewer()
    {
        var result = CreateUnderReviewResult();

        result.Approve(
            "reviewer-001",
            "Approved",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(4));

        Assert.Equal(ResultStatus.Approved, result.Status);
        Assert.Equal("reviewer-001", result.ReviewedByUserId);
        Assert.Equal("Approved", result.ReviewComments);
        Assert.NotNull(result.ReviewedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Approve_ShouldRejectDifferentReviewer()
    {
        var result = CreateUnderReviewResult();

        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            result.Approve(
                "reviewer-002",
                null,
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(4)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Reject_ShouldRejectResultAssignedToReviewer()
    {
        var result = CreateUnderReviewResult();

        result.Reject(
            "reviewer-001",
            "Insufficient evidence",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(4));

        Assert.Equal(ResultStatus.Rejected, result.Status);
        Assert.Equal("Insufficient evidence", result.RejectionReason);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ReturnToDraft_ShouldAllowCreatorToReviseRejectedResult()
    {
        var result = CreateRejectedResult();

        result.ReturnToDraft(
            "user-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(5));

        Assert.Equal(ResultStatus.Draft, result.Status);
        Assert.Null(result.SubmittedByUserId);
        Assert.Null(result.ReviewedByUserId);
        Assert.Null(result.SubmittedAt);
        Assert.Null(result.ReviewedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ReturnToDraft_ShouldRejectNonCreator()
    {
        var result = CreateRejectedResult();

        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            result.ReturnToDraft(
                "another-user",
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(5)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Archive_ShouldArchiveApprovedResult()
    {
        var result = CreateApprovedResult();

        result.Archive(
            "admin-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(5));

        Assert.Equal(ResultStatus.Archived, result.Status);
        Assert.Equal("admin-001", result.ArchivedByUserId);
        Assert.NotNull(result.ArchivedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Archive_ShouldRejectDraftResult()
    {
        var result = CreateResult();

        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            result.Archive(
                "admin-001",
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1)));
    }

    private static AnalysisResult CreateSubmittedResult()
    {
        var result = CreateResult();

        result.AddEntry(
            "Chemical Shift",
            ResultValueType.Numeric,
            null,
            7.25m,
            "ppm",
            null,
            null,
            null,
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1));

        result.Submit(
            "user-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(2));

        return result;
    }

    private static AnalysisResult CreateUnderReviewResult()
    {
        var result = CreateSubmittedResult();

        result.StartReview(
            "reviewer-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(3));

        return result;
    }

    private static AnalysisResult CreateRejectedResult()
    {
        var result = CreateUnderReviewResult();

        result.Reject(
            "reviewer-001",
            "Insufficient evidence",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(4));

        return result;
    }

    private static AnalysisResult CreateApprovedResult()
    {
        var result = CreateUnderReviewResult();

        result.Approve(
            "reviewer-001",
            "Approved",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(4));

        return result;
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: tests\LIMS.UnitTests\Domain\Results\AnalysisResultWorkflowTests.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Results;
using LIMS.Domain.Results.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Results.Exceptions;
using Xunit;

namespace LIMS.UnitTests.Results;

public sealed class AnalysisResultWorkflowTests
{
    private static readonly Guid LaboratoryId = Guid.NewGuid();
    private static readonly Guid ExperimentId = Guid.NewGuid();
    private static readonly Guid SampleId = Guid.NewGuid();

    private static readonly DateTime CreatedAt =
        new(2026, 11, 6, 10, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc);

    private static AnalysisResult CreateDraft()
    {
        return AnalysisResult.Create(
            LaboratoryId,
            ExperimentId,
            SampleId,
            null,
            "RES-001",
            "NMR Analysis",
            null,
            "creator-001",
            CreatedAt);
    }

    private static AnalysisResult CreateSubmitted()
    {
        var result = CreateDraft();

        AddEntry(result);

        result.Submit(
            "creator-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(2));

        return result;
    }

    private static AnalysisResult CreateUnderReview()
    {
        var result = CreateSubmitted();

        result.StartReview(
            "reviewer-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(3));

        return result;
    }

    private static void AddEntry(AnalysisResult result)
    {
        result.AddEntry(
            "Chemical Shift",
            ResultValueType.Numeric,
            null,
            7.25m,
            "ppm",
            null,
            "NMR",
            null,
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Workflow_ShouldSupportCompleteApprovalProcess()
    {
        var result = CreateDraft();

        AddEntry(result);

        Assert.Equal(ResultStatus.Draft, result.Status);

        result.Submit(
            "creator-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(2));

        Assert.Equal(ResultStatus.Submitted, result.Status);

        result.StartReview(
            "reviewer-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(3));

        Assert.Equal(ResultStatus.UnderReview, result.Status);

        result.Approve(
            "reviewer-001",
            "All measurements verified.",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(4));

        Assert.Equal(ResultStatus.Approved, result.Status);

        result.Archive(
            "admin-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(5));

        Assert.Equal(ResultStatus.Archived, result.Status);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Workflow_ShouldSupportRejectionAndReturnToDraft()
    {
        var result = CreateUnderReview();

        result.Reject(
            "reviewer-001",
            "Additional measurements required.",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(4));

        Assert.Equal(ResultStatus.Rejected, result.Status);

        result.ReturnToDraft(
            "creator-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(5));

        Assert.Equal(ResultStatus.Draft, result.Status);
        Assert.Null(result.SubmittedByUserId);
        Assert.Null(result.ReviewedByUserId);
        Assert.Null(result.RejectionReason);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Workflow_ShouldAllowRejectedResultToBeArchived()
    {
        var result = CreateUnderReview();

        result.Reject(
            "reviewer-001",
            "Invalid measurement.",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(4));

        result.Archive(
            "admin-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(5));

        Assert.Equal(ResultStatus.Archived, result.Status);
        Assert.Equal("admin-001", result.ArchivedByUserId);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Workflow_ShouldNotAllowEditingAfterSubmission()
    {
        var result = CreateSubmitted();

        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            result.UpdateInformation(
                "Updated title",
                "Updated description",
                null,
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(3)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Workflow_ShouldNotAllowAddingEntriesAfterSubmission()
    {
        var result = CreateSubmitted();

        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            result.AddEntry(
                "Temperature",
                ResultValueType.Numeric,
                null,
                25m,
                "C",
                null,
                null,
                null,
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(3)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Workflow_ShouldNotAllowStartingReviewTwice()
    {
        var result = CreateUnderReview();

        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            result.StartReview(
                "reviewer-002",
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(4)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Workflow_ShouldNotAllowApprovalBeforeReview()
    {
        var result = CreateSubmitted();

        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            result.Approve(
                "reviewer-001",
                null,
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(3)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Workflow_ShouldNotAllowRejectionBeforeReview()
    {
        var result = CreateSubmitted();

        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            result.Reject(
                "reviewer-001",
                "Invalid result.",
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(3)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Workflow_ShouldNotAllowReturningApprovedResultToDraft()
    {
        var result = CreateUnderReview();

        result.Approve(
            "reviewer-001",
            null,
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(4));

        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            result.ReturnToDraft(
                "creator-001",
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(5)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Workflow_ShouldNotAllowArchivingResultTwice()
    {
        var result = CreateUnderReview();

        result.Approve(
            "reviewer-001",
            null,
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(4));

        result.Archive(
            "admin-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(5));

        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            result.Archive(
                "admin-001",
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(6)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Workflow_ShouldKeepEntriesWhenReturningRejectedResultToDraft()
    {
        var result = CreateUnderReview();

        result.Reject(
            "reviewer-001",
            "Additional evidence required.",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(4));

        result.ReturnToDraft(
            "creator-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(5));

        Assert.Single(result.Entries);
        Assert.Equal("Chemical Shift", result.Entries.Single().ParameterName);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Workflow_ShouldPreserveOriginalCreator()
    {
        var result = CreateUnderReview();

        result.Reject(
            "reviewer-001",
            "Additional evidence required.",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(4));

        result.ReturnToDraft(
            "creator-001",
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(5));

        Assert.Equal("creator-001", result.CreatedByUserId);
    }
}

FILE: tests\LIMS.UnitTests\Domain\Results\ResultEntryTests.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Results.Entities;
using LIMS.Domain.Results.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Results.Exceptions;
using Xunit;

namespace LIMS.UnitTests.Results;

public sealed class ResultEntryTests
{
    private static readonly Guid ResultId =
        Guid.NewGuid();

    private static readonly DateTime CreatedAt =
        new(2026, 11, 6, 10, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc);

    [Fact]
    public void Constructor_ShouldCreateNumericEntry()
    {
        var entry = new ResultEntry(
            ResultId,
            "Chemical Shift",
            ResultValueType.Numeric,
            null,
            7.25m,
            "ppm",
            null,
            "NMR",
            null);

        Assert.NotEqual(Guid.Empty, entry.Id);
        Assert.Equal(ResultId, entry.ResultId);
        Assert.Equal("Chemical Shift", entry.ParameterName);
        Assert.Equal(ResultValueType.Numeric, entry.ValueType);
        Assert.Equal(7.25m, entry.NumericValue);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Constructor_ShouldRejectEmptyResultId()
    {
        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            new ResultEntry(
                Guid.Empty,
                "Chemical Shift",
                ResultValueType.Numeric,
                null,
                7.25m,
                "ppm",
                null,
                null,
                null));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Constructor_ShouldRejectNumericEntryWithoutNumericValue()
    {
        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            new ResultEntry(
                ResultId,
                "Chemical Shift",
                ResultValueType.Numeric,
                null,
                null,
                "ppm",
                null,
                null,
                null));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Constructor_ShouldRejectNegativeUncertainty()
    {
        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            new ResultEntry(
                ResultId,
                "Chemical Shift",
                ResultValueType.Numeric,
                null,
                7.25m,
                "ppm",
                -0.1m,
                null,
                null));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Constructor_ShouldCreateTextEntry()
    {
        var entry = new ResultEntry(
            ResultId,
            "Observation",
            ResultValueType.Text,
            "Yellow solution",
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null);

        Assert.Equal(ResultValueType.Text, entry.ValueType);
        Assert.Equal("Yellow solution", entry.Value);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Constructor_ShouldRejectTextEntryWithoutValue()
    {
        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            new ResultEntry(
                ResultId,
                "Observation",
                ResultValueType.Text,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("true")]
    [InlineData("false")]
    [InlineData("TRUE")]
    [InlineData("FALSE")]
    public void Constructor_ShouldAcceptBooleanValues(string value)
    {
        var entry = new ResultEntry(
            ResultId,
            "Detected",
            ResultValueType.Boolean,
            value,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null);

        Assert.Equal(value, entry.Value);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("yes")]
    [InlineData("no")]
    [InlineData("1")]
    [InlineData("0")]
    public void Constructor_ShouldRejectInvalidBooleanValues(string value)
    {
        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            new ResultEntry(
                ResultId,
                "Detected",
                ResultValueType.Boolean,
                value,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("2026-11-06")]
    [InlineData("2025-01-15")]
    public void Constructor_ShouldAcceptValidDateValues(string value)
    {
        var entry = new ResultEntry(
            ResultId,
            "Analysis Date",
            ResultValueType.Date,
            value,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null);

        Assert.Equal(value, entry.Value);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Constructor_ShouldRejectInvalidDateValue()
    {
        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            new ResultEntry(
                ResultId,
                "Analysis Date",
                ResultValueType.Date,
                "not-a-date",
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null));
    }
}

FILE: tests\LIMS.UnitTests\Domain\Results\ResultEntryValidationTests.cs
====================================================================================================
using LIMS.Domain.Results;
using LIMS.Domain.Results.Enums;
using LIMS.Domain.Results.Exceptions;
using Xunit;

namespace LIMS.UnitTests.Results;

public sealed class ResultEntryValidationTests
{
    private static readonly Guid LaboratoryId = Guid.NewGuid();
    private static readonly Guid ExperimentId = Guid.NewGuid();
    private static readonly Guid SampleId = Guid.NewGuid();

    private static readonly DateTime CreatedAt =
        new(2026, 11, 6, 10, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc);

    private static AnalysisResult CreateResult()
    {
        return AnalysisResult.Create(
            LaboratoryId,
            ExperimentId,
            SampleId,
            null,
            "RES-VALIDATION-001",
            "Validation Test",
            null,
            "creator-001",
            CreatedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddEntry_ShouldAcceptNumericValue()
    {
        var result = CreateResult();

        var entryId = result.AddEntry(
            "pH",
            ResultValueType.Numeric,
            null,
            7.2m,
            null,
            0.1m,
            "pH meter",
            null,
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1));

        Assert.NotEqual(Guid.Empty, entryId);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddEntry_ShouldRejectNumericValueWithoutMeasurement()
    {
        var result = CreateResult();

        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            result.AddEntry(
                "pH",
                ResultValueType.Numeric,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddEntry_ShouldAcceptTextValue()
    {
        var result = CreateResult();

        var entryId = result.AddEntry(
            "Appearance",
            ResultValueType.Text,
            "Clear liquid",
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1));

        Assert.NotEqual(Guid.Empty, entryId);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddEntry_ShouldAcceptBooleanValue()
    {
        var result = CreateResult();

        var entryId = result.AddEntry(
            "PrecipitateDetected",
            ResultValueType.Boolean,
            "true",
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1));

        Assert.NotEqual(Guid.Empty, entryId);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddEntry_ShouldRejectInvalidBooleanValue()
    {
        var result = CreateResult();

        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            result.AddEntry(
                "PrecipitateDetected",
                ResultValueType.Boolean,
                "yes",
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddEntry_ShouldAcceptDateValue()
    {
        var result = CreateResult();

        var entryId = result.AddEntry(
            "MeasurementDate",
            ResultValueType.Date,
            "2026-11-06",
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1));

        Assert.NotEqual(Guid.Empty, entryId);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddEntry_ShouldRejectInvalidDateValue()
    {
        var result = CreateResult();

        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            result.AddEntry(
                "MeasurementDate",
                ResultValueType.Date,
                "invalid-date",
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddEntry_ShouldRejectDuplicateParameterIgnoringCase()
    {
        var result = CreateResult();

        result.AddEntry(
            "Temperature",
            ResultValueType.Numeric,
            null,
            25m,
            "C",
            null,
            null,
            null,
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1));

        Assert.Throws<AnalysisResultDomainException>(() =>
            result.AddEntry(
                "TEMPERATURE",
                ResultValueType.Numeric,
                null,
                26m,
                "C",
                null,
                null,
                null,
                CreatedAt.AddMinutes(2)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddEntry_ShouldAllowParameterNameAgainAfterRemoval()
    {
        var result = CreateResult();

        var entryId = result.AddEntry(
            "Temperature",
            ResultValueType.Numeric,
            null,
            25m,
            "C",
            null,
            null,
            null,
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(1));

        result.RemoveEntry(
            entryId,
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(2));

        var newEntryId = result.AddEntry(
            "Temperature",
            ResultValueType.Numeric,
            null,
            26m,
            "C",
            null,
            null,
            null,
            CreatedAt.AddMinutes(3));

        Assert.NotEqual(entryId, newEntryId);
        Assert.Single(result.Entries);
    }
}
